# Stream B — Audio Pipeline Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Rebuild Mumla's capture pipeline so that every microphone frame is resampled to 48 kHz, cleaned by a selectable preprocessor (Speex / RNNoise / WebRTC APM echo cancellation), judged by a hysteresis voice activity detector *on the cleaned signal*, and only then boosted and encoded — with the input/output threads made robust, silence detection surfaced, Bluetooth SCO capture handled at the SCO rate, and an audio settings screen that lets users see and hear the effect.

**Architecture:** All pipeline logic (`CapturePipeline`, `VoiceActivityDetector`, `CapturePreprocessor` chain, far-end chunking, source/rate policies) is pure Kotlin in `se.lublin.humla.audio.capture` and is unit-tested against pure-Kotlin fakes; the native libraries (xiph RNNoise v0.2, freedesktop webrtc-audio-processing v2.1 + abseil-cpp 20240722.0) sit behind two tiny C-ABI wrappers (`humla_rnnoise`, `humla_apm`) that have host-side `ctest` targets, and behind thin Kotlin `external fun` objects (`RnnoiseNative`, `WebRtcApmNative`). `AudioInput`/`AudioOutput`/`AudioHandler` are converted to Kotlin with injectable `PcmCaptureSource`/`PcmPlaybackSink`/`EncoderFactory` seams so their threading and lifecycle are tested on the JVM (Robolectric only where `Context`/`AudioManager` is needed).

**Tech Stack:** Kotlin 2.x, kotlinx-coroutines (`StateFlow`, injectable dispatchers), JUnit 4 + Robolectric + MockK + Google Truth + `kotlinx-coroutines-test`; CMake 4.1.2 (SDK) / 4.4 (host) with NDK 29.0.14206865; C11/C++17; xiph RNNoise v0.2 (commit `c9137adac37fe21ede831f8a0aa31c17560c01e7`); freedesktop webrtc-audio-processing v2.1 (commit `c5a2a02a196d1c9dbe5e28b393b5cf634a35e711`); abseil-cpp 20240722.0 (commit `4447c7562e3bc702ade25105912dce503f0c4010`); AndroidX Preference.

**Spec:** `/home/becker/git/mumla/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-19-mumla-modernization.md` — §3.3 (B1–B11), §4 (interfaces between streams), §2 (global constraints). The spec wins over this plan wherever they disagree.

## Global Constraints

(Copied verbatim from spec §2 — every task's requirements implicitly include this section.)

- **Android floor:** `minSdk = 31`, `targetSdk = 36`, `compileSdk = 36`. Delete code paths that only exist for API < 31 when you touch the file that contains them.
- **Language:** all new files are Kotlin. A Java file that a task modifies in a non-trivial way (more than a few lines) is converted to Kotlin in that task, before the behavior change, as its own commit (`refactor: convert X to kotlin`). Generated protobuf code stays Java.
- **Concurrency:** Kotlin coroutines (`kotlinx-coroutines-android`). No new `AsyncTask`, no new bare `Thread` except where the audio path needs a dedicated real-time thread (capture and playback loops).
- **TDD is mandatory:** for every behavior change write the failing test first, run it and see it fail, then implement, then see it pass. Tests run on the JVM: JUnit 4 + Robolectric (Android framework classes) + MockK + Google Truth + `kotlinx-coroutines-test`. No instrumentation tests are required; design seams (interfaces, fakes) so that logic is testable without a device or native libs. Native code (C/C++) gets host-side unit tests where practical (CMake `ctest` targets built for the host), otherwise the JNI layer stays a thin pass-through and the Kotlin side is tested against a fake.
- **Commits:** Conventional Commits, English, one commit per logical unit (`feat:`, `fix:`, `refactor:`, `test:`, `build:`, `docs:`, `chore:`), optional scope in parentheses (`fix(humla):`), imperative subject ≤ 72 chars, optional body. **No trailers of any kind** — no `Co-Authored-By`, no `Claude-Session`, no `Signed-off-by`.
- **Humla lives in the main repo.** The `libraries/humla` git submodule is inlined (Foundation stream). After that, `libraries/humla` is an ordinary directory; third-party native sources (opus, speex, celt, rnnoise, webrtc-audio-processing) are git submodules of the main repo pointing at upstream release tags.
- **Native build:** CMake via AGP `externalNativeBuild`, one `CMakeLists.txt` under `libraries/humla/src/main/cpp/`. `ndk-build`, `Android.mk`, `Application.mk` and the `javacpp` bindings are removed. ABIs: `arm64-v8a`, `armeabi-v7a`, `x86_64`. NDK version `29.0.14206865`, SDK CMake `4.1.2`, build-tools `36.1.0` — set in `flake.nix` (`ndkVersions`, `cmakeVersions`, `buildToolsVersions`) and in `libraries/humla/build.gradle` (`ndkVersion`) by the Foundation stream; the dev shell currently ships NDK 26.1.10909125 and is bumped in F6.
- **Dependencies:** every dependency at its latest stable release at the time of the task. Spongycastle is replaced by BouncyCastle (`org.bouncycastle:bcprov-jdk18on`, `bcpkix-jdk18on`). `javacpp`, `guava` are removed. `protobuf-java` is updated and `Mumble.java` regenerated from `libraries/humla/src/Mumble.proto` with the matching `protoc` (use the `protobuf-gradle-plugin`, do not check in generated code).
- **Licensing:** only GPLv3-compatible dependencies (BSD, MIT, Apache-2.0, LGPL). RNNoise (BSD-3) and WebRTC audio processing (BSD-3) qualify. Add each new third-party component to `NOTICE.md` at the repo root.
- **Build must stay green:** `nix develop --command ./gradlew assembleFossDebug testFossDebugUnitTest :libraries:humla:testDebugUnitTest` passes at the end of every task. Lint runs with `abortOnError = true`; do not disable lint checks to get green, fix the finding.
- **Do not break the protocol:** wire behavior toward Mumble servers (TCP/UDP messages, crypt state, codec negotiation, CELT/Speex legacy support) is preserved.
- **User-facing strings** go to `res/values/strings.xml` / `preference.xml` (English source). Do not edit translation files; Weblate handles them.

## 0. Context every implementer must know

### 0.1 Starting state (post-Foundation, assumed by every task)

- `libraries/humla` is an ordinary directory. Kotlin plugin, version catalog `gradle/libs.versions.toml`, JUnit4 + Robolectric + MockK + Truth + coroutines-test are configured for both modules (F3). Kotlin sources live next to Java under `src/main/java/...`; tests under `src/test/java/...`.
- `app/src/main/java/se/lublin/mumla/Settings.kt` exists (F3) with the same public API as today's `Settings.java` (constants `PREF_*`/`DEFAULT_*` in a `companion object`, accessors like `getEchoCancellationMethod()`, `getDetectionThreshold()`, `isPreprocessorEnabled()`).
- Native code is built by `libraries/humla/src/main/cpp/CMakeLists.txt` (F6). Kotlin wrappers exist in package `se.lublin.humla.audio.native`: `OpusEncoderNative`, `OpusDecoderNative`, `SpeexPreprocessNative`, `SpeexResamplerNative`, `SpeexJitterNative`, `SpeexDecoderNative`, `Celt7Native`, `Celt11Native`. This plan never calls those objects directly from pipeline logic; it goes through the two small adapter interfaces `SpeexPreprocessApi` (Task 5) and `Resampler` (Task 8) so that the only place that must match F6's exact method names is a ≤ 15-line adapter.
- Today's Java files this stream owns and their line ranges (verified on branch `modernization`, commit `6f4f899`):
  - `libraries/humla/src/main/java/se/lublin/humla/protocol/AudioHandler.java` (620 lines): constructor 96–134, `setCodec` 214–254 (wraps encoder in `PreprocessingEncoder`/`ResamplingEncoder`), `shutdown` 333–350, `onAudioInputReceived` 411–472 (VAD on the raw frame at 412, boost 436–448, encode 450–459), `Builder` 515–619.
  - `libraries/humla/src/main/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/AudioInput.java` (216 lines): sample-rate probing 57–77, AEC 101–123, `stopRecording` join without timeout 139–149, record loop 187–211.
  - `libraries/humla/src/main/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/AudioOutput.java` (276 lines): `mPacketLock` locked at 220 and **not unlocked on the `return` at 232** (leak), `wait()` without timeout 147, `join()` without timeout 107.
  - `libraries/humla/src/main/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/encoder/PreprocessingEncoder.java` (98 lines): AGC on (46, 51) although the build is fixed-point; `GET_PROB_START` used as a setter at 57; preprocessing gated on the talking state because it is inside the encoder.
  - `libraries/humla/src/main/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/encoder/ResamplingEncoder.java` (87 lines): resampling after VAD.
  - `libraries/humla/src/main/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/inputmode/ActivityInputMode.java` (64 lines): dBFS formula 37–44, 250 ms hold 26/51.
  - `libraries/humla/src/main/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/inputmode/{IInputMode,ContinuousInputMode,ToggleInputMode}.java`.
  - `libraries/humla/src/main/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/BluetoothScoReceiver.java` (74 lines) — read-only for this stream (stream A's A4 replaces the SCO mechanism; B only consumes "bluetooth on/off" via `Builder.setBluetoothEnabled`).
  - `app/src/main/java/se/lublin/mumla/preference/AudioSettingsFragment.java` (79 lines), `app/src/main/res/xml/settings_audio.xml` (160 lines).
- Java-visible API of `AudioHandler` used by stream A's `HumlaService` (must survive the Kotlin conversion unchanged): `AudioHandler.Builder` with `setContext/setLogger/setAudioStream/setAudioSource/setTargetBitrate/setTargetFramesPerPacket/setInputSampleRate/setAmplitudeBoost/setBluetoothEnabled/setHalfDuplexEnabled/setPreprocessorEnabled/setEchoCancellationMethod/setEncodeListener/setTalkingListener/setInputMode` and `initialize(User, int, HumlaUDPMessageType, byte): AudioHandler`; instance methods `isInitialized()`, `setVoiceTargetId(byte)`, `clearVoiceTarget()`, `shutdown()`, `getCodec()`, `recreateEncoder()`, `isHalfDuplex()`, `getCurrentBandwidth()`; constants `AudioHandler.SAMPLE_RATE`, `AudioHandler.FRAME_SIZE`, `AudioHandler.MAX_BUFFER_SIZE`; nested `AudioHandler.AudioEncodeListener`. Verified with `grep -rn 'mAudioHandler\.\|AudioHandler\.' libraries/humla/src/main/java app/src/main/java`.
- Java-visible API of the input modes used by `HumlaService` (lines 275–277, 553, 891–934): `new ToggleInputMode()`, `toggleInputMode.isTalkingOn()`, `setTalkingOn(boolean)`, `toggleTalkingOn()`, `new ActivityInputMode(float)`, `activityInputMode.setThreshold(float)`, `new ContinuousInputMode()`.

### 0.3 Decisions

1. **webrtc-audio-processing is built by a CMake port of its meson file lists, not by meson `ExternalProject`.** Reasons: (a) the meson build downloads abseil and a wrapdb patch at build time — not reproducible and not offline-capable; (b) AGP invokes CMake once per ABI with its own toolchain file, and an `ExternalProject` would have to translate that into a meson cross file per ABI; (c) with a CMake port the same target `humla_apm` is compiled for the host by the `ctest` project with zero extra machinery. The file lists in Task 2 are copied 1:1 from the 2.1 tarball's `meson.build` files; abseil is consumed through its own CMake (`add_subdirectory`).
2. **RNNoise model weights are checked in as a 1.4 MB binary blob** (`weights_blob.bin`, sha256 above) and embedded into the native library with `.incbin`; RNNoise is compiled with `-DUSE_WEIGHTS_FILE` and the model is loaded with `rnnoise_model_from_buffer`. Regeneration is documented and pinned in `model/README.md` (Task 1). This is reproducible, needs no network at build time, and avoids a 29 MB generated C file in git.
3. **Two separate JNI shared libraries** `libhumlarnnoise.so` and `libhumlaapm.so` (not merged into the codec libraries) so a load failure of one never disables the codecs.
4. **Kotlin seams:** `CapturePreprocessor`, `VoiceActivityDetector`, `CapturePipeline`, `Resampler`, `PcmCaptureSource`, `PcmPlaybackSink`, `EncoderFactory`, `SpeexPreprocessApi`, `RnnoiseApi`, `WebRtcApmApi` — all interfaces with pure-Kotlin fakes under `src/test`. The JNI `object`s only forward.
5. **Preference keys** (all additive in `Settings.kt`): `noise_suppression` (`none|speex|rnnoise`, default `speex`; the legacy `preprocessor_enabled=false` migrates to `none`), `echo_cancellation_method` keeps its key and legacy values `none|system`, gains `webrtc`; `vad_mode` (`amplitude|probability`, default `probability`), `vad_start` (0–100, default 60), `vad_stop` (0–100, default 30), `vad_hold_ms` (default 250), `android_noise_suppressor` (default false), `android_agc` (default false). The legacy `vadThreshold` slider keeps driving `Amplitude` mode (stop = start − 0.15).
6. **Composition rule (B2):** `CapturePreprocessorFactory` builds `[WebRtcApm?] → [Speex | RNNoise]?`; the VAD probability is the last non-null one in the chain.

## Task 1: RNNoise native library, embedded model, C wrapper, host ctest (B4 native part)

**Files:**
- Create: `.gitmodules` entries (cross-stream, see §7) + submodule `libraries/humla/src/main/cpp/third_party/rnnoise`
- Create: `libraries/humla/src/main/cpp/rnnoise/CMakeLists.txt`
- Create: `libraries/humla/src/main/cpp/rnnoise/humla_rnnoise.h`, `humla_rnnoise.c`
- Create: `libraries/humla/src/main/cpp/rnnoise/model/README.md`, `rnnoise_data.h`, `rnnoise_data_init.c`, `rnnoise_model_blob.S`, `weights_blob.bin`
- Create: `libraries/humla/src/main/cpp/tests/CMakeLists.txt`, `libraries/humla/src/main/cpp/tests/test_rnnoise.c`
- Modify: `NOTICE.md` (cross-stream, add one entry)

**Interfaces:**
- Produces (C ABI, used by Task 3 JNI and Task 1 tests):
  ```c
  #define HUMLA_RNNOISE_FRAME_SIZE 480
  typedef struct humla_rnnoise humla_rnnoise;
  humla_rnnoise *humla_rnnoise_create(void);            /* NULL on failure */
  float humla_rnnoise_process(humla_rnnoise *h, int16_t *frame); /* in place, 480 samples @48k mono; returns VAD probability 0..1 */
  void humla_rnnoise_destroy(humla_rnnoise *h);
  ```
- Produces CMake target `humla_rnnoise` (STATIC, PUBLIC include dir `libraries/humla/src/main/cpp/rnnoise`).

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the model directory files**

`libraries/humla/src/main/cpp/rnnoise/model/README.md`:
```markdown
# RNNoise model (pinned)

RNNoise v0.2 keeps its weights out of git; `autogen.sh` downloads
`https://media.xiph.org/rnnoise/models/rnnoise_data-0b50c45.tar.gz`
(sha256 4ac81c5c0884ec4bd5907026aaae16209b7b76cd9d7f71af582094a2f98f4b43,
`model_version` = 0b50c45 at tag v0.2).

We build RNNoise with `-DUSE_WEIGHTS_FILE`, so the 29 MB `rnnoise_data.c`
arrays are compiled out, and embed the binary weight blob instead:

- `weights_blob.bin` (1 401 600 bytes,
  sha256 47edcad7baeffb6442d9bfe8ea3b3ae728b50055c3abca1d69b4e31a806da27d)
  produced from the tarball's `src/rnnoise_data.c` with
  `gcc -O1 -Iinclude -Isrc -DDUMP_BINARY_WEIGHTS -o dump_weights_blob src/write_weights.c && ./dump_weights_blob`
  in a checkout of rnnoise v0.2 that has the tarball's `src/rnnoise_data.[ch]` copied in.
- `rnnoise_data.h`: verbatim copy of the tarball's `src/rnnoise_data.h`.
- `rnnoise_data_init.c`: the `init_rnnoise()` function from the tarball's
  `src/rnnoise_data.c` (the only part that survives `USE_WEIGHTS_FILE`).
- `rnnoise_model_blob.S`: `.incbin`s `weights_blob.bin` as
  `humla_rnnoise_model_blob` / `humla_rnnoise_model_blob_size`.

To upgrade: bump the submodule, read its `model_version`, repeat the steps
above, update the hashes here and in `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-19-b-audio.md`.
```

`libraries/humla/src/main/cpp/rnnoise/model/rnnoise_data_init.c` (this is the tail of the tarball's `rnnoise_data.c` after the `USE_WEIGHTS_FILE` guards; copy it verbatim from `build/rnnoise-model/src/src/rnnoise_data.c` lines starting at `#ifndef DUMP_BINARY_WEIGHTS` — it must read exactly like this):
```c
/* Extracted from rnnoise_data.c (model 0b50c45); see README.md */
#ifdef HAVE_CONFIG_H
#include "config.h"
#endif

#include "rnnoise_data.h"

int init_rnnoise(RNNoise *model,const WeightArray *arrays) {
if (linear_init(&model->conv1,arrays,"conv1_bias",NULL,NULL,"conv1_weights_float",NULL,NULL,NULL,195,128)) return 1;
if (linear_init(&model->conv2,arrays,"conv2_bias","conv2_subias","conv2_weights_int8","conv2_weights_float",NULL,NULL,"conv2_scale",384,384)) return 1;
if (linear_init(&model->gru1_input,arrays,"gru1_input_bias","gru1_input_subias","gru1_input_weights_int8","gru1_input_weights_float","gru1_input_weights_idx",NULL,"gru1_input_scale",384,1152)) return 1;
if (linear_init(&model->gru1_recurrent,arrays,"gru1_recurrent_bias","gru1_recurrent_subias","gru1_recurrent_weights_int8","gru1_recurrent_weights_float","gru1_recurrent_weights_idx","gru1_recurrent_weights_diag","gru1_recurrent_scale",384,1152)) return 1;
if (linear_init(&model->gru2_input,arrays,"gru2_input_bias","gru2_input_subias","gru2_input_weights_int8","gru2_input_weights_float","gru2_input_weights_idx",NULL,"gru2_input_scale",384,1152)) return 1;
if (linear_init(&model->gru2_recurrent,arrays,"gru2_recurrent_bias","gru2_recurrent_subias","gru2_recurrent_weights_int8","gru2_recurrent_weights_float","gru2_recurrent_weights_idx","gru2_recurrent_weights_diag","gru2_recurrent_scale",384,1152)) return 1;
if (linear_init(&model->gru3_input,arrays,"gru3_input_bias","gru3_input_subias","gru3_input_weights_int8","gru3_input_weights_float","gru3_input_weights_idx",NULL,"gru3_input_scale",384,1152)) return 1;
if (linear_init(&model->gru3_recurrent,arrays,"gru3_recurrent_bias","gru3_recurrent_subias","gru3_recurrent_weights_int8","gru3_recurrent_weights_float","gru3_recurrent_weights_idx","gru3_recurrent_weights_diag","gru3_recurrent_scale",384,1152)) return 1;
if (linear_init(&model->dense_out,arrays,"dense_out_bias",NULL,NULL,"dense_out_weights_float",NULL,NULL,NULL,384,32)) return 1;
if (linear_init(&model->vad_dense,arrays,"vad_dense_bias",NULL,NULL,"vad_dense_weights_float",NULL,NULL,NULL,384,1)) return 1;
return 0;
}
```

```asm
/* Embeds weights_blob.bin; HUMLA_RNNOISE_BLOB_PATH is passed by CMake. */
    .section .rodata
    .balign 16
    .globl humla_rnnoise_model_blob
humla_rnnoise_model_blob:
    .incbin HUMLA_RNNOISE_BLOB_PATH
humla_rnnoise_model_blob_end:
    .balign 4
    .globl humla_rnnoise_model_blob_size
humla_rnnoise_model_blob_size:
    .int humla_rnnoise_model_blob_end - humla_rnnoise_model_blob
```

`libraries/humla/src/main/cpp/tests/test_rnnoise.c`:
```c
#include "humla_rnnoise.h"
#include <math.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

static int failures = 0;
#define CHECK(cond, msg) do { if (!(cond)) { fprintf(stderr, "FAIL: %s\n", msg); failures++; } } while (0)

static double energy(const int16_t *f, int n) {
    double e = 0; for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) e += (double)f[i] * f[i]; return e / n;
}

/* Deterministic LCG so the test is reproducible. */
static uint32_t seed = 12345;
static int16_t noise_sample(void) { seed = seed * 1664525u + 1013904223u; return (int16_t)((seed >> 16) % 8000) - 4000; }

int main(void) {
    humla_rnnoise *h = humla_rnnoise_create();
    CHECK(h != NULL, "create returns a state (embedded model loads)");
    if (!h) return 1;
    CHECK(HUMLA_RNNOISE_FRAME_SIZE == 480, "frame size is 480 samples (10 ms @ 48 kHz)");

    int16_t frame[HUMLA_RNNOISE_FRAME_SIZE];
    memset(frame, 0, sizeof frame);
    float p = humla_rnnoise_process(h, frame);
    CHECK(p >= 0.0f && p <= 1.0f, "probability is within [0,1]");
    CHECK(p < 0.5f, "silence is not classified as voice");
    CHECK(energy(frame, HUMLA_RNNOISE_FRAME_SIZE) == 0.0, "silence in gives silence out");

    /* 1 s of white noise: RNNoise must attenuate it. Compare the last 50 frames (after warm-up). */
    double in_e = 0, out_e = 0;
    for (int n = 0; n < 100; n++) {
        for (int i = 0; i < HUMLA_RNNOISE_FRAME_SIZE; i++) frame[i] = noise_sample();
        double ie = energy(frame, HUMLA_RNNOISE_FRAME_SIZE);
        humla_rnnoise_process(h, frame);
        if (n >= 50) { in_e += ie; out_e += energy(frame, HUMLA_RNNOISE_FRAME_SIZE); }
    }
    CHECK(out_e < in_e / 2.0, "stationary white noise is attenuated by at least 3 dB");

    humla_rnnoise_destroy(h);
    printf("%s\n", failures ? "FAILED" : "OK");
    return failures ? 1 : 0;
}
```

```cmake
# RNNoise v0.2 (BSD-3) built with the model embedded from model/weights_blob.bin.
enable_language(ASM)
set(RNNOISE_DIR ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/../third_party/rnnoise)
set(RNNOISE_MODEL_DIR ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/model)

add_library(humla_rnnoise STATIC
    ${RNNOISE_DIR}/src/denoise.c
    ${RNNOISE_DIR}/src/rnn.c
    ${RNNOISE_DIR}/src/pitch.c
    ${RNNOISE_DIR}/src/kiss_fft.c
    ${RNNOISE_DIR}/src/celt_lpc.c
    ${RNNOISE_DIR}/src/nnet.c
    ${RNNOISE_DIR}/src/nnet_default.c
    ${RNNOISE_DIR}/src/parse_lpcnet_weights.c
    ${RNNOISE_DIR}/src/rnnoise_tables.c
    ${RNNOISE_MODEL_DIR}/rnnoise_data_init.c
    ${RNNOISE_MODEL_DIR}/rnnoise_model_blob.S
    ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/humla_rnnoise.c)

target_include_directories(humla_rnnoise
    PUBLIC ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}
    PRIVATE ${RNNOISE_DIR}/include ${RNNOISE_DIR}/src ${RNNOISE_MODEL_DIR})
target_compile_definitions(humla_rnnoise PRIVATE
    RNNOISE_BUILD
    USE_WEIGHTS_FILE
    HUMLA_RNNOISE_BLOB_PATH="${RNNOISE_MODEL_DIR}/weights_blob.bin")
target_compile_options(humla_rnnoise PRIVATE -O2 -fvisibility=hidden -Wno-unused-function)
set_property(TARGET humla_rnnoise PROPERTY POSITION_INDEPENDENT_CODE ON)
# Rebuild the blob object when the weights change.
set_source_files_properties(${RNNOISE_MODEL_DIR}/rnnoise_model_blob.S PROPERTIES
    OBJECT_DEPENDS ${RNNOISE_MODEL_DIR}/weights_blob.bin)
if(NOT ANDROID)
  target_link_libraries(humla_rnnoise PUBLIC m)
endif()
```

`libraries/humla/src/main/cpp/rnnoise/humla_rnnoise.h`:
```c
#ifndef HUMLA_RNNOISE_H
#define HUMLA_RNNOISE_H
#include <stdint.h>
#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif
#define HUMLA_RNNOISE_FRAME_SIZE 480
typedef struct humla_rnnoise humla_rnnoise;
/* Allocates a denoiser using the embedded model. Returns NULL on failure. */
humla_rnnoise *humla_rnnoise_create(void);
/* Denoises one 10 ms 48 kHz mono frame in place and returns the VAD probability [0,1]. */
float humla_rnnoise_process(humla_rnnoise *h, int16_t *frame);
void humla_rnnoise_destroy(humla_rnnoise *h);
#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif
#endif
```

`libraries/humla/src/main/cpp/rnnoise/humla_rnnoise.c`:
```c
#include "humla_rnnoise.h"
#include <rnnoise.h>
#include <math.h>
#include <stdlib.h>

extern const unsigned char humla_rnnoise_model_blob[];
extern const unsigned int humla_rnnoise_model_blob_size;

struct humla_rnnoise {
    RNNModel *model;
    DenoiseState *st;
    float in[HUMLA_RNNOISE_FRAME_SIZE];
    float out[HUMLA_RNNOISE_FRAME_SIZE];
};

humla_rnnoise *humla_rnnoise_create(void) {
    if (rnnoise_get_frame_size() != HUMLA_RNNOISE_FRAME_SIZE) return NULL;
    humla_rnnoise *h = calloc(1, sizeof *h);
    if (!h) return NULL;
    h->model = rnnoise_model_from_buffer(humla_rnnoise_model_blob, (int)humla_rnnoise_model_blob_size);
    if (!h->model) { free(h); return NULL; }
    h->st = rnnoise_create(h->model);
    if (!h->st) { rnnoise_model_free(h->model); free(h); return NULL; }
    return h;
}

float humla_rnnoise_process(humla_rnnoise *h, int16_t *frame) {
    for (int i = 0; i < HUMLA_RNNOISE_FRAME_SIZE; i++) h->in[i] = (float)frame[i];
    float p = rnnoise_process_frame(h->st, h->out, h->in);
    for (int i = 0; i < HUMLA_RNNOISE_FRAME_SIZE; i++) {
        float v = h->out[i];
        if (v > 32767.f) v = 32767.f; else if (v < -32768.f) v = -32768.f;
        frame[i] = (int16_t)lrintf(v);
    }
    if (p < 0.f) p = 0.f; else if (p > 1.f) p = 1.f;
    return p;
}

void humla_rnnoise_destroy(humla_rnnoise *h) {
    if (!h) return;
    rnnoise_destroy(h->st);
    rnnoise_model_free(h->model);
    free(h);
}
```

`libraries/humla/src/main/cpp/tests/test_apm.cpp`:
```cpp
#include "humla_apm.h"
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

static int failures = 0;
#define CHECK(cond, msg) do { if (!(cond)) { std::fprintf(stderr, "FAIL: %s\n", msg); failures++; } } while (0)

static const int kRate = 48000;
static const int kFrame = kRate / 100;

static double energy(const int16_t* f, int n) {
    double e = 0; for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) e += double(f[i]) * f[i]; return e / n;
}

int main() {
    // 1. Noise suppression only: zeros in, zeros out, no error.
    humla_apm_config ns_only{0, 1, 2, 0, 1};
    humla_apm* ns = humla_apm_create(kRate, &ns_only);
    CHECK(ns != nullptr, "create with NS+HPF succeeds at 48 kHz");
    if (ns) {
        int16_t frame[kFrame];
        std::memset(frame, 0, sizeof frame);
        int err = 0;
        for (int n = 0; n < 20 && err == 0; n++) err = humla_apm_process_capture(ns, frame);
        CHECK(err == 0, "process_capture returns 0");
        CHECK(energy(frame, kFrame) == 0.0, "silence stays silent");
        CHECK(humla_apm_last_capture_level_dbfs(ns) <= -99.0f, "silence reports -100 dBFS");
        humla_apm_destroy(ns);
    }

    // 2. Unsupported rate is rejected.
    CHECK(humla_apm_create(44100, &ns_only) == nullptr, "44.1 kHz is rejected");

    // 3. AEC3: capture is a pure echo of the render signal; after convergence the residual must be ≥ 6 dB down.
    humla_apm_config aec{1, 0, 0, 0, 1};
    humla_apm* apm = humla_apm_create(kRate, &aec);
    CHECK(apm != nullptr, "create with AEC3 succeeds");
    if (apm) {
        double in_e = 0, out_e = 0;
        double phase = 0;
        for (int n = 0; n < 300; n++) {           // 3 s
            int16_t render[kFrame], capture[kFrame];
            for (int i = 0; i < kFrame; i++) {
                render[i] = int16_t(8000.0 * std::sin(phase));
                capture[i] = int16_t(render[i] / 2);   // echo path: -6 dB, zero delay
                phase += 2.0 * M_PI * 1000.0 / kRate;
            }
            humla_apm_process_render(apm, render);
            humla_apm_set_stream_delay_ms(apm, 0);
            double ie = energy(capture, kFrame);
            int err = humla_apm_process_capture(apm, capture);
            CHECK(err == 0, "AEC process_capture returns 0");
            if (n >= 250) { in_e += ie; out_e += energy(capture, kFrame); }
        }
        CHECK(out_e < in_e / 4.0, "echo of the render signal is attenuated by at least 6 dB after 2.5 s");
        humla_apm_destroy(apm);
    }

    std::printf("%s\n", failures ? "FAILED" : "OK");
    return failures ? 1 : 0;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the CMake port**

`libraries/humla/src/main/cpp/webrtc_apm/CMakeLists.txt` (source lists copied from the v2.1 `meson.build` files; keep the order so a diff against upstream stays reviewable):
```cmake
# webrtc-audio-processing v2.1 (BSD-3) — CMake port of the upstream meson file lists.
# Upstream: webrtc/{rtc_base,api,system_wrappers,common_audio,third_party/pffft,third_party/rnnoise,
#           modules/audio_coding,modules/audio_processing}/meson.build
set(WAP_DIR ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/../third_party/webrtc-audio-processing)
set(WEBRTC_DIR ${WAP_DIR}/webrtc)
set(ABSL_DIR ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/../third_party/abseil-cpp)

set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 17)
set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)
set(CMAKE_C_STANDARD 11)
set(CMAKE_POSITION_INDEPENDENT_CODE ON)

if(NOT TARGET absl::base)
  set(ABSL_PROPAGATE_CXX_STD ON CACHE BOOL "" FORCE)
  set(ABSL_ENABLE_INSTALL OFF CACHE BOOL "" FORCE)
  set(ABSL_BUILD_TESTING OFF CACHE BOOL "" FORCE)
  set(ABSL_USE_SYSTEM_INCLUDES ON CACHE BOOL "" FORCE)
  add_subdirectory(${ABSL_DIR} ${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/abseil-cpp EXCLUDE_FROM_ALL)
endif()

# ---- platform / arch flags (mirrors top-level meson.build) ----
set(WAP_DEFS WEBRTC_LIBRARY_IMPL WEBRTC_ENABLE_SYMBOL_EXPORT _WINSOCKAPI_ NDEBUG WEBRTC_POSIX WEBRTC_LINUX WEBRTC_APM_DEBUG_DUMP=0)
set(WAP_OS_LIBS "")
if(ANDROID)
  list(APPEND WAP_DEFS WEBRTC_ANDROID)
  list(APPEND WAP_OS_LIBS log)
else()
  find_package(Threads REQUIRED)
  list(APPEND WAP_OS_LIBS Threads::Threads)
endif()

set(WAP_ARCH_ARM32 OFF)
set(WAP_ARCH_ARM64 OFF)
set(WAP_ARCH_X86 OFF)
set(WAP_ARCH_FLAGS "")
if(ANDROID)
  if(ANDROID_ABI STREQUAL "arm64-v8a")
    set(WAP_ARCH_ARM64 ON)
  elseif(ANDROID_ABI STREQUAL "armeabi-v7a")
    set(WAP_ARCH_ARM32 ON)
  elseif(ANDROID_ABI STREQUAL "x86_64" OR ANDROID_ABI STREQUAL "x86")
    set(WAP_ARCH_X86 ON)
  endif()
else()
  if(CMAKE_SYSTEM_PROCESSOR MATCHES "^(aarch64|arm64)$")
    set(WAP_ARCH_ARM64 ON)
  elseif(CMAKE_SYSTEM_PROCESSOR MATCHES "^arm")
    set(WAP_ARCH_ARM32 ON)
  elseif(CMAKE_SYSTEM_PROCESSOR MATCHES "^(x86_64|AMD64|i[3-6]86)$")
    set(WAP_ARCH_X86 ON)
  endif()
endif()

elseif(WAP_ARCH_ARM32)
  list(APPEND WAP_DEFS WEBRTC_ARCH_ARM WEBRTC_ARCH_ARM_V7 WEBRTC_HAS_NEON)

      modules/audio_processing/aec3/vector_math_avx2.cc modules/audio_processing/agc2/rnn_vad/vector_math_avx2.cc)
  list(TRANSFORM WAP_SSE2_SOURCES PREPEND ${WEBRTC_DIR}/)
  list(TRANSFORM WAP_AVX2_SOURCES PREPEND ${WEBRTC_DIR}/)
  add_library(humla_apm_sse2 OBJECT ${WAP_SSE2_SOURCES})
  target_compile_options(humla_apm_sse2 PRIVATE -msse2 -O2 -fvisibility=hidden)
  add_library(humla_apm_avx2 OBJECT ${WAP_AVX2_SOURCES})
  target_compile_options(humla_apm_avx2 PRIVATE -mavx2 -mfma -O2 -fvisibility=hidden)
  foreach(t humla_apm_sse2 humla_apm_avx2)
    target_include_directories(${t} PRIVATE ${WEBRTC_DIR} ${WAP_DIR})
    target_compile_definitions(${t} PRIVATE ${WAP_DEFS})
    target_link_libraries(${t} PRIVATE absl::base absl::strings)
  endforeach()
  target_sources(humla_apm PRIVATE $<TARGET_OBJECTS:humla_apm_sse2> $<TARGET_OBJECTS:humla_apm_avx2>)
endif()
```

`libraries/humla/src/main/cpp/webrtc_apm/humla_apm.h`:
```c
#ifndef HUMLA_APM_H
#define HUMLA_APM_H
#include <stdint.h>
#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif
typedef struct humla_apm humla_apm;
typedef struct {
    int echo_cancellation;        /* AEC3, full (non-mobile) mode */
    int noise_suppression;
    int noise_suppression_level;  /* 0 low, 1 moderate, 2 high, 3 very high */
    int gain_control;             /* AGC2 adaptive digital */
    int high_pass;
} humla_apm_config;
/* sample_rate_hz must be 8000, 16000, 32000 or 48000. Returns NULL otherwise or on allocation failure. */
humla_apm *humla_apm_create(int sample_rate_hz, const humla_apm_config *cfg);
/* Process one 10 ms mono near-end frame in place. Returns 0 on success (webrtc::AudioProcessing::kNoError). */
int humla_apm_process_capture(humla_apm *h, int16_t *frame);
/* Feed one 10 ms mono far-end (playback) frame; the buffer may be modified. Returns 0 on success. */
int humla_apm_process_render(humla_apm *h, int16_t *frame);
int humla_apm_set_stream_delay_ms(humla_apm *h, int delay_ms);
/* RMS level in dBFS of the last frame returned by humla_apm_process_capture, -100 for digital silence. */
float humla_apm_last_capture_level_dbfs(const humla_apm *h);
void humla_apm_destroy(humla_apm *h);
#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif
#endif
```

`libraries/humla/src/main/cpp/webrtc_apm/humla_apm.cpp`:
```cpp
#include "humla_apm.h"
#include <api/audio/audio_processing.h>
#include <modules/audio_processing/include/audio_processing.h>
#include <cmath>

struct humla_apm {
    rtc::scoped_refptr<webrtc::AudioProcessing> apm;
    webrtc::StreamConfig config;
    float last_level_dbfs;
};

static float level_dbfs(const int16_t* frame, size_t n) {
    double acc = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++) acc += double(frame[i]) * frame[i];
    double rms = std::sqrt(acc / double(n));
    if (rms < 1.0) return -100.f;
    float db = float(20.0 * std::log10(rms / 32768.0));
    return db < -100.f ? -100.f : db;
}

extern "C" humla_apm* humla_apm_create(int sample_rate_hz, const humla_apm_config* cfg) {
    if (!cfg) return nullptr;
    if (sample_rate_hz != 8000 && sample_rate_hz != 16000 && sample_rate_hz != 32000 && sample_rate_hz != 48000) return nullptr;
    rtc::scoped_refptr<webrtc::AudioProcessing> apm = webrtc::AudioProcessingBuilder().Create();
    if (!apm) return nullptr;
    webrtc::AudioProcessing::Config c;
    c.pipeline.maximum_internal_processing_rate = 48000;
    c.high_pass_filter.enabled = cfg->high_pass != 0;
    c.echo_canceller.enabled = cfg->echo_cancellation != 0;
    c.echo_canceller.mobile_mode = false;
    c.noise_suppression.enabled = cfg->noise_suppression != 0;
    using NS = webrtc::AudioProcessing::Config::NoiseSuppression;
    int lvl = cfg->noise_suppression_level < 0 ? 0 : (cfg->noise_suppression_level > 3 ? 3 : cfg->noise_suppression_level);
    c.noise_suppression.level = static_cast<NS::Level>(lvl);
    c.gain_controller2.enabled = cfg->gain_control != 0;
    c.gain_controller2.adaptive_digital.enabled = cfg->gain_control != 0;
    c.gain_controller2.fixed_digital.gain_db = 0.0f;
    apm->ApplyConfig(c);
    auto* h = new humla_apm{apm, webrtc::StreamConfig(sample_rate_hz, 1), -100.f};
    return h;
}

extern "C" int humla_apm_process_capture(humla_apm* h, int16_t* frame) {
    if (!h || !frame) return -1;
    int err = h->apm->ProcessStream(frame, h->config, h->config, frame);
    if (err == webrtc::AudioProcessing::kNoError) h->last_level_dbfs = level_dbfs(frame, h->config.num_frames());
    return err;
}

extern "C" int humla_apm_process_render(humla_apm* h, int16_t* frame) {
    if (!h || !frame) return -1;
    return h->apm->ProcessReverseStream(frame, h->config, h->config, frame);
}

extern "C" int humla_apm_set_stream_delay_ms(humla_apm* h, int delay_ms) {
    if (!h) return -1;
    return h->apm->set_stream_delay_ms(delay_ms);
}

extern "C" float humla_apm_last_capture_level_dbfs(const humla_apm* h) {
    return h ? h->last_level_dbfs : -100.f;
}

extern "C" void humla_apm_destroy(humla_apm* h) {
    delete h;   // releases the scoped_refptr
}
```

**Files:**
- Create: `libraries/humla/src/main/cpp/jni_rnnoise.cpp`, `libraries/humla/src/main/cpp/jni_webrtc_apm.cpp`
- Create: `libraries/humla/src/main/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/native/RnnoiseNative.kt`, `WebRtcApmNative.kt`
- Modify: `libraries/humla/src/main/cpp/CMakeLists.txt` (append two `add_subdirectory` and two `add_library(... SHARED)` blocks at the end of the file)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `humla_rnnoise_*` (Task 1), `humla_apm_*` (Task 2).
- Produces (Kotlin, used by the adapters in Tasks 5–6):
  ```kotlin
  object RnnoiseNative { const val FRAME_SIZE = 480; fun create(): Long; fun processFrame(handle: Long, frame: ShortArray): Float; fun destroy(handle: Long) }
  object WebRtcApmNative { fun create(sampleRate: Int, echoCancellation: Boolean, noiseSuppression: Boolean, noiseSuppressionLevel: Int, gainControl: Boolean, highPass: Boolean): Long; fun processCapture(handle: Long, frame: ShortArray): Int; fun processRender(handle: Long, frame: ShortArray): Int; fun setStreamDelayMs(handle: Long, delayMs: Int): Int; fun lastCaptureLevelDbfs(handle: Long): Float; fun destroy(handle: Long) }
  ```
  Native libraries: `libhumlarnnoise.so`, `libhumlaapm.so`.

This task has no JVM test: the JNI layer is a pass-through (spec §2 "otherwise the JNI layer stays a thin pass-through and the Kotlin side is tested against a fake"); the wrappers underneath are covered by the host `ctest`s of Tasks 1–2, and Step 4 verifies the libraries are actually built and packaged for all three ABIs.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the Kotlin native objects**

`libraries/humla/src/main/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/native/RnnoiseNative.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.native

/**
 * JNI surface of libhumlarnnoise (xiph RNNoise v0.2 + embedded model).
 * Handles are opaque native pointers; 0 means creation failed. Not thread-safe per handle.
 */
object RnnoiseNative {
    const val FRAME_SIZE = 480

    init {
        System.loadLibrary("humlarnnoise")
    }

    external fun create(): Long

    /** Denoises [frame] (at least [FRAME_SIZE] samples, 48 kHz mono, 16-bit) in place. Returns the VAD probability in [0, 1], or -1 on error. */
    external fun processFrame(handle: Long, frame: ShortArray): Float

    external fun destroy(handle: Long)
}
```

`libraries/humla/src/main/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/native/WebRtcApmNative.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.native

/**
 * JNI surface of libhumlaapm (freedesktop webrtc-audio-processing v2.1).
 * One handle may be used concurrently from one capture thread ([processCapture]) and one
 * playback thread ([processRender]); everything else must not overlap with those calls.
 */
object WebRtcApmNative {
    init {
        System.loadLibrary("humlaapm")
    }

    /** [sampleRate] must be 8000, 16000, 32000 or 48000; [noiseSuppressionLevel] 0..3. Returns 0 on failure. */
    external fun create(
        sampleRate: Int,
        echoCancellation: Boolean,
        noiseSuppression: Boolean,
        noiseSuppressionLevel: Int,
        gainControl: Boolean,
        highPass: Boolean,
    ): Long

    /** Processes one 10 ms mono near-end frame in place. Returns 0 on success. */
    external fun processCapture(handle: Long, frame: ShortArray): Int

    /** Feeds one 10 ms mono far-end frame (may be modified). Returns 0 on success. */
    external fun processRender(handle: Long, frame: ShortArray): Int

    external fun setStreamDelayMs(handle: Long, delayMs: Int): Int

    /** dBFS RMS level of the last capture frame processed, -100 for silence. */
    external fun lastCaptureLevelDbfs(handle: Long): Float

    external fun destroy(handle: Long)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the JNI files**

`libraries/humla/src/main/cpp/jni_rnnoise.cpp`:
```cpp
#include <jni.h>
#include "rnnoise/humla_rnnoise.h"

extern "C" {

JNIEXPORT jlong JNICALL
Java_se_lublin_humla_audio_native_RnnoiseNative_create(JNIEnv*, jobject) {
    return reinterpret_cast<jlong>(humla_rnnoise_create());
}

JNIEXPORT jfloat JNICALL
Java_se_lublin_humla_audio_native_RnnoiseNative_processFrame(JNIEnv* env, jobject, jlong handle, jshortArray frame) {
    auto* h = reinterpret_cast<humla_rnnoise*>(handle);
    if (!h || !frame || env->GetArrayLength(frame) < HUMLA_RNNOISE_FRAME_SIZE) return -1.0f;
    jshort* data = env->GetShortArrayElements(frame, nullptr);
    if (!data) return -1.0f;
    float p = humla_rnnoise_process(h, reinterpret_cast<int16_t*>(data));
    env->ReleaseShortArrayElements(frame, data, 0);  // mode 0: copy back and free
    return p;
}

JNIEXPORT void JNICALL
Java_se_lublin_humla_audio_native_RnnoiseNative_destroy(JNIEnv*, jobject, jlong handle) {
    humla_rnnoise_destroy(reinterpret_cast<humla_rnnoise*>(handle));
}

}  // extern "C"
```

`libraries/humla/src/main/cpp/jni_webrtc_apm.cpp`:
```cpp
#include <jni.h>
#include "webrtc_apm/humla_apm.h"

namespace {
int process(JNIEnv* env, jlong handle, jshortArray frame, int (*fn)(humla_apm*, int16_t*)) {
    auto* h = reinterpret_cast<humla_apm*>(handle);
    if (!h || !frame) return -1;
    jshort* data = env->GetShortArrayElements(frame, nullptr);
    if (!data) return -1;
    int err = fn(h, reinterpret_cast<int16_t*>(data));
    env->ReleaseShortArrayElements(frame, data, 0);
    return err;
}
}  // namespace

extern "C" {

JNIEXPORT jlong JNICALL
Java_se_lublin_humla_audio_native_WebRtcApmNative_create(JNIEnv*, jobject, jint sampleRate, jboolean aec,
                                                          jboolean ns, jint nsLevel, jboolean agc, jboolean highPass) {
    humla_apm_config cfg{aec ? 1 : 0, ns ? 1 : 0, nsLevel, agc ? 1 : 0, highPass ? 1 : 0};
    return reinterpret_cast<jlong>(humla_apm_create(sampleRate, &cfg));
}

JNIEXPORT jint JNICALL
Java_se_lublin_humla_audio_native_WebRtcApmNative_processCapture(JNIEnv* env, jobject, jlong handle, jshortArray frame) {
    return process(env, handle, frame, humla_apm_process_capture);
}

JNIEXPORT jint JNICALL
Java_se_lublin_humla_audio_native_WebRtcApmNative_processRender(JNIEnv* env, jobject, jlong handle, jshortArray frame) {
    return process(env, handle, frame, humla_apm_process_render);
}

JNIEXPORT jint JNICALL
Java_se_lublin_humla_audio_native_WebRtcApmNative_setStreamDelayMs(JNIEnv*, jobject, jlong handle, jint delayMs) {
    return humla_apm_set_stream_delay_ms(reinterpret_cast<humla_apm*>(handle), delayMs);
}

JNIEXPORT jfloat JNICALL
Java_se_lublin_humla_audio_native_WebRtcApmNative_lastCaptureLevelDbfs(JNIEnv*, jobject, jlong handle) {
    return humla_apm_last_capture_level_dbfs(reinterpret_cast<humla_apm*>(handle));
}

JNIEXPORT void JNICALL
Java_se_lublin_humla_audio_native_WebRtcApmNative_destroy(JNIEnv*, jobject, jlong handle) {
    humla_apm_destroy(reinterpret_cast<humla_apm*>(handle));
}

}  // extern "C"
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the fake and the failing tests**

`libraries/humla/src/test/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/capture/fakes/FakePreprocessor.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.fakes

import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.CapturePreprocessor

/** Records every frame it sees (copied), applies [transform] in place and returns a fixed [probability]. */
class FakePreprocessor(
    private val probability: Float? = null,
    private val transform: (ShortArray) -> Unit = {},
    private val onProcess: () -> Unit = {},
) : CapturePreprocessor {
    val frames = mutableListOf<ShortArray>()
    var released = false
        private set

    override fun process(frame: ShortArray): Float? {
        frames += frame.copyOf()
        transform(frame)
        onProcess()
        return probability
    }

    override fun release() {
        released = true
    }
}
```

`libraries/humla/src/test/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/capture/ChainedPreprocessorTest.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.capture

import com.google.common.truth.Truth.assertThat
import org.junit.Test
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.fakes.FakePreprocessor

class ChainedPreprocessorTest {
    @Test
    fun `stages run in order and modify the same frame`() {
        val doubler = FakePreprocessor(transform = { f -> for (i in f.indices) f[i] = (f[i] * 2).toShort() })
        val plusOne = FakePreprocessor(transform = { f -> for (i in f.indices) f[i] = (f[i] + 1).toShort() })
        val chain = ChainedPreprocessor(listOf(doubler, plusOne))
        val frame = shortArrayOf(10, 20)

        chain.process(frame)

        assertThat(frame.toList()).containsExactly(21.toShort(), 41.toShort()).inOrder() // (x*2)+1, not (x+1)*2
        assertThat(plusOne.frames.single().toList()).containsExactly(20.toShort(), 40.toShort()).inOrder()
    }

    @Test
    fun `probability comes from the last stage that provides one`() {
        val chain = ChainedPreprocessor(listOf(FakePreprocessor(0.2f), FakePreprocessor(0.9f), FakePreprocessor(null)))
        assertThat(chain.process(ShortArray(480))).isEqualTo(0.9f)
    }

    @Test
    fun `probability is null when no stage provides one`() {
        val chain = ChainedPreprocessor(listOf(FakePreprocessor(null), FakePreprocessor(null)))
        assertThat(chain.process(ShortArray(480))).isNull()
    }

    @Test
    fun `release releases every stage`() {
        val a = FakePreprocessor(); val b = FakePreprocessor()
        ChainedPreprocessor(listOf(a, b)).release()
        assertThat(a.released).isTrue()
        assertThat(b.released).isTrue()
    }

    @Test
    fun `noop preprocessor leaves the frame untouched and has no opinion`() {
        val frame = shortArrayOf(1, -2, 3)
        assertThat(NoopPreprocessor.process(frame)).isNull()
        assertThat(frame.toList()).containsExactly(1.toShort(), (-2).toShort(), 3.toShort()).inOrder()
    }
}
```

`libraries/humla/src/test/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/capture/ModesTest.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.capture

import com.google.common.truth.Truth.assertThat
import org.junit.Test

class ModesTest {
    @Test
    fun `legacy echo value system maps to android effect`() {
        assertThat(EchoCancellationMode.fromPreferenceValue("system")).isEqualTo(EchoCancellationMode.ANDROID)
    }

    @Test
    fun `webrtc echo value is recognised`() {
        assertThat(EchoCancellationMode.fromPreferenceValue("webrtc")).isEqualTo(EchoCancellationMode.WEBRTC)
    }

    @Test
    fun `unknown or missing echo value falls back to none`() {
        assertThat(EchoCancellationMode.fromPreferenceValue("bogus")).isEqualTo(EchoCancellationMode.NONE)
        assertThat(EchoCancellationMode.fromPreferenceValue(null)).isEqualTo(EchoCancellationMode.NONE)
    }

    @Test
    fun `noise suppression values map and default to speex`() {
        assertThat(NoiseSuppressionMode.fromPreferenceValue("rnnoise")).isEqualTo(NoiseSuppressionMode.RNNOISE)
        assertThat(NoiseSuppressionMode.fromPreferenceValue("none")).isEqualTo(NoiseSuppressionMode.NONE)
        assertThat(NoiseSuppressionMode.fromPreferenceValue(null)).isEqualTo(NoiseSuppressionMode.SPEEX)
    }

    @Test
    fun `android effects any is true when either effect is on`() {
        assertThat(AndroidAudioEffects().any).isFalse()
        assertThat(AndroidAudioEffects(noiseSuppressor = true).any).isTrue()
        assertThat(AndroidAudioEffects(automaticGainControl = true).any).isTrue()
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the interface, chain and modes**

`libraries/humla/src/main/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/capture/CapturePreprocessor.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.capture

/**
 * One stage of the capture pipeline (spec B2). [process] is called for EVERY 10 ms 48 kHz mono
 * frame, never gated on the talking state, and modifies [frame] in place.
 * @return this stage's voice probability in [0, 1], or null when the stage has no opinion.
 */
interface CapturePreprocessor {
    fun process(frame: ShortArray): Float?

    /** Frees native resources; the instance must not be used afterwards. Idempotent. */
    fun release()
}

object NoopPreprocessor : CapturePreprocessor {
    override fun process(frame: ShortArray): Float? = null
    override fun release() = Unit
}

/** Runs [stages] in order on the same frame; the probability is the last non-null one. */
class ChainedPreprocessor(private val stages: List<CapturePreprocessor>) : CapturePreprocessor {
    override fun process(frame: ShortArray): Float? {
        var probability: Float? = null
        for (stage in stages) {
            stage.process(frame)?.let { probability = it }
        }
        return probability
    }

    override fun release() {
        stages.forEach { it.release() }
    }
}
```

`libraries/humla/src/main/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/capture/Modes.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.capture

/** Preference values are stable on-disk identifiers (see app Settings.kt). */
enum class NoiseSuppressionMode(val preferenceValue: String) {
    NONE("none"),
    SPEEX("speex"),
    RNNOISE("rnnoise");

    companion object {
        @JvmStatic
        fun fromPreferenceValue(value: String?): NoiseSuppressionMode =
            entries.firstOrNull { it.preferenceValue == value } ?: SPEEX
    }
}

/** "system" is the legacy value of the android.media.audiofx.AcousticEchoCanceler option. */
enum class EchoCancellationMode(val preferenceValue: String) {
    NONE("none"),
    ANDROID("system"),
    WEBRTC("webrtc");

    companion object {
        @JvmStatic
        fun fromPreferenceValue(value: String?): EchoCancellationMode =
            entries.firstOrNull { it.preferenceValue == value } ?: NONE
    }
}

/** android.media.audiofx effects attached to the AudioRecord session (spec B6). */
data class AndroidAudioEffects(
    val noiseSuppressor: Boolean = false,
    val automaticGainControl: Boolean = false,
) {
    val any: Boolean get() = noiseSuppressor || automaticGainControl
}
```

## Task 5: Speex preprocessor stage with correct control calls (B9)

**Files:**
- Create: `libraries/humla/src/main/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/capture/SpeexPreprocessor.kt`
- Modify (only if F6's `SpeexPreprocessNative` lacks an int setter/getter for `speex_preprocess_ctl`): `libraries/humla/src/main/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/native/SpeexPreprocessNative.kt` and `libraries/humla/src/main/cpp/jni_speex.cpp`
- Test: `libraries/humla/src/test/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/capture/fakes/FakeSpeexPreprocessApi.kt`
- Test: `libraries/humla/src/test/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/capture/SpeexPreprocessorTest.kt`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `CapturePreprocessor` (Task 4).
- Produces:
  ```kotlin
  interface SpeexPreprocessApi { fun create(frameSize: Int, sampleRate: Int): Long; fun run(state: Long, frame: ShortArray): Boolean; fun setInt(state: Long, request: Int, value: Int); fun getInt(state: Long, request: Int): Int; fun destroy(state: Long) }
  object SpeexPreprocessNativeApi : SpeexPreprocessApi
  class SpeexPreprocessor(api: SpeexPreprocessApi, frameSize: Int = 480, sampleRate: Int = 48000, noiseSuppressDb: Int = -25) : CapturePreprocessor { var noiseSuppressDb: Int; companion object { const val DEFAULT_NOISE_SUPPRESS_DB = -25; val SUPPORTED_NOISE_SUPPRESS_DB = listOf(-15, -25, -35) } }
  ```

Background (verified in `libraries/humla/src/main/jni/speex/libspeex/preprocess.c`): `SPEEX_PREPROCESS_SET_AGC` is compiled out under `FIXED_POINT` (line 1057) so today's AGC calls are no-ops; `speech_prob` is always computed (line 992) and `SPEEX_PREPROCESS_GET_PROB` (line 1190) returns it scaled 0..100 regardless of the VAD flag; `SET_NOISE_SUPPRESS` stores `-ABS(value)` (line 1147). Today's `PreprocessingEncoder.java:57` passes `GET_PROB_START` (15) where `SET_PROB_START` (14) was meant.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the fake and the failing tests**

`libraries/humla/src/test/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/capture/fakes/FakeSpeexPreprocessApi.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.fakes

import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.SpeexPreprocessApi

class FakeSpeexPreprocessApi(
    var probability: Int = 0,
    private val onRun: (ShortArray) -> Unit = {},
) : SpeexPreprocessApi {
    val setCalls = mutableListOf<Pair<Int, Int>>()   // (request, value)
    val getRequests = mutableListOf<Int>()
    var created = 0
    var destroyed = 0
    var createdWith: Pair<Int, Int>? = null
    var failCreate = false

    override fun create(frameSize: Int, sampleRate: Int): Long {
        createdWith = frameSize to sampleRate
        if (failCreate) return 0L
        created++
        return 0x5EED
    }

    override fun run(state: Long, frame: ShortArray): Boolean {
        check(state == 0x5EEDL) { "unknown state $state" }
        onRun(frame)
        return probability >= 50
    }

    override fun setInt(state: Long, request: Int, value: Int) {
        check(state == 0x5EEDL); setCalls += request to value
    }

    override fun getInt(state: Long, request: Int): Int {
        check(state == 0x5EEDL); getRequests += request
        return if (request == SpeexPreprocessorRequests.GET_PROB) probability else 0
    }

    override fun destroy(state: Long) { check(state == 0x5EEDL); destroyed++ }
}

/** Speex request ids as in speex_preprocess.h; duplicated here so the test does not read them from the code under test. */
object SpeexPreprocessorRequests {
    const val SET_DENOISE = 0
    const val SET_AGC = 2
    const val SET_VAD = 4
    const val SET_DEREVERB = 8
    const val SET_PROB_START = 14
    const val GET_PROB_START = 15
    const val SET_PROB_CONTINUE = 16
    const val SET_NOISE_SUPPRESS = 18
    const val GET_PROB = 45
    const val SET_AGC_TARGET = 46
}
```

`libraries/humla/src/test/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/capture/SpeexPreprocessorTest.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.capture

import com.google.common.truth.Truth.assertThat
import org.junit.Assert.assertThrows
import org.junit.Test
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.fakes.FakeSpeexPreprocessApi
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.fakes.SpeexPreprocessorRequests as R

class SpeexPreprocessorTest {
    private val api = FakeSpeexPreprocessApi()

    @Test
    fun `creates the state for 10 ms frames at 48 kHz`() {
        SpeexPreprocessor(api)
        assertThat(api.createdWith).isEqualTo(480 to 48000)
    }

    @Test
    fun `enables denoise and vad, never agc`() {
        SpeexPreprocessor(api)
        assertThat(api.setCalls).contains(R.SET_DENOISE to 1)
        assertThat(api.setCalls).contains(R.SET_VAD to 1)
        assertThat(api.setCalls.map { it.first }).containsNoneOf(R.SET_AGC, R.SET_AGC_TARGET)
    }

    @Test
    fun `uses the SET request for the start probability, not the GET request`() {
        SpeexPreprocessor(api)
        assertThat(api.setCalls.map { it.first }).contains(R.SET_PROB_START)
        assertThat(api.setCalls.map { it.first }).doesNotContain(R.GET_PROB_START)
    }

    @Test
    fun `applies the requested noise suppression in dB at construction and on change`() {
        val p = SpeexPreprocessor(api, noiseSuppressDb = -35)
        assertThat(api.setCalls).contains(R.SET_NOISE_SUPPRESS to -35)
        p.noiseSuppressDb = -15
        assertThat(api.setCalls.last()).isEqualTo(R.SET_NOISE_SUPPRESS to -15)
    }

    @Test
    fun `rejects unsupported noise suppression values`() {
        val p = SpeexPreprocessor(api)
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException::class.java) { p.noiseSuppressDb = -20 }
    }

    @Test
    fun `process runs speex on the frame and reports its probability as a fraction`() {
        val halving = FakeSpeexPreprocessApi(probability = 73, onRun = { f -> for (i in f.indices) f[i] = (f[i] / 2).toShort() })
        val p = SpeexPreprocessor(halving)
        val frame = ShortArray(480) { 1000 }

        val probability = p.process(frame)

        assertThat(frame[0]).isEqualTo(500.toShort())
        assertThat(probability).isEqualTo(0.73f)
        assertThat(halving.getRequests).containsExactly(R.GET_PROB)
    }

    @Test
    fun `construction fails loudly when speex cannot allocate`() {
        api.failCreate = true
        assertThrows(IllegalStateException::class.java) { SpeexPreprocessor(api) }
    }

    @Test
    fun `release destroys the state exactly once`() {
        val p = SpeexPreprocessor(api)
        p.release(); p.release()
        assertThat(api.destroyed).isEqualTo(1)
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `SpeexPreprocessor` and the native adapter**

`libraries/humla/src/main/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/capture/SpeexPreprocessor.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.capture

import se.lublin.humla.audio.native.SpeexPreprocessNative

/** The subset of speex_preprocess_* the pipeline needs. Real: [SpeexPreprocessNativeApi]; tests use a fake. */
interface SpeexPreprocessApi {
    /** speex_preprocess_state_init; returns 0 on failure. */
    fun create(frameSize: Int, sampleRate: Int): Long
    /** speex_preprocess_run; true when Speex's own VAD flags the frame as voice. */
    fun run(state: Long, frame: ShortArray): Boolean
    /** speex_preprocess_ctl with an spx_int32_t argument. */
    fun setInt(state: Long, request: Int, value: Int)
    fun getInt(state: Long, request: Int): Int
    fun destroy(state: Long)
}

/**
 * Adapter over the F6 JNI object. This is the only place that depends on the exact names of
 * [SpeexPreprocessNative]; if F6 chose different ones, fix them here (and add `ctlSetInt` /
 * `ctlGetInt` to the JNI if it only exposes a pointer-based `ctl`).
 */
object SpeexPreprocessNativeApi : SpeexPreprocessApi {
    override fun create(frameSize: Int, sampleRate: Int): Long = SpeexPreprocessNative.stateInit(frameSize, sampleRate)
    override fun run(state: Long, frame: ShortArray): Boolean = SpeexPreprocessNative.run(state, frame) != 0
    override fun setInt(state: Long, request: Int, value: Int) { SpeexPreprocessNative.ctlSetInt(state, request, value) }
    override fun getInt(state: Long, request: Int): Int = SpeexPreprocessNative.ctlGetInt(state, request)
    override fun destroy(state: Long) = SpeexPreprocessNative.stateDestroy(state)
}

/**
 * Speex denoiser (spec B9): denoise + VAD probability, no AGC (the fixed-point build has none),
 * configurable suppression. Runs on every frame; the probability comes from SPEEX_PREPROCESS_GET_PROB.
 */
class SpeexPreprocessor(
    private val api: SpeexPreprocessApi,
    frameSize: Int = 480,
    sampleRate: Int = 48000,
    noiseSuppressDb: Int = DEFAULT_NOISE_SUPPRESS_DB,
) : CapturePreprocessor {
    private var state: Long = api.create(frameSize, sampleRate)

    init {
        check(state != 0L) { "speex_preprocess_state_init($frameSize, $sampleRate) failed" }
        api.setInt(state, SET_DENOISE, 1)
        api.setInt(state, SET_DEREVERB, 1)
        api.setInt(state, SET_VAD, 1)
        api.setInt(state, SET_PROB_START, PROB_START)
        api.setInt(state, SET_PROB_CONTINUE, PROB_CONTINUE)
        this.noiseSuppressDb = noiseSuppressDb
    }

    /** One of [SUPPORTED_NOISE_SUPPRESS_DB]; more negative = stronger suppression. */
    var noiseSuppressDb: Int = noiseSuppressDb
        set(value) {
            require(value in SUPPORTED_NOISE_SUPPRESS_DB) { "unsupported noise suppression $value dB" }
            field = value
            api.setInt(state, SET_NOISE_SUPPRESS, value)
        }

    override fun process(frame: ShortArray): Float {
        api.run(state, frame)
        return api.getInt(state, GET_PROB).coerceIn(0, 100) / 100f
    }

    override fun release() {
        if (state != 0L) {
            api.destroy(state)
            state = 0L
        }
    }

    companion object {
        const val DEFAULT_NOISE_SUPPRESS_DB = -25
        val SUPPORTED_NOISE_SUPPRESS_DB = listOf(-15, -25, -35)

        /** Speex VAD start/continue thresholds in percent; only affect speex's own flag, not GET_PROB. */
        private const val PROB_START = 99
        private const val PROB_CONTINUE = 90

        // speex_preprocess.h request ids
        private const val SET_DENOISE = 0
        private const val SET_VAD = 4
        private const val SET_DEREVERB = 8
        private const val SET_PROB_START = 14
        private const val SET_PROB_CONTINUE = 16
        private const val SET_NOISE_SUPPRESS = 18
        private const val GET_PROB = 45
    }
}
```

If `SpeexPreprocessNative` (F6) does not have `ctlSetInt`/`ctlGetInt`, add them to it:
```kotlin
external fun ctlSetInt(state: Long, request: Int, value: Int): Int
external fun ctlGetInt(state: Long, request: Int): Int
```
and to `libraries/humla/src/main/cpp/jni_speex.cpp`:
```cpp
extern "C" JNIEXPORT jint JNICALL
Java_se_lublin_humla_audio_native_SpeexPreprocessNative_ctlSetInt(JNIEnv*, jobject, jlong state, jint request, jint value) {
    spx_int32_t v = value;
    return speex_preprocess_ctl(reinterpret_cast<SpeexPreprocessState*>(state), request, &v);
}
extern "C" JNIEXPORT jint JNICALL
Java_se_lublin_humla_audio_native_SpeexPreprocessNative_ctlGetInt(JNIEnv*, jobject, jlong state, jint request) {
    spx_int32_t v = 0;
    speex_preprocess_ctl(reinterpret_cast<SpeexPreprocessState*>(state), request, &v);
    return v;
}
```

`libraries/humla/src/test/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/capture/fakes/FakeRnnoiseApi.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.fakes

import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.RnnoiseApi

class FakeRnnoiseApi(var probability: Float = 0f, private val onProcess: (ShortArray) -> Unit = {}) : RnnoiseApi {
    var failCreate = false
    var destroyed = 0
    val processedLengths = mutableListOf<Int>()
    override fun create(): Long = if (failCreate) 0L else 0x0A11
    override fun processFrame(handle: Long, frame: ShortArray): Float {
        check(handle == 0x0A11L); processedLengths += frame.size; onProcess(frame); return probability
    }
    override fun destroy(handle: Long) { check(handle == 0x0A11L); destroyed++ }
}
```

`libraries/humla/src/test/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/capture/fakes/FakeWebRtcApmApi.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.fakes

import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.WebRtcApmApi
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.WebRtcApmConfig

class FakeWebRtcApmApi(
    var levelDbfs: Float = -100f,
    var captureError: Int = 0,
    private val onCapture: (ShortArray) -> Unit = {},
) : WebRtcApmApi {
    var createdWith: Pair<Int, WebRtcApmConfig>? = null
    var failCreate = false
    var destroyed = 0
    val renderFrames = mutableListOf<ShortArray>()
    var captureCalls = 0
    override fun create(sampleRate: Int, config: WebRtcApmConfig): Long {
        createdWith = sampleRate to config
        return if (failCreate) 0L else 0xA9E
    }
    override fun processCapture(handle: Long, frame: ShortArray): Int {
        check(handle == 0xA9EL); captureCalls++; onCapture(frame); return captureError
    }
    override fun processRender(handle: Long, frame: ShortArray): Int { check(handle == 0xA9EL); renderFrames += frame.copyOf(); return 0 }
    override fun lastCaptureLevelDbfs(handle: Long): Float { check(handle == 0xA9EL); return levelDbfs }
    override fun destroy(handle: Long) { check(handle == 0xA9EL); destroyed++ }
}
```

```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.fakes

import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.FarEndSink

class RecordingFarEndSink : FarEndSink {
    val frames = mutableListOf<ShortArray>()
    override fun analyzeReverseStream(frame: ShortArray) { frames += frame.copyOf() }
}
```

```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.capture

import com.google.common.truth.Truth.assertThat
import org.junit.Assert.assertThrows
import org.junit.Test
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.fakes.FakeRnnoiseApi

class RnnoisePreprocessorTest {
    @Test
    fun `process denoises in place and returns the model probability`() {
        val api = FakeRnnoiseApi(probability = 0.87f, onProcess = { f -> f.fill(7) })
        val frame = ShortArray(480) { 1000 }
        val p = RnnoisePreprocessor(api).process(frame)
        assertThat(p).isEqualTo(0.87f)
        assertThat(frame[479]).isEqualTo(7.toShort())
    }

    @Test
    fun `process clamps an out-of-range native result`() {
        assertThat(RnnoisePreprocessor(FakeRnnoiseApi(probability = 1.4f)).process(ShortArray(480))).isEqualTo(1f)
        assertThat(RnnoisePreprocessor(FakeRnnoiseApi(probability = -1f)).process(ShortArray(480))).isEqualTo(0f)
    }

    @Test
    fun `process rejects frames that are not 480 samples`() {
        val p = RnnoisePreprocessor(FakeRnnoiseApi())
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException::class.java) { p.process(ShortArray(441)) }
    }

    @Test
    fun `construction fails when the native state cannot be created`() {
        assertThrows(IllegalStateException::class.java) { RnnoisePreprocessor(FakeRnnoiseApi().apply { failCreate = true }) }
    }

    @Test
    fun `release destroys once`() {
        val api = FakeRnnoiseApi()
        RnnoisePreprocessor(api).apply { release(); release() }
        assertThat(api.destroyed).isEqualTo(1)
    }
}
```

`libraries/humla/src/test/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/capture/WebRtcApmPreprocessorTest.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.capture

import com.google.common.truth.Truth.assertThat
import org.junit.Test
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.fakes.FakeWebRtcApmApi

class WebRtcApmPreprocessorTest {
    private val config = WebRtcApmConfig(echoCancellation = true, noiseSuppression = true, gainControl = true)

    @Test
    fun `creates the apm at 48 kHz with the given config`() {
        val api = FakeWebRtcApmApi()
        WebRtcApmPreprocessor(api, config)
        assertThat(api.createdWith).isEqualTo(48000 to config)
    }

    @Test
    fun `capture frames go through the apm and the probability follows the output level`() {
        val api = FakeWebRtcApmApi(levelDbfs = -35f, onCapture = { f -> f.fill(3) })
        val frame = ShortArray(480) { 500 }
        val p = WebRtcApmPreprocessor(api, config).process(frame)
        assertThat(frame[0]).isEqualTo(3.toShort())
        assertThat(p).isEqualTo(0.5f)   // -35 dBFS is halfway between -50 (0.0) and -20 (1.0)
    }

    @Test
    fun `a native error yields no probability`() {
        assertThat(WebRtcApmPreprocessor(FakeWebRtcApmApi(captureError = -3), config).process(ShortArray(480))).isNull()
    }

    @Test
    fun `far-end frames are forwarded to the reverse stream`() {
        val api = FakeWebRtcApmApi()
        val p = WebRtcApmPreprocessor(api, config)
        p.analyzeReverseStream(ShortArray(480) { 9 })
        assertThat(api.renderFrames).hasSize(1)
        assertThat(api.renderFrames[0][0]).isEqualTo(9.toShort())
    }

    @Test
    fun `far-end frames after release are dropped instead of touching freed state`() {
        val api = FakeWebRtcApmApi()
        val p = WebRtcApmPreprocessor(api, config)
        p.release()
        p.analyzeReverseStream(ShortArray(480))
        assertThat(api.renderFrames).isEmpty()
        assertThat(api.destroyed).isEqualTo(1)
    }

    @Test
    fun `level to probability is clamped`() {
        assertThat(LevelToProbability.fromDbfs(-100f)).isEqualTo(0f)
        assertThat(LevelToProbability.fromDbfs(-50f)).isEqualTo(0f)
        assertThat(LevelToProbability.fromDbfs(-20f)).isEqualTo(1f)
        assertThat(LevelToProbability.fromDbfs(0f)).isEqualTo(1f)
    }
}
```

`libraries/humla/src/test/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/capture/FarEndFrameChunkerTest.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.capture

import com.google.common.truth.Truth.assertThat
import org.junit.Test
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.fakes.RecordingFarEndSink

class FarEndFrameChunkerTest {
    @Test
    fun `regroups arbitrary buffers into exact 480-sample frames in order`() {
        val sink = RecordingFarEndSink()
        val chunker = FarEndFrameChunker(480, sink)
        val first = ShortArray(1000) { it.toShort() }        // values 0..999
        val second = ShortArray(440) { (2000 + it).toShort() }

        chunker.push(first, first.size)
        assertThat(sink.frames).hasSize(2)
        assertThat(sink.frames[0][0]).isEqualTo(0.toShort())
        assertThat(sink.frames[1][0]).isEqualTo(480.toShort())
        assertThat(sink.frames[1][479]).isEqualTo(959.toShort())

        chunker.push(second, second.size)
        assertThat(sink.frames).hasSize(3)
        assertThat(sink.frames[2][0]).isEqualTo(960.toShort())    // leftover 40 samples first
        assertThat(sink.frames[2][40]).isEqualTo(2000.toShort())
        assertThat(sink.frames[2][479]).isEqualTo(2439.toShort())
    }

    @Test
    fun `only the first length samples of the buffer are used`() {
        val sink = RecordingFarEndSink()
        FarEndFrameChunker(4, sink).push(shortArrayOf(1, 2, 3, 4, 99, 99), 4)
        assertThat(sink.frames.single().toList()).containsExactly(1.toShort(), 2.toShort(), 3.toShort(), 4.toShort()).inOrder()
    }
}
```

```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.capture

import com.google.common.truth.Truth.assertThat
import org.junit.Test
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.fakes.FakeRnnoiseApi
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.fakes.FakeSpeexPreprocessApi
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.fakes.FakeWebRtcApmApi

class CapturePreprocessorFactoryTest {
    private val order = mutableListOf<String>()
    private val speex = FakeSpeexPreprocessApi(probability = 40, onRun = { order += "speex" })
    private val rnnoise = FakeRnnoiseApi(probability = 0.95f, onProcess = { order += "rnnoise" })
    private val apm = FakeWebRtcApmApi(levelDbfs = -35f, onCapture = { order += "apm" })
    private val logs = mutableListOf<String>()
    private val factory = CapturePreprocessorFactory({ speex }, { rnnoise }, { apm }, { logs += it })

    @Test
    fun `none and none gives a noop chain without far-end sink`() {
        val chain = factory.create(NoiseSuppressionMode.NONE, EchoCancellationMode.NONE)
        assertThat(chain.preprocessor).isSameInstanceAs(NoopPreprocessor)
        assertThat(chain.farEndSink).isNull()
    }

    @Test
    fun `webrtc echo runs before rnnoise and the probability comes from rnnoise`() {
        val chain = factory.create(NoiseSuppressionMode.RNNOISE, EchoCancellationMode.WEBRTC)
        val p = chain.preprocessor.process(ShortArray(480))
        assertThat(order).containsExactly("apm", "rnnoise").inOrder()
        assertThat(p).isEqualTo(0.95f)
        assertThat(chain.farEndSink).isNotNull()
    }

    @Test
    fun `webrtc echo alone provides a level-based probability and a far-end sink`() {
        val chain = factory.create(NoiseSuppressionMode.NONE, EchoCancellationMode.WEBRTC)
        assertThat(chain.preprocessor.process(ShortArray(480))).isEqualTo(0.5f)
        assertThat(chain.farEndSink).isNotNull()
        assertThat(apm.createdWith!!.second).isEqualTo(WebRtcApmConfig(echoCancellation = true, noiseSuppression = true, gainControl = true))
    }

    @Test
    fun `android echo mode adds no webrtc stage`() {
        val chain = factory.create(NoiseSuppressionMode.SPEEX, EchoCancellationMode.ANDROID)
        chain.preprocessor.process(ShortArray(480))
        assertThat(order).containsExactly("speex")
        assertThat(chain.farEndSink).isNull()
    }

    @Test
    fun `speex gets the requested suppression`() {
        factory.create(NoiseSuppressionMode.SPEEX, EchoCancellationMode.NONE, speexNoiseSuppressDb = -35)
        assertThat(speex.setCalls).contains(18 to -35)
    }

    @Test
    fun `a stage whose native state fails is skipped and logged, the rest keeps working`() {
        rnnoise.failCreate = true
        val chain = factory.create(NoiseSuppressionMode.RNNOISE, EchoCancellationMode.WEBRTC)
        val p = chain.preprocessor.process(ShortArray(480))
        assertThat(order).containsExactly("apm")
        assertThat(p).isEqualTo(0.5f)
        assertThat(logs).hasSize(1)
        assertThat(logs[0]).contains("RNNoise")
    }
}
```

`libraries/humla/src/main/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/capture/RnnoisePreprocessor.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.capture

import se.lublin.humla.audio.native.RnnoiseNative

interface RnnoiseApi {
    fun create(): Long
    fun processFrame(handle: Long, frame: ShortArray): Float
    fun destroy(handle: Long)
}

object RnnoiseNativeApi : RnnoiseApi {
    override fun create(): Long = RnnoiseNative.create()
    override fun processFrame(handle: Long, frame: ShortArray): Float = RnnoiseNative.processFrame(handle, frame)
    override fun destroy(handle: Long) = RnnoiseNative.destroy(handle)
}

/** RNNoise stage (spec B4): 48 kHz, exactly 480-sample frames, returns the model's VAD probability. */
class RnnoisePreprocessor(private val api: RnnoiseApi) : CapturePreprocessor {
    private var handle: Long = api.create()

    init {
        check(handle != 0L) { "rnnoise_create failed" }
    }

    override fun process(frame: ShortArray): Float {
        require(frame.size == FRAME_SIZE) { "RNNoise needs exactly $FRAME_SIZE samples, got ${frame.size}" }
        return api.processFrame(handle, frame).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
    }

    override fun release() {
        if (handle != 0L) {
            api.destroy(handle)
            handle = 0L
        }
    }

    companion object {
        const val FRAME_SIZE = RnnoiseNative.FRAME_SIZE
    }
}
```

`libraries/humla/src/main/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/capture/WebRtcApmPreprocessor.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.capture

import se.lublin.humla.audio.native.WebRtcApmNative

data class WebRtcApmConfig(
    val echoCancellation: Boolean,
    val noiseSuppression: Boolean,
    /** 0 low, 1 moderate, 2 high, 3 very high (webrtc NoiseSuppression::Level). */
    val noiseSuppressionLevel: Int = 2,
    val gainControl: Boolean,
    val highPass: Boolean = true,
)

interface WebRtcApmApi {
    fun create(sampleRate: Int, config: WebRtcApmConfig): Long
    fun processCapture(handle: Long, frame: ShortArray): Int
    fun processRender(handle: Long, frame: ShortArray): Int
    fun lastCaptureLevelDbfs(handle: Long): Float
    fun destroy(handle: Long)
}

object WebRtcApmNativeApi : WebRtcApmApi {
    override fun create(sampleRate: Int, config: WebRtcApmConfig): Long = WebRtcApmNative.create(
        sampleRate, config.echoCancellation, config.noiseSuppression, config.noiseSuppressionLevel,
        config.gainControl, config.highPass,
    )
    override fun processCapture(handle: Long, frame: ShortArray): Int = WebRtcApmNative.processCapture(handle, frame)
    override fun processRender(handle: Long, frame: ShortArray): Int = WebRtcApmNative.processRender(handle, frame)
    override fun lastCaptureLevelDbfs(handle: Long): Float = WebRtcApmNative.lastCaptureLevelDbfs(handle)
    override fun destroy(handle: Long) = WebRtcApmNative.destroy(handle)
}

/** Receives the mixed playback signal (far end) so an echo canceller can subtract it from the capture. */
interface FarEndSink {
    /** [frame] is one 10 ms 48 kHz mono frame; called on the playback thread. */
    fun analyzeReverseStream(frame: ShortArray)
}

/** Maps an RMS level in dBFS onto a "voice probability": -50 dBFS → 0, -20 dBFS → 1, linear between. */
object LevelToProbability {
    const val SILENCE_DBFS = -50f
    const val FULL_DBFS = -20f
    fun fromDbfs(dbfs: Float): Float = ((dbfs - SILENCE_DBFS) / (FULL_DBFS - SILENCE_DBFS)).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
}

/**
 * WebRTC APM stage (spec B2/B3): NS + AEC3 + AGC2 + high-pass on the capture path,
 * far-end frames from [analyzeReverseStream]. The "VAD" is derived from the output level.
 * Capture and render calls may run on two different threads (APM guarantees that); release()
 * must not overlap with either — the pipeline stops both threads first.
 */
class WebRtcApmPreprocessor(
    private val api: WebRtcApmApi,
    config: WebRtcApmConfig,
    sampleRate: Int = 48000,
) : CapturePreprocessor, FarEndSink {
    private var handle: Long = api.create(sampleRate, config)

    @Volatile
    private var released = false

    init {
        check(handle != 0L) { "webrtc AudioProcessing creation failed for $sampleRate Hz" }
    }

    override fun process(frame: ShortArray): Float? {
        if (released) return null
        val err = api.processCapture(handle, frame)
        if (err != 0) return null
        return LevelToProbability.fromDbfs(api.lastCaptureLevelDbfs(handle))
    }

    override fun analyzeReverseStream(frame: ShortArray) {
        if (released) return
        api.processRender(handle, frame)
    }

    override fun release() {
        if (!released) {
            released = true
            api.destroy(handle)
            handle = 0L
        }
    }
}
```

`libraries/humla/src/main/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/capture/FarEndFrameChunker.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.capture

/** Re-blocks the playback thread's arbitrarily sized mix buffers into exact [frameSize] frames for a [FarEndSink]. */
class FarEndFrameChunker(private val frameSize: Int, private val sink: FarEndSink) {
    private val pending = ShortArray(frameSize)
    private var filled = 0

    fun push(samples: ShortArray, length: Int) {
        var offset = 0
        while (offset < length) {
            val n = minOf(frameSize - filled, length - offset)
            System.arraycopy(samples, offset, pending, filled, n)
            filled += n
            offset += n
            if (filled == frameSize) {
                sink.analyzeReverseStream(pending)
                filled = 0
            }
        }
    }
}
```

`libraries/humla/src/main/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/capture/CapturePreprocessorFactory.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.capture

/** The assembled chain plus the far-end sink (present only when a WebRTC echo canceller is in the chain). */
class CaptureChain(val preprocessor: CapturePreprocessor, val farEndSink: FarEndSink?)

/**
 * Builds the preprocessor chain per spec B2: WebRTC APM (when echo cancellation = WEBRTC) first,
 * then Speex or RNNoise. A stage whose native library or state fails is logged and skipped so the
 * pipeline degrades to the remaining stages (and the amplitude VAD) instead of failing.
 */
class CapturePreprocessorFactory(
    private val speexApi: () -> SpeexPreprocessApi = { SpeexPreprocessNativeApi },
    private val rnnoiseApi: () -> RnnoiseApi = { RnnoiseNativeApi },
    private val apmApi: () -> WebRtcApmApi = { WebRtcApmNativeApi },
    private val log: (String) -> Unit = {},
) {
    fun create(
        noise: NoiseSuppressionMode,
        echo: EchoCancellationMode,
        speexNoiseSuppressDb: Int = SpeexPreprocessor.DEFAULT_NOISE_SUPPRESS_DB,
    ): CaptureChain {
        val stages = mutableListOf<CapturePreprocessor>()
        var farEnd: FarEndSink? = null

        if (echo == EchoCancellationMode.WEBRTC) {
            tryStage("WebRTC APM") {
                WebRtcApmPreprocessor(apmApi(), WebRtcApmConfig(echoCancellation = true, noiseSuppression = true, gainControl = true))
            }?.let { stages += it; farEnd = it }
        }
        when (noise) {
            NoiseSuppressionMode.NONE -> Unit
            NoiseSuppressionMode.SPEEX -> tryStage("Speex") { SpeexPreprocessor(speexApi(), noiseSuppressDb = speexNoiseSuppressDb) }?.let { stages += it }
            NoiseSuppressionMode.RNNOISE -> tryStage("RNNoise") { RnnoisePreprocessor(rnnoiseApi()) }?.let { stages += it }
        }

        val preprocessor: CapturePreprocessor = when (stages.size) {
            0 -> NoopPreprocessor
            1 -> stages[0]
            else -> ChainedPreprocessor(stages)
        }
        return CaptureChain(preprocessor, farEnd)
    }

    private fun <T : CapturePreprocessor> tryStage(name: String, build: () -> T): T? = try {
        build()
    } catch (e: UnsatisfiedLinkError) {
        log("$name unavailable: ${e.message}"); null
    } catch (e: IllegalStateException) {
        log("$name unavailable: ${e.message}"); null
    }
}
```
