`IInputMode.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.inputmode

/** A talk state engine, providing information regarding when it is appropriate to send audio. */
interface IInputMode {
    /** Called when new input is received from the audio recording thread. @return true if the input should be transmitted. */
    fun shouldTransmit(pcm: ShortArray, length: Int): Boolean

    /**
     * Called before any audio processing to wait for a change in input availability (e.g. push to talk
     * blocks the input thread until the button is pressed). Must return immediately while shouldTransmit is true.
     */
    fun waitForInput()
}
```

`ContinuousInputMode.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.inputmode

/** An input mode that always transmits audio. */
class ContinuousInputMode : IInputMode {
    override fun shouldTransmit(pcm: ShortArray, length: Int): Boolean = true
    override fun waitForInput() = Unit
}
```

`ToggleInputMode.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.inputmode

import android.util.Log
import java.util.concurrent.locks.ReentrantLock
import kotlin.concurrent.withLock

/** An input mode that depends on a toggle, such as push to talk. */
class ToggleInputMode : IInputMode {
    private val toggleLock = ReentrantLock()
    private val toggleCondition = toggleLock.newCondition()

    @Volatile
    private var inputOn = false

    fun toggleTalkingOn() = setTalkingOn(!inputOn)

    fun isTalkingOn(): Boolean = inputOn

    fun setTalkingOn(talking: Boolean) {
        toggleLock.withLock {
            inputOn = talking
            toggleCondition.signalAll()
        }
    }

    override fun shouldTransmit(pcm: ShortArray, length: Int): Boolean = inputOn

    override fun waitForInput() {
        toggleLock.withLock {
            if (!inputOn) {
                Log.v(TAG, "PTT: Suspending audio input.")
                val start = System.currentTimeMillis()
                try {
                    toggleCondition.await()
                } catch (e: InterruptedException) {
                    Log.w(TAG, "Blocking for PTT interrupted, likely due to input thread shutdown.")
                }
                Log.v(TAG, "PTT: Suspended audio input for " + (System.currentTimeMillis() - start) + "ms.")
            }
        }
    }

    private companion object {
        val TAG: String = ToggleInputMode::class.java.name
    }
}
```

`ActivityInputMode.kt` (1:1 port of the dBFS formula and the 250 ms hold):
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.inputmode

import kotlin.math.log10
import kotlin.math.sqrt

/** An input mode that sends audio if the amplitude exceeds a certain threshold. */
class ActivityInputMode(private var vadThreshold: Float) : IInputMode {
    private var vadLastDetected = 0L

    override fun shouldTransmit(pcm: ShortArray, length: Int): Boolean {
        var sum = 1.0f
        for (i in 0 until length) sum += pcm[i] * pcm[i].toFloat()
        val micLevel = sqrt(sum / length)
        val peakSignal = (20.0f * log10(micLevel / 32768.0f)) / 96.0f
        var talking = (peakSignal + 1) >= vadThreshold
        if (talking) vadLastDetected = System.nanoTime()
        talking = talking || (System.nanoTime() - vadLastDetected) < SPEECH_DELAY
        return talking
    }

    override fun waitForInput() = Unit

    fun setThreshold(threshold: Float) {
        vadThreshold = threshold
    }

    private companion object {
        /** Continue speech for 250 ms to prevent dropping. */
        const val SPEECH_DELAY = 250_000_000L
    }
}
```

```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.capture

import com.google.common.truth.Truth.assertThat
import org.junit.Assert.assertThrows
import org.junit.Test

class VoiceActivityDetectorTest {
    private var nowNanos = 0L
    private fun ms(v: Long) = v * 1_000_000L
    private fun detector(config: VadConfig) = VoiceActivityDetector(config) { nowNanos }
    private fun constant(value: Int) = ShortArray(480) { value.toShort() }

    @Test
    fun `amplitude score is the legacy dBFS mapping`() {
        // rms 3277 → 20*log10(3277/32768) = -20.0 dB → 1 + (-20/96) = 0.7917
        assertThat(VoiceActivityDetector.amplitudeScore(constant(3277), 480)).isWithin(0.002f).of(0.792f)
        assertThat(VoiceActivityDetector.amplitudeScore(constant(32767), 480)).isWithin(0.001f).of(1.0f)
        assertThat(VoiceActivityDetector.amplitudeScore(constant(0), 480)).isLessThan(0f)
    }

    @Test
    fun `amplitude mode compares the frame level against the start threshold`() {
        assertThat(detector(VadConfig.amplitude(0.7f)).isVoice(constant(3277), 480, null)).isTrue()
        assertThat(detector(VadConfig.amplitude(0.85f)).isVoice(constant(3277), 480, null)).isFalse()
    }

    @Test
    fun `amplitude config derives the stop threshold 0_15 below start`() {
        val c = VadConfig.amplitude(0.5f)
        assertThat(c.mode).isEqualTo(VadMode.AMPLITUDE)
        assertThat(c.startThreshold).isEqualTo(0.5f)
        assertThat(c.stopThreshold).isWithin(0.0001f).of(0.35f)
        assertThat(c.holdTimeMs).isEqualTo(250L)
        assertThat(VadConfig.amplitude(0.1f).stopThreshold).isEqualTo(0f)
    }

    @Test
    fun `probability defaults are start 0_6 stop 0_3 hold 250`() {
        assertThat(VadConfig.DEFAULT).isEqualTo(VadConfig(VadMode.PROBABILITY, 0.6f, 0.3f, 250L))
    }

    @Test
    fun `stop threshold above start is rejected`() {
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException::class.java) { VadConfig(VadMode.PROBABILITY, 0.3f, 0.6f, 250L) }
    }

    @Test
    fun `probability mode uses the preprocessor probability, not the level`() {
        val d = detector(VadConfig.probability())
        assertThat(d.isVoice(constant(0), 480, 0.9f)).isTrue()      // silent frame, model says voice
        nowNanos = ms(1000)
        assertThat(d.isVoice(constant(32767), 480, 0.1f)).isFalse()  // loud frame, model says noise
    }

    @Test
    fun `hysteresis keeps talking while above stop and needs start to begin again`() {
        val d = detector(VadConfig.probability(start = 0.8f, stop = 0.6f, holdTimeMs = 0))
        assertThat(d.isVoice(constant(0), 480, 0.7f)).isFalse()   // below start: not talking
        assertThat(d.isVoice(constant(0), 480, 0.85f)).isTrue()   // above start: talking
        assertThat(d.isVoice(constant(0), 480, 0.7f)).isTrue()    // above stop while talking: still talking
        assertThat(d.isVoice(constant(0), 480, 0.5f)).isFalse()   // below stop: stops
        assertThat(d.isVoice(constant(0), 480, 0.7f)).isFalse()   // below start again: stays off
    }

    @Test
    fun `hold time keeps talking for holdTimeMs after the last detection`() {
        val d = detector(VadConfig.probability(holdTimeMs = 250))
        nowNanos = 0
        assertThat(d.isVoice(constant(0), 480, 0.9f)).isTrue()
        nowNanos = ms(100)
        assertThat(d.isVoice(constant(0), 480, 0.0f)).isTrue()
        nowNanos = ms(249)
        assertThat(d.isVoice(constant(0), 480, 0.0f)).isTrue()
        nowNanos = ms(251)
        assertThat(d.isVoice(constant(0), 480, 0.0f)).isFalse()
    }

    @Test
    fun `probability mode falls back to the level when no stage gives a probability`() {
        val d = detector(VadConfig.probability(start = 0.7f, stop = 0.5f))
        assertThat(d.isVoice(constant(3277), 480, null)).isTrue()   // 0.792 ≥ 0.7
        assertThat(detector(VadConfig.probability(start = 0.85f, stop = 0.5f)).isVoice(constant(3277), 480, null)).isFalse()
    }

    @Test
    fun `config can be replaced at runtime`() {
        val d = detector(VadConfig.probability(start = 0.95f, stop = 0.9f, holdTimeMs = 0))
        assertThat(d.isVoice(constant(0), 480, 0.8f)).isFalse()
        d.config = VadConfig.probability(start = 0.5f, stop = 0.3f, holdTimeMs = 0)

        assertThat(VadMode.fromPreferenceValue("amplitude")).isEqualTo(VadMode.AMPLITUDE)
        assertThat(VadMode.fromPreferenceValue("nonsense")).isEqualTo(VadMode.PROBABILITY)
    }
}
```

`libraries/humla/src/main/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/capture/VadConfig.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.capture

enum class VadMode(val preferenceValue: String) {
    /** Legacy dBFS level detector; kept for devices where the models fail. */
    AMPLITUDE("amplitude"),
    /** Uses the preprocessor chain's voice probability. */
    PROBABILITY("probability");

    companion object {
        @JvmStatic
        fun fromPreferenceValue(value: String?): VadMode = entries.firstOrNull { it.preferenceValue == value } ?: PROBABILITY
    }
}

/** Start/stop hysteresis plus hold time (spec B5). Thresholds are scores in [0, 1]. */
data class VadConfig(
    val mode: VadMode,
    val startThreshold: Float,
    val stopThreshold: Float,
    val holdTimeMs: Long,
) {
    init {
        require(startThreshold in 0f..1f) { "startThreshold out of range: $startThreshold" }
        require(stopThreshold in 0f..startThreshold) { "stopThreshold must be within [0, startThreshold]: $stopThreshold" }
        require(holdTimeMs >= 0) { "holdTimeMs must not be negative" }
    }

    companion object {
        const val AMPLITUDE_HYSTERESIS = 0.15f
        const val DEFAULT_HOLD_MS = 250L

        /** Legacy single slider: stop = start − 0.15. */
        @JvmStatic
        fun amplitude(threshold: Float, holdTimeMs: Long = DEFAULT_HOLD_MS): VadConfig {
            val start = threshold.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
            return VadConfig(VadMode.AMPLITUDE, start, (start - AMPLITUDE_HYSTERESIS).coerceIn(0f, start), holdTimeMs)
        }

        @JvmStatic
        fun probability(start: Float = 0.6f, stop: Float = 0.3f, holdTimeMs: Long = DEFAULT_HOLD_MS): VadConfig =
            VadConfig(VadMode.PROBABILITY, start, stop, holdTimeMs)

        @JvmField
        val DEFAULT: VadConfig = probability()
    }
}
```

`libraries/humla/src/main/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/capture/VoiceActivityDetector.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.capture

import kotlin.math.log10
import kotlin.math.sqrt

/**
 * Decides per frame whether the user is talking. Stateful (hysteresis + hold); call from one thread.
 * [clock] returns nanoseconds and is injectable for tests.
 */
class VoiceActivityDetector(
    config: VadConfig,
    private val clock: () -> Long = System::nanoTime,
) {
    @Volatile
    var config: VadConfig = config

    private var talking = false
    private var lastVoiceNanos = Long.MIN_VALUE / 2

    /**
     * @param pcm the (already preprocessed) frame
     * @param probability the preprocessor chain's probability for this frame, or null if none
     */
    fun isVoice(pcm: ShortArray, length: Int, probability: Float?): Boolean {
        val c = config
        val score = when (c.mode) {
            VadMode.AMPLITUDE -> amplitudeScore(pcm, length)
            VadMode.PROBABILITY -> probability ?: amplitudeScore(pcm, length)
        }
        val now = clock()
        val threshold = if (talking) c.stopThreshold else c.startThreshold
        val detected = score >= threshold
        if (detected) lastVoiceNanos = now
        talking = detected || (now - lastVoiceNanos) < c.holdTimeMs * 1_000_000L
        return talking
    }

    companion object {
        /** Legacy mapping: 1 + 20·log10(rms/32768)/96 — full scale → 1.0, −96 dBFS → 0.0, silence → negative. */
        fun amplitudeScore(pcm: ShortArray, length: Int): Float {
            var sum = 1.0
            for (i in 0 until length) sum += pcm[i].toDouble() * pcm[i].toDouble()
            val rms = sqrt(sum / length)
            return (1.0 + 20.0 * log10(rms / 32768.0) / 96.0).toFloat()
        }
    }
}
```

```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.inputmode

import com.google.common.truth.Truth.assertThat
import org.junit.Test
import org.junit.runner.RunWith
import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner
import java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit

@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
class ToggleInputModeTest {
    @Test
    fun `transmits only while toggled on, ignoring audio and probability`() {
        val mode = ToggleInputMode()
        assertThat(mode.shouldTransmit(ShortArray(480) { 32767 }, 480, 1.0f)).isFalse()
        mode.setTalkingOn(true)
        assertThat(mode.shouldTransmit(ShortArray(480), 480, 0.0f)).isTrue()
        mode.toggleTalkingOn()
        assertThat(mode.isTalkingOn()).isFalse()
    }

    @Test
    fun `waitForInput blocks while off and wakes when toggled on`() {
        val mode = ToggleInputMode()
        val returned = CountDownLatch(1)
        Thread { mode.waitForInput(); returned.countDown() }.start()
        assertThat(returned.await(200, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS)).isFalse()
        mode.setTalkingOn(true)
        assertThat(returned.await(2, TimeUnit.SECONDS)).isTrue()
    }

    @Test
    fun `continuous mode always transmits`() {
        assertThat(ContinuousInputMode().shouldTransmit(ShortArray(480), 480, null)).isTrue()
    }
}
```

```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.inputmode

import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.VadConfig
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.VadMode
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.VoiceActivityDetector

/** Voice-activity input mode: delegates to a [VoiceActivityDetector] (spec B5). */
class ActivityInputMode(private val detector: VoiceActivityDetector) : IInputMode {
    /** Legacy constructor kept for HumlaService: a single amplitude slider. */
    constructor(detectionThreshold: Float) : this(VoiceActivityDetector(VadConfig.amplitude(detectionThreshold)))

    val vadConfig: VadConfig get() = detector.config

    /** Legacy slider: only meaningful in amplitude mode; in probability mode the slider is ignored. */
    fun setThreshold(threshold: Float) {
        val current = detector.config
        if (current.mode == VadMode.AMPLITUDE) {
            detector.config = VadConfig.amplitude(threshold, current.holdTimeMs)
        }
    }

    fun setVadConfig(config: VadConfig) {
        detector.config = config
    }

    override fun shouldTransmit(pcm: ShortArray, length: Int, vadProbability: Float?): Boolean =
        detector.isVoice(pcm, length, vadProbability)

    override fun waitForInput() = Unit
}
```

- Produces:
  ```kotlin

  class CaptureFrame(val samples: ShortArray, val length: Int, val transmit: Boolean, val probability: Float?)
  ```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the fake resampler and the failing tests**

`libraries/humla/src/test/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/capture/fakes/FakeResampler.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.fakes

import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.Resampler

/** Repeats each input sample [factor] times (a crude upsampler that keeps values recognisable). */
class FakeResampler(private val factor: Int) : Resampler {
    var released = false
    override fun resample(input: ShortArray, inputLength: Int, output: ShortArray): Int {
        val n = minOf(inputLength * factor, output.size)
        for (i in 0 until n) output[i] = input[i / factor]
        return n
    }
    override fun release() { released = true }
}
```

`libraries/humla/src/test/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/capture/CapturePipelineTest.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.capture

import com.google.common.truth.Truth.assertThat
import org.junit.Test
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.fakes.FakePreprocessor
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.fakes.FakeResampler
import se.lublin.humla.audio.inputmode.ActivityInputMode
import se.lublin.humla.audio.inputmode.ContinuousInputMode
import se.lublin.humla.audio.inputmode.ToggleInputMode

class CapturePipelineTest {
    private fun constant(value: Int, size: Int = 480) = ShortArray(size) { value.toShort() }

    /** Regression for the user-reported "voice activation triggered by background noise" (spec §6). */
    @Test
    fun `vad decides on the preprocessed frame, not the raw frame`() {
        val loudNoise = constant(8000)   // level score ≈ 0.87, far above a 0.5 amplitude threshold
        val gate = FakePreprocessor(transform = { it.fill(0) })   // a denoiser that removes everything

        val withDenoiser = CapturePipeline(null, gate, ActivityInputMode(0.5f))
        val withoutDenoiser = CapturePipeline(null, NoopPreprocessor, ActivityInputMode(0.5f))

        assertThat(withDenoiser.process(loudNoise, 480).transmit).isFalse()
        assertThat(withoutDenoiser.process(loudNoise, 480).transmit).isTrue()
    }

    @Test
    fun `probability mode transmits on the preprocessor probability`() {
        val silent = constant(0)
        val mode = ActivityInputMode(VoiceActivityDetector(VadConfig.probability()) { 0L })
        assertThat(CapturePipeline(null, FakePreprocessor(0.9f), mode).process(silent, 480).transmit).isTrue()
        val mode2 = ActivityInputMode(VoiceActivityDetector(VadConfig.probability()) { 0L })
        assertThat(CapturePipeline(null, FakePreprocessor(0.1f), mode2).process(silent, 480).transmit).isFalse()
    }

    @Test
    fun `preprocessing runs on every frame even while not transmitting`() {
        val fake = FakePreprocessor()
        val ptt = ToggleInputMode()   // off
        val pipeline = CapturePipeline(null, fake, ptt)
        repeat(3) { pipeline.process(constant(100), 480) }
        assertThat(fake.frames).hasSize(3)
    }

    @Test
    fun `the resampler runs before the preprocessor`() {
        val fake = FakePreprocessor()
        val pipeline = CapturePipeline(FakeResampler(3), fake, ContinuousInputMode())
        val input = ShortArray(160) { (it + 1).toShort() }
        val out = pipeline.process(input, 160)
        assertThat(out.length).isEqualTo(480)
        assertThat(fake.frames.single()[0]).isEqualTo(1.toShort())
        assertThat(fake.frames.single()[3]).isEqualTo(2.toShort())
        assertThat(fake.frames.single()[479]).isEqualTo(160.toShort())
    }

    @Test
    fun `a short resampler output is zero-padded to a full frame`() {
        val pipeline = CapturePipeline(FakeResampler(3), FakePreprocessor(), ContinuousInputMode())
        val out = pipeline.process(constant(7, 100), 100)   // 300 samples come back
        assertThat(out.length).isEqualTo(480)
        assertThat(out.samples[299]).isEqualTo(7.toShort())
        assertThat(out.samples[300]).isEqualTo(0.toShort())
    }

    @Test
    fun `boost is applied after preprocessing and only when transmitting`() {
        val seen = FakePreprocessor()
        val pipeline = CapturePipeline(null, seen, ContinuousInputMode(), amplitudeBoost = 2f)
        val out = pipeline.process(constant(1000), 480)
        assertThat(seen.frames.single()[0]).isEqualTo(1000.toShort())   // preprocessor saw the unboosted frame
        assertThat(out.samples[0]).isEqualTo(2000.toShort())

        val muted = CapturePipeline(null, FakePreprocessor(), ToggleInputMode(), amplitudeBoost = 2f)
        assertThat(muted.process(constant(1000), 480).samples[0]).isEqualTo(1000.toShort())
    }

    @Test
    fun `boost clamps to 16-bit range`() {
        val pipeline = CapturePipeline(null, NoopPreprocessor, ContinuousInputMode(), amplitudeBoost = 2f)
        val out = pipeline.process(ShortArray(480) { if (it % 2 == 0) 20000 else -20000 }, 480)
        assertThat(out.samples[0]).isEqualTo(Short.MAX_VALUE)
        assertThat(out.samples[1]).isEqualTo(Short.MIN_VALUE)
    }

    @Test
    fun `the returned frame reports the probability`() {
        val out = CapturePipeline(null, FakePreprocessor(0.42f), ContinuousInputMode()).process(constant(0), 480)
        assertThat(out.probability).isEqualTo(0.42f)
    }

    @Test
    fun `release releases resampler and preprocessor`() {
        val r = FakeResampler(1); val p = FakePreprocessor()
        CapturePipeline(r, p, ContinuousInputMode()).release()
        assertThat(r.released).isTrue()
        assertThat(p.released).isTrue()
    }
}
```

`libraries/humla/src/main/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/capture/Resampler.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.capture

import se.lublin.humla.audio.native.SpeexResamplerNative

/** Mono sample-rate converter. */
interface Resampler {
    /** Converts [inputLength] samples of [input] into [output]. @return the number of output samples written (≤ output.size). */
    fun resample(input: ShortArray, inputLength: Int, output: ShortArray): Int
    fun release()
}

/**
 * Adapter over F6's [SpeexResamplerNative] (speex_resampler_init / speex_resampler_process_int).
 * The only place that depends on that object's exact method names.
 */
class SpeexResampler(inputRate: Int, outputRate: Int, quality: Int = DEFAULT_QUALITY) : Resampler {
    private var state: Long = SpeexResamplerNative.init(1, inputRate, outputRate, quality)

    init {
        check(state != 0L) { "speex_resampler_init($inputRate -> $outputRate) failed" }
    }

    override fun resample(input: ShortArray, inputLength: Int, output: ShortArray): Int =
        SpeexResamplerNative.processInt(state, 0, input, inputLength, output, output.size)

    override fun release() {
        if (state != 0L) {
            SpeexResamplerNative.destroy(state)
            state = 0L
        }
    }

    companion object {
        /** Same quality the old ResamplingEncoder used. */
        const val DEFAULT_QUALITY = 3
    }
}
```

`libraries/humla/src/main/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/capture/CapturePipeline.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.capture

import se.lublin.humla.audio.inputmode.IInputMode

/** Result of one pipeline pass. [samples] is the pipeline's reusable buffer: valid until the next [CapturePipeline.process]. */
class CaptureFrame(val samples: ShortArray, val length: Int, val transmit: Boolean, val probability: Float?)

/**
 * Spec B1: input → resample to 48 kHz (if needed) → preprocess every frame → VAD → amplitude boost.
 * Single-threaded: call [process] only from the capture thread.
 */
class CapturePipeline(
    private val resampler: Resampler?,
    private val preprocessor: CapturePreprocessor,
    private val inputMode: IInputMode,
    @Volatile var amplitudeBoost: Float = 1f,
    frameSize: Int = 480,
) {
    private val frame = ShortArray(frameSize)

    fun process(input: ShortArray, inputLength: Int): CaptureFrame {
        val produced = if (resampler != null) {
            resampler.resample(input, inputLength, frame)
        } else {
            val n = minOf(inputLength, frame.size)
            System.arraycopy(input, 0, frame, 0, n)
            n
        }
        if (produced < frame.size) frame.fill(0, produced, frame.size)

        val probability = preprocessor.process(frame)
        val transmit = inputMode.shouldTransmit(frame, frame.size, probability)
        if (transmit && amplitudeBoost != 1f) boost(frame, amplitudeBoost)
        return CaptureFrame(frame, frame.size, transmit, probability)
    }

    fun release() {
        resampler?.release()
        preprocessor.release()
    }

    private fun boost(samples: ShortArray, factor: Float) {
        for (i in samples.indices) {
            val v = samples[i] * factor
            samples[i] = when {
                v > Short.MAX_VALUE -> Short.MAX_VALUE
                v < Short.MIN_VALUE -> Short.MIN_VALUE
                else -> v.toInt().toShort()
            }
        }
    }
}
```

- Produces:

  ```kotlin
  data class CaptureRequest(val audioSource: Int, val targetSampleRate: Int, val effects: AndroidAudioEffects = AndroidAudioEffects(), val echo: EchoCancellationMode = NONE, val preferredDevice: AudioDeviceInfo? = null)
  ```

- [ ] **Step 1: Mechanical conversion of `AudioInput.java` to Kotlin (no behavior change)**

Delete `AudioInput.java`; create `libraries/humla/src/main/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/AudioInput.kt` (keep the GPL header):
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio

import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.media.AudioFormat
import android.media.AudioRecord
import android.media.audiofx.AcousticEchoCanceler
import android.util.Log
import se.lublin.humla.exception.AudioInitializationException
import se.lublin.humla.protocol.AudioHandler

class AudioInput(
    private val listener: AudioInputListener,
    audioSource: Int,
    targetSampleRate: Int,
    private val echoCancellationMethod: String,
) : Runnable {
    fun interface AudioInputListener {
        fun onAudioInputReceived(frame: ShortArray, frameSize: Int)
    }

    private var audioRecord: AudioRecord? = null
    private var aec: AcousticEchoCanceler? = null
    val frameSize: Int
    private var recordThread: Thread? = null
    private var recording = false

    init {
        for (i in 0..SAMPLE_RATES.size) {
            val sampleRate = if (i == 0) targetSampleRate else SAMPLE_RATES[i - 1]
            try {
                audioRecord = setupAudioRecord(sampleRate, audioSource)
                if (enableEchoCancellation()) Log.w(TAG, "echo cancellation enabled: $echoCancellationMethod")
                break
            } catch (e: AudioInitializationException) {
                // keep probing
            }
        }
        if (audioRecord == null) throw AudioInitializationException("Unable to initialize AudioInput.")
        frameSize = (sampleRate * AudioHandler.FRAME_SIZE) / AudioHandler.SAMPLE_RATE
    }

    private fun enableEchoCancellation(): Boolean {
        val record = audioRecord ?: return false
        return when (echoCancellationMethod) {
            "system" -> {
                if (!AcousticEchoCanceler.isAvailable()) { Log.e(TAG, "could not enable system AEC: not available"); return false }
                aec?.release()
                val created = AcousticEchoCanceler.create(record.audioSessionId)
                if (created == null) { Log.e(TAG, "could not enable system AEC: create failed"); return false }
                created.enabled = true
                aec = created
                true
            }
            "none" -> { Log.w(TAG, "echocancellation not enabled by user"); false }
            else -> { Log.w(TAG, "ignoring unknown echocancellation method: $echoCancellationMethod"); false }
        }
    }

    fun startRecording() {
        recording = true
        recordThread = Thread(this).also { it.start() }
    }

    fun stopRecording() {
        if (!recording) return
        recording = false
        try {
            recordThread?.interrupt()
            recordThread?.join()
            recordThread = null
        } catch (e: InterruptedException) {
            e.printStackTrace()
        }
    }

    fun shutdown() {
        stopRecording()
        aec?.release(); aec = null
        audioRecord?.release(); audioRecord = null
    }

    fun isRecording(): Boolean = recording

    val sampleRate: Int get() = audioRecord!!.sampleRate

    override fun run() {
        android.os.Process.setThreadPriority(android.os.Process.THREAD_PRIORITY_URGENT_AUDIO)
        val record = audioRecord ?: return
        Log.i(TAG, "started")
        record.startRecording()
        if (record.state != AudioRecord.STATE_INITIALIZED) return
        val buffer = ShortArray(frameSize)
        while (recording) {
            val read = record.read(buffer, 0, frameSize)
            if (read > 0) listener.onAudioInputReceived(buffer, frameSize) else Log.e(TAG, "Error fetching audio! AudioRecord error $read")
        }
        record.stop()
        Log.i(TAG, "stopped")
    }

    companion object {
        private val TAG: String = AudioInput::class.java.name
        @JvmField val SAMPLE_RATES = intArrayOf(48000, 44100, 16000, 8000)

        @SuppressLint("MissingPermission") // RECORD_AUDIO is requested by the app (spec P3); a library cannot request it.
        @Throws(AudioInitializationException::class)
        private fun setupAudioRecord(sampleRate: Int, audioSource: Int): AudioRecord {
            val minBufferSize = AudioRecord.getMinBufferSize(sampleRate, AudioFormat.CHANNEL_IN_MONO, AudioFormat.ENCODING_PCM_16BIT)
            if (minBufferSize <= 0) throw AudioInitializationException("Invalid buffer size returned (unsupported sample rate).")
            val record = try {
                AudioRecord(audioSource, sampleRate, AudioFormat.CHANNEL_IN_MONO, AudioFormat.ENCODING_PCM_16BIT, minBufferSize)
            } catch (e: IllegalArgumentException) {
                throw AudioInitializationException(e)
            }
            if (record.state == AudioRecord.STATE_UNINITIALIZED) { record.release(); throw AudioInitializationException("AudioRecord failed to initialize!") }
            return record
        }
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the fake source and the failing tests**

`libraries/humla/src/test/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/capture/fakes/FakeCaptureSource.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.fakes

import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.PcmCaptureSource
import java.util.concurrent.CopyOnWriteArrayList
import java.util.concurrent.LinkedBlockingQueue
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit

/**
 * Serves [frames] one per read(), then blocks (polling) until stop(). With [readErrorAfterFrames] it
 * returns that error code once the frames are consumed. With [ignoreStop] reads keep blocking for 1.5 s
 * after stop() to simulate a hung AudioRecord.
 */
class FakeCaptureSource(
    override val sampleRate: Int = 48000,
    frames: List<ShortArray> = emptyList(),
    private val readErrorAfterFrames: Int? = null,
    private val ignoreStop: Boolean = false,
) : PcmCaptureSource {
    override val audioSessionId: Int = 42
    val events = CopyOnWriteArrayList<String>()
    var silenceListener: ((Boolean) -> Unit)? = null
        private set
    private val queue = LinkedBlockingQueue<ShortArray>(frames)

    @Volatile
    var stopped = false
        private set

    override fun start() { events += "start"; stopped = false }

    /** Like a native AudioRecord.read(): ignores thread interrupts; only stop() (or an error) ends it. */
    override fun read(buffer: ShortArray, length: Int): Int {
        while (true) {
            try {
                val frame = queue.poll(10, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS)
                if (frame != null) {
                    val n = minOf(frame.size, length)
                    System.arraycopy(frame, 0, buffer, 0, n)
                    return n
                }
                if (readErrorAfterFrames != null) return readErrorAfterFrames
                if (stopped) {
                    if (ignoreStop) Thread.sleep(1500)
                    return 0
                }
            } catch (e: InterruptedException) {
                // swallow, as a blocking native read would
            }
        }
    }

    override fun stop() { events += "stop"; stopped = true }
    override fun release() { events += "release" }
    override fun setSilenceListener(listener: ((Boolean) -> Unit)?) { silenceListener = listener }
}
```

`libraries/humla/src/test/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/AudioInputTest.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio

import com.google.common.truth.Truth.assertThat
import org.junit.Test
import org.junit.runner.RunWith
import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.CaptureState
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.fakes.FakeCaptureSource
import java.util.concurrent.CopyOnWriteArrayList
import java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit

@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
class AudioInputTest {
    private val received = CopyOnWriteArrayList<ShortArray>()
    private val states = CopyOnWriteArrayList<CaptureState>()
    private fun frame(value: Int) = ShortArray(480) { value.toShort() }

    @Test
    fun `delivers 10 ms frames to the listener in order`() {
        val latch = CountDownLatch(3)
        val source = FakeCaptureSource(frames = listOf(frame(1), frame(2), frame(3)))
        val input = AudioInput({ f, _ -> received += f.copyOf(); latch.countDown() }, source)
        input.startRecording()
        assertThat(latch.await(2, TimeUnit.SECONDS)).isTrue()
        input.shutdown()
        assertThat(received.map { it[0].toInt() }).containsExactly(1, 2, 3).inOrder()
        assertThat(input.frameSize).isEqualTo(480)
    }

    @Test
    fun `frame size follows the source rate`() {
        assertThat(AudioInput({ _, _ -> }, FakeCaptureSource(sampleRate = 16000)).frameSize).isEqualTo(160)
    }

    @Test
    fun `stopRecording stops the source before joining and clears the recording flag`() {
        val source = FakeCaptureSource()
        val input = AudioInput({ _, _ -> }, source)
        input.startRecording()
        assertThat(input.isRecording()).isTrue()
        val deadline = System.currentTimeMillis() + 2000
        while ("start" !in source.events && System.currentTimeMillis() < deadline) Thread.sleep(5)
        input.stopRecording()
        assertThat(input.isRecording()).isFalse()
        assertThat(source.events).containsExactly("start", "stop", "stop").inOrder()   // stopRecording's stop, then the loop's own stop
    }

    @Test
    fun `stopRecording gives up after the join timeout instead of hanging`() {
        val source = FakeCaptureSource(ignoreStop = true)
        val input = AudioInput({ _, _ -> }, source, joinTimeoutMs = 200)
        input.startRecording()
        Thread.sleep(50)
        val started = System.nanoTime()
        input.stopRecording()
        val elapsedMs = (System.nanoTime() - started) / 1_000_000
        assertThat(elapsedMs).isLessThan(1000)
        assertThat(input.isRecording()).isFalse()
    }

    @Test
    fun `platform silencing is reported as capture states`() {
        val source = FakeCaptureSource()
        val input = AudioInput({ _, _ -> }, source, stateListener = { states += it })
        input.startRecording()
        source.silenceListener!!.invoke(true)
        source.silenceListener!!.invoke(false)
        input.shutdown()
        assertThat(states).containsExactly(CaptureState.Silenced, CaptureState.Active).inOrder()
    }

    @Test
    fun `a read error while recording is reported and ends the loop`() {
        val source = FakeCaptureSource(frames = listOf(frame(1)), readErrorAfterFrames = -3)
        val input = AudioInput({ _, _ -> }, source, stateListener = { states += it })
        input.startRecording()
        val deadline = System.currentTimeMillis() + 2000
        while (states.isEmpty() && System.currentTimeMillis() < deadline) Thread.sleep(10)
        assertThat(states.single()).isEqualTo(CaptureState.Error("AudioRecord read error -3"))
        input.shutdown()
    }

    @Test
    fun `shutdown releases the source exactly once`() {
        val source = FakeCaptureSource()
        val input = AudioInput({ _, _ -> }, source)
        input.startRecording()
        input.shutdown()
        assertThat(source.events.count { it == "release" }).isEqualTo(1)
        assertThat(source.events.last()).isEqualTo("release")
    }
}
```

```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.capture

import android.media.MediaRecorder
import com.google.common.truth.Truth.assertThat
import org.junit.Test

class AudioSourcePolicyTest {
    private val mic = MediaRecorder.AudioSource.MIC
    private val voiceComm = MediaRecorder.AudioSource.VOICE_COMMUNICATION

    @Test
    fun `plain capture keeps the requested source`() {
        assertThat(AudioSourcePolicy.resolve(mic, AndroidAudioEffects(), EchoCancellationMode.NONE)).isEqualTo(mic)
        assertThat(AudioSourcePolicy.needsCommunicationMode(AndroidAudioEffects(), EchoCancellationMode.NONE)).isFalse()
    }

    @Test
    fun `any android effect forces voice communication`() {
        assertThat(AudioSourcePolicy.resolve(mic, AndroidAudioEffects(noiseSuppressor = true), EchoCancellationMode.NONE)).isEqualTo(voiceComm)
        assertThat(AudioSourcePolicy.resolve(mic, AndroidAudioEffects(automaticGainControl = true), EchoCancellationMode.NONE)).isEqualTo(voiceComm)
    }

    @Test
    fun `android or webrtc echo cancellation forces voice communication`() {
        assertThat(AudioSourcePolicy.resolve(mic, AndroidAudioEffects(), EchoCancellationMode.ANDROID)).isEqualTo(voiceComm)
        assertThat(AudioSourcePolicy.resolve(mic, AndroidAudioEffects(), EchoCancellationMode.WEBRTC)).isEqualTo(voiceComm)
        assertThat(AudioSourcePolicy.needsCommunicationMode(AndroidAudioEffects(), EchoCancellationMode.WEBRTC)).isTrue()
    }
}
```

```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.capture

class ScoSampleRatePolicyTest {

    }

    fun `picks the highest rate up to 16 kHz`() {
        assertThat(ScoSampleRatePolicy.choose(intArrayOf(8000, 16000, 44100, 48000))).isEqualTo(16000)
        assertThat(ScoSampleRatePolicy.choose(intArrayOf(8000))).isEqualTo(8000)
    }

    @Test
    fun `falls back to the lowest rate when nothing at or below 16 kHz is offered`() {
        assertThat(ScoSampleRatePolicy.choose(intArrayOf(48000, 44100))).isEqualTo(44100)
    }
}
```

```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.capture

/** Spec §4: exposed by AudioHandler as a StateFlow; HumlaService forwards Silenced/Error to the chat log. */
sealed interface CaptureState {
    data object Active : CaptureState
    /** The OS muted our AudioRecord (e.g. another app holds the mic or we are in the background without a foreground service type). */
    data object Silenced : CaptureState
    data class Error(val message: String) : CaptureState
}
```

`libraries/humla/src/main/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/capture/AudioSourcePolicy.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.capture

import android.media.MediaRecorder

/** Spec B6: VOICE_COMMUNICATION source and MODE_IN_COMMUNICATION whenever any effect or echo cancellation is active. */
object AudioSourcePolicy {
    fun needsCommunicationMode(effects: AndroidAudioEffects, echo: EchoCancellationMode): Boolean =
        effects.any || echo != EchoCancellationMode.NONE

    fun resolve(requested: Int, effects: AndroidAudioEffects, echo: EchoCancellationMode): Int =
        if (needsCommunicationMode(effects, echo)) MediaRecorder.AudioSource.VOICE_COMMUNICATION else requested
}
```

`libraries/humla/src/main/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/capture/ScoSampleRatePolicy.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.capture

import android.media.AudioDeviceInfo
import android.media.AudioManager

/** Spec B11: capture at the SCO link rate (8 kHz narrowband / 16 kHz wideband) and resample to 48 kHz. */
object ScoSampleRatePolicy {
    const val DEFAULT_SCO_RATE = 16000

    /** [supportedRates] from AudioDeviceInfo.sampleRates (empty = any rate). */
    fun choose(supportedRates: IntArray): Int {
        if (supportedRates.isEmpty()) return DEFAULT_SCO_RATE
        return supportedRates.filter { it <= DEFAULT_SCO_RATE }.maxOrNull() ?: supportedRates.min()
    }

    fun findScoInput(audioManager: AudioManager): AudioDeviceInfo? =
        audioManager.getDevices(AudioManager.GET_DEVICES_INPUTS).firstOrNull { it.type == AudioDeviceInfo.TYPE_BLUETOOTH_SCO }
}
```

`libraries/humla/src/main/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/capture/PcmCaptureSource.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.capture

import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.media.AudioDeviceInfo
import android.media.AudioFormat
import android.media.AudioManager
import android.media.AudioRecord
import android.media.AudioRecordingConfiguration
import android.media.audiofx.AcousticEchoCanceler
import android.media.audiofx.AudioEffect
import android.media.audiofx.AutomaticGainControl
import android.media.audiofx.NoiseSuppressor
import android.util.Log
import se.lublin.humla.exception.AudioInitializationException
import java.util.concurrent.Executor

/** Blocking PCM 16-bit mono capture source. Real: [AndroidAudioRecordSource]; tests use a fake. */
interface PcmCaptureSource {
    val sampleRate: Int
    val audioSessionId: Int
    fun start()
    /** Blocking read of up to [length] samples into [buffer]; returns the count, 0 after stop(), negative on error. */
    fun read(buffer: ShortArray, length: Int): Int
    /** Stops capture; a blocked [read] returns. Safe to call more than once. */
    fun stop()
    fun release()
    /** [listener] gets true when the platform silences this client and false when it is audible again; null unregisters. */
    fun setSilenceListener(listener: ((Boolean) -> Unit)?)
}

data class CaptureRequest(
    /** MediaRecorder.AudioSource.*; may be overridden by [AudioSourcePolicy]. */
    val audioSource: Int,
    val targetSampleRate: Int,
    val effects: AndroidAudioEffects = AndroidAudioEffects(),
    val echo: EchoCancellationMode = EchoCancellationMode.NONE,
    /** Routes capture to this device (the SCO headset) when set. */
    val preferredDevice: AudioDeviceInfo? = null,
)

fun interface PcmCaptureSourceFactory {
    @Throws(AudioInitializationException::class)
    fun open(request: CaptureRequest): PcmCaptureSource
}

class AndroidAudioRecordSource internal constructor(
    private val record: AudioRecord,
    private val effects: List<AudioEffect>,
) : PcmCaptureSource {
    override val sampleRate: Int get() = record.sampleRate
    override val audioSessionId: Int get() = record.audioSessionId
    private var recordingCallback: AudioManager.AudioRecordingCallback? = null

    override fun start() = record.startRecording()

    override fun read(buffer: ShortArray, length: Int): Int = record.read(buffer, 0, length)

    override fun stop() {
        if (record.state == AudioRecord.STATE_INITIALIZED && record.recordingState == AudioRecord.RECORDSTATE_RECORDING) record.stop()
    }

    override fun release() {
        setSilenceListener(null)
        effects.forEach { it.release() }
        record.release()
    }

    override fun setSilenceListener(listener: ((Boolean) -> Unit)?) {
        recordingCallback?.let { record.unregisterAudioRecordingCallback(it) }
        recordingCallback = null
        if (listener == null) return
        val callback = object : AudioManager.AudioRecordingCallback() {
            override fun onRecordingConfigChanged(configs: MutableList<AudioRecordingConfiguration>) {
                val mine = configs.firstOrNull { it.clientAudioSessionId == record.audioSessionId } ?: return
                listener(mine.isClientSilenced)
            }
        }
        record.registerAudioRecordingCallback(Executor { it.run() }, callback)
        recordingCallback = callback
    }

    class Factory : PcmCaptureSourceFactory {
        // RECORD_AUDIO is requested by the app before it connects (spec P3); a library cannot request it.
        @SuppressLint("MissingPermission")
        override fun open(request: CaptureRequest): PcmCaptureSource {
            val source = AudioSourcePolicy.resolve(request.audioSource, request.effects, request.echo)
            val rates = listOf(request.targetSampleRate) + SAMPLE_RATES.filter { it != request.targetSampleRate }
            val record = rates.firstNotNullOfOrNull { tryOpen(source, it) }
                ?: throw AudioInitializationException("Unable to initialize AudioRecord at any of $rates Hz")
            request.preferredDevice?.let { record.preferredDevice = it }
            Log.i(TAG, "AudioRecord source=$source rate=${record.sampleRate} device=${request.preferredDevice?.productName}")
            return AndroidAudioRecordSource(record, attachEffects(record.audioSessionId, request))
        }

        @SuppressLint("MissingPermission")
        private fun tryOpen(source: Int, rate: Int): AudioRecord? {
            val minBufferSize = AudioRecord.getMinBufferSize(rate, AudioFormat.CHANNEL_IN_MONO, AudioFormat.ENCODING_PCM_16BIT)
            if (minBufferSize <= 0) return null
            val record = try {
                AudioRecord(source, rate, AudioFormat.CHANNEL_IN_MONO, AudioFormat.ENCODING_PCM_16BIT, minBufferSize)
            } catch (e: IllegalArgumentException) {
                return null
            }
            if (record.state != AudioRecord.STATE_INITIALIZED) {
                record.release()
                return null
            }
            return record
        }

        private fun attachEffects(sessionId: Int, request: CaptureRequest): List<AudioEffect> {
            val attached = mutableListOf<AudioEffect>()
            fun attach(name: String, available: Boolean, create: () -> AudioEffect?) {
                if (!available) { Log.w(TAG, "$name not available on this device"); return }
                val effect = create()
                if (effect == null) { Log.w(TAG, "$name creation failed"); return }
                effect.enabled = true
                attached += effect
                Log.i(TAG, "$name enabled")
            }
            if (request.echo == EchoCancellationMode.ANDROID) attach("AcousticEchoCanceler", AcousticEchoCanceler.isAvailable()) { AcousticEchoCanceler.create(sessionId) }
            if (request.effects.noiseSuppressor) attach("NoiseSuppressor", NoiseSuppressor.isAvailable()) { NoiseSuppressor.create(sessionId) }
            if (request.effects.automaticGainControl) attach("AutomaticGainControl", AutomaticGainControl.isAvailable()) { AutomaticGainControl.create(sessionId) }
            return attached
        }
    }

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "AndroidAudioRecordSource"
        @JvmField val SAMPLE_RATES = intArrayOf(48000, 44100, 16000, 8000)
    }
}
```

`libraries/humla/src/main/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/AudioInput.kt` — full replacement:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio

import android.os.Process
import android.util.Log
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.AndroidAudioRecordSource
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.CaptureRequest
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.CaptureState
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.EchoCancellationMode
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.PcmCaptureSource

/**
 * Owns the capture thread (the one bare Thread the audio path is allowed, spec §2) and pumps
 * 10 ms frames from a [PcmCaptureSource] to the listener. Spec B7/B8: reports platform silencing,
 * stops the source before joining, bounded join.
 */
class AudioInput(
    private val listener: AudioInputListener,
    private val source: PcmCaptureSource,
    private val stateListener: ((CaptureState) -> Unit)? = null,
    private val joinTimeoutMs: Long = DEFAULT_JOIN_TIMEOUT_MS,
) {
    /** Legacy bridge for the Java AudioHandler; deleted when AudioHandler is converted. */
    constructor(listener: AudioInputListener, audioSource: Int, targetSampleRate: Int, echoCancellationMethod: String) : this(
        listener,
        AndroidAudioRecordSource.Factory().open(
            CaptureRequest(audioSource, targetSampleRate, echo = EchoCancellationMode.fromPreferenceValue(echoCancellationMethod)),
        ),
    )

    fun interface AudioInputListener {
        fun onAudioInputReceived(frame: ShortArray, frameSize: Int)
    }

    val sampleRate: Int get() = source.sampleRate

    /** Samples per 10 ms at the source rate. */
    val frameSize: Int = source.sampleRate / 100

    @Volatile
    private var recording = false
    private var thread: Thread? = null

    @Synchronized
    fun startRecording() {
        check(thread == null) { "already recording" }
        recording = true
        source.setSilenceListener { silenced ->
            stateListener?.invoke(if (silenced) CaptureState.Silenced else CaptureState.Active)
        }
        thread = Thread(::loop, "humla-capture").also { it.start() }
    }

    /** Stops the loop: stops the source first (unblocks read), then joins with a timeout; never hangs the caller. */
    @Synchronized
    fun stopRecording() {
        val t = thread ?: return
        recording = false
        source.stop()
        t.interrupt()
        t.join(joinTimeoutMs)
        if (t.isAlive) Log.e(TAG, "capture thread did not stop within $joinTimeoutMs ms; releasing anyway")
        thread = null
        source.setSilenceListener(null)
    }

    fun shutdown() {
        stopRecording()
        source.release()
    }

    fun isRecording(): Boolean = recording

    private fun loop() {
        Process.setThreadPriority(Process.THREAD_PRIORITY_URGENT_AUDIO)
        try {
            source.start()
        } catch (e: IllegalStateException) {
            Log.e(TAG, "start failed", e)
            stateListener?.invoke(CaptureState.Error("AudioRecord start failed: ${e.message}"))
            return
        }
        Log.i(TAG, "started at $sampleRate Hz, frame $frameSize")
        val buffer = ShortArray(frameSize)
        while (recording) {
            val n = source.read(buffer, frameSize)
            if (n > 0) {
                listener.onAudioInputReceived(buffer, frameSize)
            } else if (n < 0 && recording) {
                Log.e(TAG, "AudioRecord read error $n")
                stateListener?.invoke(CaptureState.Error("AudioRecord read error $n"))
                break
            }
        }
        source.stop()
        Log.i(TAG, "stopped")
    }

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "AudioInput"
        const val DEFAULT_JOIN_TIMEOUT_MS = 2000L
    }
}
```

## Task 10: `AudioOutput` in Kotlin with a playback-sink seam; lock/wait/join fixes; far-end feed (B3, B8)

**Files:**
- Convert then modify: `libraries/humla/src/main/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/AudioOutput.java` → `AudioOutput.kt`
- Modify (one line): `libraries/humla/src/main/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/AudioOutputSpeech.java:371` `protected static class Result` → `public static class Result` (Kotlin cannot see a Java `protected` nested class from a non-subclass)
- Create: `libraries/humla/src/main/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/capture/PcmPlaybackSink.kt`
- Test: `libraries/humla/src/test/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/capture/fakes/FakePlaybackSink.kt`, `libraries/humla/src/test/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/AudioOutputTest.kt`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `FarEndSink`, `FarEndFrameChunker` (Task 6).
- Produces:
  ```kotlin
  interface PcmPlaybackSink { val minBufferSizeSamples: Int; fun play(); fun write(buffer: ShortArray, length: Int): Int; fun pause(); fun flush(); fun stop(); fun release() }
  fun interface PcmPlaybackSinkFactory { @Throws(AudioInitializationException::class) fun open(audioStream: Int, sampleRate: Int): PcmPlaybackSink }
  class AndroidAudioTrackSink : PcmPlaybackSink { class Factory : PcmPlaybackSinkFactory }
  class AudioOutput @JvmOverloads constructor(listener: AudioOutputListener, sinkFactory: PcmPlaybackSinkFactory = AndroidAudioTrackSink.Factory(), farEndSink: FarEndSink? = null, joinTimeoutMs: Long = 1000) : AudioOutputSpeech.TalkStateListener {
      internal constructor(listener, sinkFactory, farEndSink, joinTimeoutMs, mixProvider: MixProvider?)   // test seam, all five args required
      interface AudioOutputListener { fun onUserTalkStateUpdated(user: User); fun getUser(session: Int): User? }
      fun startPlaying(audioStream: Int); fun stopPlaying(); fun isPlaying(): Boolean; fun queueVoiceData(data: ByteArray, messageType: HumlaUDPMessageType)
  }
  ```
  Java callers (`AudioHandler.java` lines 133, 150, 202, 338, 406; `HumlaService` implements `AudioOutputListener`) keep compiling.

- [ ] **Step 1: Mechanical conversion of `AudioOutput.java` to Kotlin (no behavior change)**

First the one-line `AudioOutputSpeech.java` visibility change (`protected static class Result` → `public static class Result`). Then delete `AudioOutput.java` and create `AudioOutput.kt` (GPL header kept):
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio

import android.media.AudioFormat
import android.media.AudioTrack
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.Looper
import android.os.Process
import android.util.Log
import se.lublin.humla.exception.AudioInitializationException
import se.lublin.humla.exception.NativeAudioException
import se.lublin.humla.model.TalkState
import se.lublin.humla.model.User
import se.lublin.humla.net.HumlaUDPMessageType
import se.lublin.humla.net.PacketBuffer
import se.lublin.humla.protocol.AudioHandler
import java.util.Arrays
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutionException
import java.util.concurrent.Executors
import java.util.concurrent.locks.ReentrantLock

class AudioOutput(private val listener: AudioOutputListener) : Runnable, AudioOutputSpeech.TalkStateListener {
    interface AudioOutputListener {
        fun onUserTalkStateUpdated(user: User)
        fun getUser(session: Int): User?
    }

    private val audioOutputs = HashMap<Int, AudioOutputSpeech>()
    private var audioTrack: AudioTrack? = null
    private var bufferSize = 0
    private var thread: Thread? = null
    private val inactiveLock = Object()
    private val packetLock = ReentrantLock()
    private var running = false
    private val mainHandler = Handler(Looper.getMainLooper())
    private val mixer: IAudioMixer<FloatArray, ShortArray> = BasicClippingShortMixer()
    private val decodeExecutorService = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(Runtime.getRuntime().availableProcessors())

    @Throws(AudioInitializationException::class)
    fun startPlaying(audioStream: Int): Thread? {
        if (thread != null || running) return null
        val minBufferSize = AudioTrack.getMinBufferSize(AudioHandler.SAMPLE_RATE, AudioFormat.CHANNEL_OUT_MONO, AudioFormat.ENCODING_PCM_16BIT)
        bufferSize = minOf(minBufferSize, AudioHandler.FRAME_SIZE * 12)
        Log.v(TAG, "Using buffer size $bufferSize, system's min buffer size: $minBufferSize")
        audioTrack = try {
            @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
            AudioTrack(audioStream, AudioHandler.SAMPLE_RATE, AudioFormat.CHANNEL_OUT_MONO, AudioFormat.ENCODING_PCM_16BIT, bufferSize, AudioTrack.MODE_STREAM)
        } catch (e: IllegalArgumentException) {
            throw AudioInitializationException(e)
        }
        return Thread(this).also { thread = it; it.start() }
    }

    fun stopPlaying() {
        if (!running) return
        running = false
        synchronized(inactiveLock) { (inactiveLock as Object).notify() }
        try { thread?.join() } catch (e: InterruptedException) { e.printStackTrace() }
        thread = null
        packetLock.lock()
        for (speech in audioOutputs.values) speech.destroy()
        packetLock.unlock()
        audioOutputs.clear()
        audioTrack?.release()
        audioTrack = null
    }

    fun isPlaying(): Boolean = running

    override fun run() {
        Log.v(TAG, "Started thread.")
        Process.setThreadPriority(Process.THREAD_PRIORITY_URGENT_AUDIO)
        running = true
        val track = audioTrack ?: return
        track.play()
        val mix = ShortArray(bufferSize)
        while (running) {
            if (fetchAudio(mix, 0, bufferSize)) {
                track.write(mix, 0, bufferSize)
            } else {
                Log.v(TAG, "Pausing thread.")
                synchronized(inactiveLock) {
                    track.flush(); track.pause()
                    try { (inactiveLock as Object).wait() } catch (e: InterruptedException) { e.printStackTrace() }
                    track.play()
                }
                Log.v(TAG, "Resuming thread.")
            }
        }
        track.flush(); track.stop()
    }

    private fun fetchAudio(buffer: ShortArray, bufferOffset: Int, bufferSize: Int): Boolean {
        Arrays.fill(buffer, bufferOffset, bufferOffset + bufferSize, 0.toShort())
        val sources = ArrayList<IAudioMixerSource<FloatArray>>()
        try {
            packetLock.lock()
            val futures = decodeExecutorService.invokeAll(audioOutputs.values)
            for (future in futures) {
                val result = future.get()
                if (result.isAlive) {
                    sources.add(result)
                } else {
                    val speech = result.speechOutput
                    Log.v(TAG, "Deleted audio user " + speech.user.name)
                    audioOutputs.remove(speech.session)
                    speech.destroy()
                }
            }
        } catch (e: InterruptedException) {
            e.printStackTrace(); return false
        } catch (e: ExecutionException) {
            e.printStackTrace(); return false
        } finally {
            packetLock.unlock()
        }
        if (sources.isEmpty()) return false
        mixer.mix(sources, buffer, bufferOffset, bufferSize)
        return true
    }

    fun queueVoiceData(data: ByteArray, messageType: HumlaUDPMessageType) {
        if (!running) return
        val msgFlags = (data[0].toInt() and 0x1f).toByte()
        val pds = PacketBuffer(data, data.size)
        pds.skip(1)
        val session = pds.readLong().toInt()
        val user = listener.getUser(session)
        if (user != null && !user.isLocalMuted) {
            val seq = pds.readLong().toInt()
            packetLock.lock()
            var aop = audioOutputs[session]
            if (aop != null && aop.codec != messageType) { aop.destroy(); aop = null }
            if (aop == null) {
                aop = try {
                    AudioOutputSpeech(user, messageType, bufferSize, this)
                } catch (e: NativeAudioException) {
                    Log.v(TAG, "Failed to create audio user " + user.name); e.printStackTrace(); return
                }
                Log.v(TAG, "Created audio user " + user.name)
                audioOutputs[session] = aop
            }
            packetLock.unlock()
            aop.addFrameToBuffer(PacketBuffer(pds.bufferBlock(pds.left())), msgFlags, seq)
            synchronized(inactiveLock) { (inactiveLock as Object).notify() }
        }
    }

    override fun onTalkStateUpdated(session: Int, state: TalkState) {
        mainHandler.post {
            val user = listener.getUser(session)
            if (user != null && user.talkState != state) {
                user.talkState = state
                listener.onUserTalkStateUpdated(user)
            }
        }
    }

    private companion object {
        val TAG: String = AudioOutput::class.java.name
    }
}
```
(The lock leak on the `return` inside `queueVoiceData` is deliberately preserved in this step; it is fixed under test in Step 4.)

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the fake sink and the failing tests**

`libraries/humla/src/test/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/capture/fakes/FakePlaybackSink.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.fakes

import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.PcmPlaybackSink
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.PcmPlaybackSinkFactory
import java.util.concurrent.CopyOnWriteArrayList

class FakePlaybackSink(override val minBufferSizeSamples: Int = 1000, private val hangOnWrite: Boolean = false) : PcmPlaybackSink {
    val events = CopyOnWriteArrayList<String>()
    val written = CopyOnWriteArrayList<ShortArray>()
    override fun play() { events += "play" }
    override fun write(buffer: ShortArray, length: Int): Int {
        written += buffer.copyOf(length); events += "write"
        if (hangOnWrite) Thread.sleep(1500)
        return length
    }
    override fun pause() { events += "pause" }
    override fun flush() { events += "flush" }
    override fun stop() { events += "stop" }
    override fun release() { events += "release" }

    class Factory(val sink: FakePlaybackSink) : PcmPlaybackSinkFactory {
        var openedWith: Pair<Int, Int>? = null
        override fun open(audioStream: Int, sampleRate: Int): PcmPlaybackSink { openedWith = audioStream to sampleRate; return sink }
    }
}
```

`libraries/humla/src/test/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/AudioOutputTest.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio

import android.media.AudioManager
import com.google.common.truth.Truth.assertThat
import org.junit.Test
import org.junit.runner.RunWith
import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.fakes.FakePlaybackSink
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.fakes.RecordingFarEndSink
import se.lublin.humla.model.User
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger

@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
class AudioOutputTest {
    private val listener = object : AudioOutput.AudioOutputListener {
        override fun onUserTalkStateUpdated(user: User) = Unit
        override fun getUser(session: Int): User? = null
    }

    private fun await(timeoutMs: Long = 2000, condition: () -> Boolean) {
        val deadline = System.currentTimeMillis() + timeoutMs
        while (!condition() && System.currentTimeMillis() < deadline) Thread.sleep(5)
        assertThat(condition()).isTrue()
    }

    @Test
    fun `opens the sink for the requested stream at 48 kHz and idles when there is nothing to play`() {
        val sink = FakePlaybackSink(minBufferSizeSamples = 3000)
        val factory = FakePlaybackSink.Factory(sink)
        val output = AudioOutput(listener, factory)
        output.startPlaying(AudioManager.STREAM_VOICE_CALL)
        await { "pause" in sink.events }
        assertThat(factory.openedWith).isEqualTo(AudioManager.STREAM_VOICE_CALL to 48000)
        assertThat(output.isPlaying()).isTrue()
        output.stopPlaying()
        assertThat(output.isPlaying()).isFalse()
        assertThat(sink.events.takeLast(3)).containsExactly("flush", "stop", "release").inOrder()
    }

    @Test
    fun `stopPlaying while idle returns quickly`() {
        val sink = FakePlaybackSink()
        val output = AudioOutput(listener, FakePlaybackSink.Factory(sink))
        output.startPlaying(AudioManager.STREAM_MUSIC)
        await { "pause" in sink.events }
        val started = System.nanoTime()
        output.stopPlaying()
        assertThat((System.nanoTime() - started) / 1_000_000).isLessThan(500)
    }

    @Test
    fun `stopPlaying gives up after the join timeout when the sink hangs`() {
        val sink = FakePlaybackSink(hangOnWrite = true)
        val calls = AtomicInteger()
        val output = AudioOutput(listener, FakePlaybackSink.Factory(sink), null, 200,
            AudioOutput.MixProvider { buffer, _, _ -> buffer.fill(1); calls.incrementAndGet() < 5 })
        output.startPlaying(AudioManager.STREAM_MUSIC)
        await { "write" in sink.events }
        val started = System.nanoTime()
        output.stopPlaying()
        assertThat((System.nanoTime() - started) / 1_000_000).isLessThan(1000)
        assertThat(output.isPlaying()).isFalse()
    }

    @Test
    fun `mixed playback is written to the sink and fed to the far end in 480-sample frames`() {
        val sink = FakePlaybackSink(minBufferSizeSamples = 1000)   // buffer = min(1000, 5760) = 1000
        val farEnd = RecordingFarEndSink()
        val calls = AtomicInteger()
        val output = AudioOutput(listener, FakePlaybackSink.Factory(sink), farEnd, 1000,
            AudioOutput.MixProvider { buffer, _, length ->
                val n = calls.incrementAndGet()
                if (n > 2) false else { for (i in 0 until length) buffer[i] = ((n - 1) * 1000 + i).toShort(); true }
            })
        output.startPlaying(AudioManager.STREAM_MUSIC)
        await { farEnd.frames.size >= 4 }
        output.stopPlaying()
        assertThat(sink.written).hasSize(2)
        assertThat(sink.written[0].size).isEqualTo(1000)
        assertThat(farEnd.frames[0][0]).isEqualTo(0.toShort())
        assertThat(farEnd.frames[1][0]).isEqualTo(480.toShort())
        assertThat(farEnd.frames[2][0]).isEqualTo(960.toShort())      // 40 left over from the first buffer
        assertThat(farEnd.frames[2][40]).isEqualTo(1000.toShort())    // then the second buffer
        assertThat(farEnd.frames[3][0]).isEqualTo(1440.toShort())
    }
}
```

`libraries/humla/src/main/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/capture/PcmPlaybackSink.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.capture

import android.media.AudioAttributes
import android.media.AudioFormat
import android.media.AudioTrack
import se.lublin.humla.exception.AudioInitializationException

/** Blocking PCM 16-bit mono playback sink. Real: [AndroidAudioTrackSink]; tests use a fake. */
interface PcmPlaybackSink {
    /** The platform's minimum buffer size in samples for this sink. */
    val minBufferSizeSamples: Int
    fun play()
    /** Blocking write of [length] samples; returns the number written or a negative error. */
    fun write(buffer: ShortArray, length: Int): Int
    fun pause()
    fun flush()
    fun stop()
    fun release()
}

fun interface PcmPlaybackSinkFactory {
    @Throws(AudioInitializationException::class)
    fun open(audioStream: Int, sampleRate: Int): PcmPlaybackSink
}

class AndroidAudioTrackSink private constructor(
    private val track: AudioTrack,
    override val minBufferSizeSamples: Int,
) : PcmPlaybackSink {
    override fun play() = track.play()
    override fun write(buffer: ShortArray, length: Int): Int = track.write(buffer, 0, length)
    override fun pause() = track.pause()
    override fun flush() = track.flush()
    override fun stop() = track.stop()
    override fun release() = track.release()

    class Factory : PcmPlaybackSinkFactory {
        override fun open(audioStream: Int, sampleRate: Int): PcmPlaybackSink {
            val minBytes = AudioTrack.getMinBufferSize(sampleRate, AudioFormat.CHANNEL_OUT_MONO, AudioFormat.ENCODING_PCM_16BIT)
            if (minBytes <= 0) throw AudioInitializationException("AudioTrack.getMinBufferSize returned $minBytes")
            val track = try {
                AudioTrack.Builder()
                    .setAudioAttributes(AudioAttributes.Builder().setLegacyStreamType(audioStream).build())
                    .setAudioFormat(
                        AudioFormat.Builder().setSampleRate(sampleRate)
                            .setChannelMask(AudioFormat.CHANNEL_OUT_MONO)
                            .setEncoding(AudioFormat.ENCODING_PCM_16BIT).build(),
                    )
                    .setBufferSizeInBytes(minBytes)
                    .setTransferMode(AudioTrack.MODE_STREAM)
                    .build()
            } catch (e: IllegalArgumentException) {
                throw AudioInitializationException(e)
            } catch (e: UnsupportedOperationException) {
                throw AudioInitializationException(e)
            }
            if (track.state != AudioTrack.STATE_INITIALIZED) {
                track.release()
                throw AudioInitializationException("AudioTrack failed to initialize")
            }
            return AndroidAudioTrackSink(track, minBytes / 2)
        }
    }
}
```

`libraries/humla/src/main/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/AudioOutput.kt` — full replacement:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio

import android.os.Handler
import android.os.Looper
import android.os.Process
import android.util.Log
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.AndroidAudioTrackSink
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.FarEndFrameChunker
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.FarEndSink
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.PcmPlaybackSink
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.PcmPlaybackSinkFactory
import se.lublin.humla.exception.AudioInitializationException
import se.lublin.humla.exception.NativeAudioException
import se.lublin.humla.model.TalkState
import se.lublin.humla.model.User
import se.lublin.humla.net.HumlaUDPMessageType
import se.lublin.humla.net.PacketBuffer
import se.lublin.humla.protocol.AudioHandler
import java.util.Arrays
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutionException
import java.util.concurrent.Executors
import java.util.concurrent.locks.ReentrantLock
import kotlin.concurrent.withLock

/**
 * Owns the playback thread. Spec B8: packet lock always released in finally, idle wait is a
 * predicate wait with a 100 ms timeout, flags are volatile, bounded join. Spec B3: every mixed
 * buffer is also fed to the far-end sink (echo canceller reference).
 */
class AudioOutput internal constructor(
    private val listener: AudioOutputListener,
    private val sinkFactory: PcmPlaybackSinkFactory,
    private val farEndSink: FarEndSink?,
    private val joinTimeoutMs: Long,
    mixProvider: MixProvider?,
) : AudioOutputSpeech.TalkStateListener {
    @JvmOverloads
    constructor(
        listener: AudioOutputListener,
        sinkFactory: PcmPlaybackSinkFactory = AndroidAudioTrackSink.Factory(),
        farEndSink: FarEndSink? = null,
        joinTimeoutMs: Long = DEFAULT_JOIN_TIMEOUT_MS,
    ) : this(listener, sinkFactory, farEndSink, joinTimeoutMs, null)

    interface AudioOutputListener {
        fun onUserTalkStateUpdated(user: User)
        fun getUser(session: Int): User?
    }

    /** Test seam: fills [buffer] with mixed audio; false when there is nothing to play. */
    internal fun interface MixProvider {
        fun fetch(buffer: ShortArray, offset: Int, length: Int): Boolean
    }

    private val audioOutputs = HashMap<Int, AudioOutputSpeech>()
    private val packetLock = ReentrantLock()
    private val inactiveLock = Object()
    private val mixer: IAudioMixer<FloatArray, ShortArray> = BasicClippingShortMixer()
    private val decodeExecutorService = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(Runtime.getRuntime().availableProcessors())
    private val mainHandler = Handler(Looper.getMainLooper())
    private val mix: MixProvider = mixProvider ?: MixProvider { b, o, l -> fetchAudio(b, o, l) }

    @Volatile
    private var running = false

    @Volatile
    private var pendingAudio = false
    private var sink: PcmPlaybackSink? = null
    private var bufferSize = 0
    private var thread: Thread? = null
    private var chunker: FarEndFrameChunker? = null

    @Synchronized
    @Throws(AudioInitializationException::class)
    fun startPlaying(audioStream: Int) {
        if (thread != null) return
        val opened = sinkFactory.open(audioStream, AudioHandler.SAMPLE_RATE)
        bufferSize = minOf(opened.minBufferSizeSamples, AudioHandler.FRAME_SIZE * 12)
        Log.v(TAG, "Using buffer size $bufferSize, system's min buffer size: ${opened.minBufferSizeSamples}")
        sink = opened
        chunker = farEndSink?.let { FarEndFrameChunker(AudioHandler.FRAME_SIZE, it) }
        running = true
        thread = Thread(::loop, "humla-playback").also { it.start() }
    }

    @Synchronized
    fun stopPlaying() {
        val t = thread ?: return
        running = false
        synchronized(inactiveLock) { inactiveLock.notifyAll() }
        t.join(joinTimeoutMs)
        if (t.isAlive) Log.e(TAG, "playback thread did not stop within $joinTimeoutMs ms; releasing anyway")
        thread = null
        packetLock.withLock {
            audioOutputs.values.forEach { it.destroy() }
            audioOutputs.clear()
        }
        sink?.release()
        sink = null
        chunker = null
    }

    fun isPlaying(): Boolean = running

    private fun loop() {
        Log.v(TAG, "Started thread.")
        Process.setThreadPriority(Process.THREAD_PRIORITY_URGENT_AUDIO)
        val s = sink ?: return
        s.play()
        val buffer = ShortArray(bufferSize)
        while (running) {
            if (mix.fetch(buffer, 0, bufferSize)) {
                s.write(buffer, bufferSize)
                chunker?.push(buffer, bufferSize)
            } else {
                Log.v(TAG, "Pausing thread.")
                s.flush()
                s.pause()
                synchronized(inactiveLock) {
                    while (running && !pendingAudio) inactiveLock.wait(IDLE_WAIT_MS)
                    pendingAudio = false
                }
                if (running) s.play()
                Log.v(TAG, "Resuming thread.")
            }
        }
        s.flush()
        s.stop()
    }

    private fun fetchAudio(buffer: ShortArray, bufferOffset: Int, length: Int): Boolean {
        Arrays.fill(buffer, bufferOffset, bufferOffset + length, 0.toShort())
        val sources = ArrayList<IAudioMixerSource<FloatArray>>()
        packetLock.lock()
        try {
            val futures = decodeExecutorService.invokeAll(audioOutputs.values)
            for (future in futures) {
                val result = future.get()
                if (result.isAlive) {
                    sources.add(result)
                } else {
                    val speech = result.speechOutput
                    Log.v(TAG, "Deleted audio user " + speech.user.name)
                    audioOutputs.remove(speech.session)
                    speech.destroy()
                }
            }
        } catch (e: InterruptedException) {
            Log.w(TAG, "decode interrupted", e); return false
        } catch (e: ExecutionException) {
            Log.e(TAG, "decode failed", e); return false
        } finally {
            packetLock.unlock()
        }
        if (sources.isEmpty()) return false
        mixer.mix(sources, buffer, bufferOffset, length)
        return true
    }

    fun queueVoiceData(data: ByteArray, messageType: HumlaUDPMessageType) {
        if (!running) return
        val msgFlags = (data[0].toInt() and 0x1f).toByte()
        val pds = PacketBuffer(data, data.size)
        pds.skip(1)
        val session = pds.readLong().toInt()
        val user = listener.getUser(session) ?: return
        if (user.isLocalMuted) return
        val seq = pds.readLong().toInt()

        val speech: AudioOutputSpeech
        packetLock.lock()
        try {
            var existing = audioOutputs[session]
            if (existing != null && existing.codec != messageType) {
                existing.destroy()
                audioOutputs.remove(session)
                existing = null
            }
            if (existing == null) {
                existing = try {
                    AudioOutputSpeech(user, messageType, bufferSize, this)
                } catch (e: NativeAudioException) {
                    Log.e(TAG, "Failed to create audio user " + user.name, e)
                    return
                }
                Log.v(TAG, "Created audio user " + user.name)
                audioOutputs[session] = existing
            }
            speech = existing
        } finally {
            packetLock.unlock()
        }

        speech.addFrameToBuffer(PacketBuffer(pds.bufferBlock(pds.left())), msgFlags, seq)
        synchronized(inactiveLock) {
            pendingAudio = true
            inactiveLock.notifyAll()
        }
    }

    override fun onTalkStateUpdated(session: Int, state: TalkState) {
        mainHandler.post {
            val user = listener.getUser(session)
            if (user != null && user.talkState != state) {
                user.talkState = state
                listener.onUserTalkStateUpdated(user)
            }
        }
    }

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "AudioOutput"
        const val DEFAULT_JOIN_TIMEOUT_MS = 1000L
        const val IDLE_WAIT_MS = 100L
    }
}
```
`AudioHandler.java` line 150 currently reads `mOutput.startPlaying(...)` and ignores the returned `Thread`; the new method returns `Unit`, which is still a valid statement in Java — no change needed.

## Task 11: `AudioHandler` in Kotlin — pipeline wiring, Builder API for stream A, `captureState`, silence retry, bounded shutdown (B1, B2, B3, B6, B7, B11, spec §4)

**Files:**

- Create: `libraries/humla/src/main/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/encoder/EncoderFactory.kt`
- Delete: `libraries/humla/src/main/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/encoder/PreprocessingEncoder.java`, `ResamplingEncoder.java`
