- Test: `libraries/humla/src/test/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/capture/fakes/FakeEncoder.kt`, `libraries/humla/src/test/java/se/lublin/humla/protocol/AudioHandlerTest.kt`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: everything from Tasks 4–10.
- Produces (spec §4, used by stream A):
  ```kotlin
  class AudioHandler : HumlaNetworkListener(), AudioInput.AudioInputListener {
      val captureState: StateFlow<CaptureState>
      fun isInitialized(): Boolean; fun isPlaying(): Boolean; fun getCodec(): HumlaUDPMessageType?; fun recreateEncoder(); fun setCodec(codec: HumlaUDPMessageType?)
      fun getAudioStream(): Int; fun getAudioSource(): Int; fun getSampleRate(): Int; fun getBitrate(): Int; fun getFramesPerPacket(): Int; fun getAmplitudeBoost(): Float; fun isHalfDuplex(): Boolean; fun getCurrentBandwidth(): Int
      fun shutdown()   // any thread, ≤ 3 s
      fun setVoiceTargetId(id: Byte); fun clearVoiceTarget()
      interface AudioEncodeListener { fun onAudioEncoded(data: ByteArray, length: Int); fun onTalkingStateChanged(talking: Boolean) }
      class Builder { … existing setters …; fun setNoiseSuppression(mode: NoiseSuppressionMode): Builder; fun setEchoCancellation(mode: EchoCancellationMode): Builder; fun setVadConfig(config: VadConfig): Builder; fun setAudioEffects(noiseSuppressor: Boolean, automaticGainControl: Boolean): Builder; @Throws(AudioException::class) fun initialize(self: User, maxBandwidth: Int, codec: HumlaUDPMessageType?, targetId: Byte): AudioHandler }
      companion object { const val SAMPLE_RATE = 48000; const val FRAME_SIZE = 480; const val MAX_BUFFER_SIZE = 960 }
  }
  fun interface EncoderFactory { fun create(codec: HumlaUDPMessageType, framesPerPacket: Int, bitrate: Int): IEncoder?; companion object { @JvmField val DEFAULT: EncoderFactory } }
  ```
  Legacy setters `setPreprocessorEnabled(Boolean)` (true → SPEEX, false → NONE) and `setEchoCancellationMethod(String)` (via `EchoCancellationMode.fromPreferenceValue`) stay so `HumlaService.configureExtras` compiles until stream A maps the new `EXTRAS_*` keys.

- [ ] **Step 1: Mechanical conversion of `AudioHandler.java` to Kotlin (no behavior change)**

Delete `AudioHandler.java`; create `libraries/humla/src/main/java/se/lublin/humla/protocol/AudioHandler.kt` (GPL header kept):
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.protocol

import android.content.Context
import android.media.AudioManager
import android.media.MediaRecorder
import android.util.Log
import se.lublin.humla.R
import se.lublin.humla.audio.AudioInput
import se.lublin.humla.audio.AudioOutput
import se.lublin.humla.audio.encoder.CELT11Encoder
import se.lublin.humla.audio.encoder.CELT7Encoder
import se.lublin.humla.audio.encoder.IEncoder
import se.lublin.humla.audio.encoder.OpusEncoder
import se.lublin.humla.audio.encoder.PreprocessingEncoder
import se.lublin.humla.audio.encoder.ResamplingEncoder
import se.lublin.humla.audio.inputmode.IInputMode
import se.lublin.humla.exception.AudioException
import se.lublin.humla.exception.NativeAudioException
import se.lublin.humla.model.User
import se.lublin.humla.net.HumlaConnection
import se.lublin.humla.net.HumlaUDPMessageType
import se.lublin.humla.net.PacketBuffer
import se.lublin.humla.protobuf.Mumble
import se.lublin.humla.util.HumlaLogger
import se.lublin.humla.util.HumlaNetworkListener

class AudioHandler private constructor(
    private val context: Context,
    private val logger: HumlaLogger,
    private val audioStream: Int,
    audioSource: Int,
    private val sampleRate: Int,
    private var bitrate: Int,
    private var framesPerPacket: Int,
    private val inputMode: IInputMode,
    private var targetId: Byte,
    private val amplitudeBoost: Float,
    private var bluetoothOn: Boolean,
    private val halfDuplex: Boolean,
    private val preprocessorEnabled: Boolean,
    private val echoCancellationMethod: String,
    private val encodeListener: AudioEncodeListener,
    outputListener: AudioOutput.AudioOutputListener,
) : HumlaNetworkListener(), AudioInput.AudioInputListener {
    private val audioManager = context.getSystemService(Context.AUDIO_SERVICE) as AudioManager
    private val audioSource: Int
    private val input: AudioInput
    private val output: AudioOutput
    private var session = 0
    private var codec: HumlaUDPMessageType? = null
    private var encoder: IEncoder? = null
    private var frameCounter = 0
    private var initialized = false
    private var muted = false
    private var talking = false
    private val encoderLock = Any()

    init {
        var actualSource = audioSource
        if (echoCancellationMethod == "system") {
            audioManager.mode = AudioManager.MODE_IN_COMMUNICATION
            actualSource = MediaRecorder.AudioSource.VOICE_COMMUNICATION
        }
        this.audioSource = actualSource
        input = AudioInput(this, actualSource, sampleRate, echoCancellationMethod)
        output = AudioOutput(outputListener)
    }

    @Synchronized
    @Throws(AudioException::class)
    fun initialize(self: User, maxBandwidth: Int, codec: HumlaUDPMessageType?) {
        if (initialized) return
        session = self.session
        setMaxBandwidth(maxBandwidth)
        setCodec(codec)
        muted = self.isMuted || self.isLocalMuted || self.isSuppressed
        synchronized(input) {
            if (input.isRecording()) throw AudioException("Attempted to start recording while recording!")
            input.startRecording()
        }
        output.startPlaying(if (bluetoothOn) AudioManager.STREAM_VOICE_CALL else audioStream)
        initialized = true
    }

    fun isInitialized(): Boolean = initialized
    fun isPlaying(): Boolean = synchronized(output) { output.isPlaying() }
    fun getCodec(): HumlaUDPMessageType? = codec
    @Throws(NativeAudioException::class) fun recreateEncoder() = setCodec(codec)

    @Throws(NativeAudioException::class)
    fun setCodec(codec: HumlaUDPMessageType?) {
        this.codec = codec
        encoder?.destroy(); encoder = null
        if (codec == null) { Log.w(TAG, "setCodec(null) Input disabled."); return }
        var created: IEncoder = when (codec) {
            HumlaUDPMessageType.UDPVoiceCELTAlpha -> CELT7Encoder(SAMPLE_RATE, FRAME_SIZE, 1, framesPerPacket, bitrate, MAX_BUFFER_SIZE)
            HumlaUDPMessageType.UDPVoiceCELTBeta -> CELT11Encoder(SAMPLE_RATE, 1, framesPerPacket)
            HumlaUDPMessageType.UDPVoiceOpus -> OpusEncoder(SAMPLE_RATE, 1, FRAME_SIZE, framesPerPacket, bitrate, MAX_BUFFER_SIZE)
            else -> { Log.w(TAG, "Unsupported codec, input disabled."); return }
        }
        if (preprocessorEnabled) created = PreprocessingEncoder(created, FRAME_SIZE, SAMPLE_RATE)
        if (input.sampleRate != SAMPLE_RATE) created = ResamplingEncoder(created, 1, input.sampleRate, FRAME_SIZE, SAMPLE_RATE)
        encoder = created
    }

    fun getAudioStream(): Int = audioStream
    fun getAudioSource(): Int = audioSource
    fun getSampleRate(): Int = sampleRate
    fun getBitrate(): Int = bitrate
    fun getFramesPerPacket(): Int = framesPerPacket
    fun getAmplitudeBoost(): Float = amplitudeBoost
    fun isHalfDuplex(): Boolean = halfDuplex
    fun getCurrentBandwidth(): Int = HumlaConnection.calculateAudioBandwidth(bitrate, framesPerPacket)

    private fun setMaxBandwidth(maxBandwidth: Int) {
        if (maxBandwidth == -1) return
        var newBitrate = bitrate
        var newFpp = framesPerPacket
        if (HumlaConnection.calculateAudioBandwidth(newBitrate, newFpp) > maxBandwidth) {
            if (newFpp <= 4 && maxBandwidth <= 32000) newFpp = 4
            else if (newFpp == 1 && maxBandwidth <= 64000) newFpp = 2
            else if (newFpp == 2 && maxBandwidth <= 48000) newFpp = 4
            while (HumlaConnection.calculateAudioBandwidth(newBitrate, newFpp) > maxBandwidth && newBitrate > 8000) newBitrate -= 1000
        }
        newBitrate = maxOf(8000, newBitrate)
        if (newBitrate != bitrate || newFpp != framesPerPacket) {
            bitrate = newBitrate
            framesPerPacket = newFpp
            logger.logInfo(context.getString(R.string.audio_max_bandwidth, maxBandwidth / 1000, maxBandwidth / 1000, newFpp * 10))
        }
    }

    @Synchronized
    fun shutdown() {
        synchronized(input) { input.shutdown() }
        synchronized(output) { output.stopPlaying() }
        synchronized(encoderLock) { encoder?.destroy(); encoder = null }
        initialized = false
        bluetoothOn = false
        encodeListener.onTalkingStateChanged(false)
    }

    override fun messageCodecVersion(msg: Mumble.CodecVersion) {
        if (!initialized) return
        val newCodec = when {
            msg.hasOpus() && msg.opus -> HumlaUDPMessageType.UDPVoiceOpus
            msg.hasBeta() && !msg.preferAlpha -> HumlaUDPMessageType.UDPVoiceCELTBeta
            else -> HumlaUDPMessageType.UDPVoiceCELTAlpha
        }
        if (newCodec != codec) {
            try { synchronized(encoderLock) { setCodec(newCodec) } } catch (e: NativeAudioException) { e.printStackTrace() }
        }
    }

    override fun messageServerSync(msg: Mumble.ServerSync) {
        setMaxBandwidth(if (msg.hasMaxBandwidth()) msg.maxBandwidth else -1)
    }

    override fun messageUserState(msg: Mumble.UserState) {
        if (!initialized) return
        if (msg.hasSession() && msg.session == session && (msg.hasMute() || msg.hasSelfMute() || msg.hasSuppress())) {
            muted = msg.mute || msg.selfMute || msg.suppress
        }
    }

    override fun messageVoiceData(data: ByteArray, messageType: HumlaUDPMessageType) {
        synchronized(output) { output.queueVoiceData(data, messageType) }
    }

    override fun onAudioInputReceived(frame: ShortArray, frameSize: Int) {
        var nowTalking = inputMode.shouldTransmit(frame, frameSize, null)
        nowTalking = nowTalking && !muted
        if (talking xor nowTalking) {
            encodeListener.onTalkingStateChanged(nowTalking)
            if (halfDuplex) audioManager.adjustStreamVolume(audioStream, if (nowTalking) AudioManager.ADJUST_MUTE else AudioManager.ADJUST_UNMUTE, 0)
            synchronized(encoderLock) {
                if (!nowTalking) try { encoder?.terminate() } catch (e: NativeAudioException) { e.printStackTrace() }
            }
        }
        if (nowTalking) {
            if (amplitudeBoost != 1.0f) {
                for (i in 0 until frameSize) {
                    val v = frame[i] * amplitudeBoost
                    frame[i] = when { v > Short.MAX_VALUE -> Short.MAX_VALUE; v < Short.MIN_VALUE -> Short.MIN_VALUE; else -> v.toInt().toShort() }
                }
            }
            synchronized(encoderLock) {
                encoder?.let { try { it.encode(frame, frameSize); frameCounter++ } catch (e: NativeAudioException) { e.printStackTrace() } }
            }
        }
        synchronized(encoderLock) { encoder?.let { if (it.isReady) sendEncodedAudio(it) } }
        talking = nowTalking
        if (!nowTalking) inputMode.waitForInput()
    }

    fun setVoiceTargetId(id: Byte) { targetId = id }
    fun clearVoiceTarget() { targetId = 0 }

    private fun sendEncodedAudio(enc: IEncoder) {
        val frames = enc.bufferedFrames
        var flags = 0
        flags = flags or ((codec?.ordinal ?: 0) shl 5)
        flags = flags or (targetId.toInt() and 0x1F)
        val packetBuffer = ByteArray(1024)
        packetBuffer[0] = (flags and 0xFF).toByte()
        val ds = PacketBuffer(packetBuffer, 1024)
        ds.skip(1)
        ds.writeLong((frameCounter - frames).toLong())
        enc.getEncodedData(ds)
        val length = ds.size()
        ds.rewind()
        encodeListener.onAudioEncoded(ds.dataBlock(length), length)
    }

    interface AudioEncodeListener {
        fun onAudioEncoded(data: ByteArray, length: Int)
        fun onTalkingStateChanged(talking: Boolean)
    }

    class Builder {
        private var context: Context? = null
        private var logger: HumlaLogger? = null
        private var audioStream = 0
        private var audioSource = 0
        private var targetBitrate = 0
        private var targetFramesPerPacket = 0
        private var inputSampleRate = 0
        private var amplitudeBoost = 1f
        private var bluetoothEnabled = false
        private var halfDuplexEnabled = false
        private var preprocessorEnabled = false
        private var echoCancellationMethod = "none"
        private var inputMode: IInputMode? = null
        private var encodeListener: AudioEncodeListener? = null
        private var talkingListener: AudioOutput.AudioOutputListener? = null

        fun setContext(context: Context) = apply { this.context = context }
        fun setLogger(logger: HumlaLogger) = apply { this.logger = logger }
        fun setAudioStream(audioStream: Int) = apply { this.audioStream = audioStream }
        fun setAudioSource(audioSource: Int) = apply { this.audioSource = audioSource }
        fun setTargetBitrate(targetBitrate: Int) = apply { this.targetBitrate = targetBitrate }
        fun setTargetFramesPerPacket(targetFramesPerPacket: Int) = apply { this.targetFramesPerPacket = targetFramesPerPacket }
        fun setInputSampleRate(inputSampleRate: Int) = apply { this.inputSampleRate = inputSampleRate }
        fun setAmplitudeBoost(amplitudeBoost: Float) = apply { this.amplitudeBoost = amplitudeBoost }
        fun setBluetoothEnabled(bluetoothEnabled: Boolean) = apply { this.bluetoothEnabled = bluetoothEnabled }
        fun setHalfDuplexEnabled(halfDuplexEnabled: Boolean) = apply { this.halfDuplexEnabled = halfDuplexEnabled }
        fun setPreprocessorEnabled(preprocessorEnabled: Boolean) = apply { this.preprocessorEnabled = preprocessorEnabled }
        fun setEchoCancellationMethod(echoCancellationMethod: String) = apply { this.echoCancellationMethod = echoCancellationMethod }
        fun setEncodeListener(encodeListener: AudioEncodeListener) = apply { this.encodeListener = encodeListener }
        fun setTalkingListener(talkingListener: AudioOutput.AudioOutputListener) = apply { this.talkingListener = talkingListener }
        fun setInputMode(inputMode: IInputMode) = apply { this.inputMode = inputMode }

        @Throws(AudioException::class)
        fun initialize(self: User, maxBandwidth: Int, codec: HumlaUDPMessageType?, targetId: Byte): AudioHandler {
            val handler = AudioHandler(
                requireNotNull(context), requireNotNull(logger), audioStream, audioSource, inputSampleRate, targetBitrate,
                targetFramesPerPacket, requireNotNull(inputMode), targetId, amplitudeBoost, bluetoothEnabled, halfDuplexEnabled,
                preprocessorEnabled, echoCancellationMethod, requireNotNull(encodeListener), requireNotNull(talkingListener),
            )
            handler.initialize(self, maxBandwidth, codec)
            return handler
        }
    }

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "AudioHandler"
        const val SAMPLE_RATE = 48000
        const val FRAME_SIZE = SAMPLE_RATE / 100
        const val MAX_BUFFER_SIZE = 960
    }
}
```
(Only deliberate deviation: `AudioManager.setStreamMute` — deprecated since API 23 and a lint error at minSdk 31 — becomes `adjustStreamVolume(ADJUST_MUTE/ADJUST_UNMUTE)`, the documented replacement with identical semantics.)

`libraries/humla/src/test/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/capture/fakes/FakeEncoder.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.fakes

import se.lublin.humla.audio.encoder.EncoderFactory
import se.lublin.humla.audio.encoder.IEncoder
import se.lublin.humla.net.HumlaUDPMessageType
import se.lublin.humla.net.PacketBuffer
import java.util.concurrent.CopyOnWriteArrayList

/** One-frame-per-packet encoder that records what it was given. */
class FakeEncoder : IEncoder {
    val encodedFrames = CopyOnWriteArrayList<ShortArray>()
    @Volatile var terminated = 0
    @Volatile var destroyed = 0
    @Volatile private var buffered = 0

    override fun encode(input: ShortArray, inputSize: Int): Int { encodedFrames += input.copyOf(inputSize); buffered = 1; return inputSize }
    override fun getBufferedFrames(): Int = buffered
    override fun isReady(): Boolean = buffered > 0
    override fun getEncodedData(packetBuffer: PacketBuffer) { packetBuffer.append(byteArrayOf(0x7F), 1); buffered = 0 }
    override fun terminate() { terminated++ }
    override fun destroy() { destroyed++ }

    class Factory : EncoderFactory {
        val created = CopyOnWriteArrayList<Pair<HumlaUDPMessageType, FakeEncoder>>()
        override fun create(codec: HumlaUDPMessageType, framesPerPacket: Int, bitrate: Int): IEncoder =
            FakeEncoder().also { created += codec to it }
    }
}
```

`libraries/humla/src/test/java/se/lublin/humla/protocol/AudioHandlerTest.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.protocol

import android.media.AudioManager
import android.media.MediaRecorder
import androidx.test.core.app.ApplicationProvider
import com.google.common.truth.Truth.assertThat
import kotlinx.coroutines.ExperimentalCoroutinesApi
import kotlinx.coroutines.test.StandardTestDispatcher
import kotlinx.coroutines.test.TestScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.test.advanceTimeBy
import kotlinx.coroutines.test.runCurrent
import org.junit.Test
import org.junit.runner.RunWith
import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner
import se.lublin.humla.audio.AudioOutput
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.CaptureRequest
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.CaptureState
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.CapturePreprocessorFactory
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.EchoCancellationMode
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.NoiseSuppressionMode
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.PcmCaptureSource
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.PcmCaptureSourceFactory
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.fakes.FakeCaptureSource
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.fakes.FakeEncoder
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.fakes.FakePlaybackSink
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.fakes.FakeResampler
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.fakes.FakeRnnoiseApi
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.fakes.FakeSpeexPreprocessApi
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.fakes.FakeWebRtcApmApi
import se.lublin.humla.audio.inputmode.ActivityInputMode
import se.lublin.humla.audio.inputmode.ContinuousInputMode
import se.lublin.humla.audio.inputmode.IInputMode
import se.lublin.humla.model.User
import se.lublin.humla.net.HumlaUDPMessageType
import se.lublin.humla.util.HumlaLogger
import java.util.concurrent.CopyOnWriteArrayList

@OptIn(ExperimentalCoroutinesApi::class)
@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
class AudioHandlerTest {
    private val context = ApplicationProvider.getApplicationContext<android.content.Context>()
    private val audioManager = context.getSystemService(android.content.Context.AUDIO_SERVICE) as AudioManager
    private val warnings = CopyOnWriteArrayList<String>()
    private val logger = object : HumlaLogger {
        override fun logInfo(message: String) = Unit
        override fun logWarning(message: String) { warnings += message }
        override fun logError(message: String) = Unit
    }
    private val talkingStates = CopyOnWriteArrayList<Boolean>()
    private val packets = CopyOnWriteArrayList<ByteArray>()
    private val encodeListener = object : AudioHandler.AudioEncodeListener {
        override fun onAudioEncoded(data: ByteArray, length: Int) { packets += data.copyOf(length) }
        override fun onTalkingStateChanged(talking: Boolean) { talkingStates += talking }
    }
    private val outputListener = object : AudioOutput.AudioOutputListener {
        override fun onUserTalkStateUpdated(user: User) = Unit
        override fun getUser(session: Int): User? = null
    }
    private val self = User(7, "me")
    private val dispatcher = StandardTestDispatcher()
    private val scope = TestScope(dispatcher)
    private val encoders = FakeEncoder.Factory()
    private val speex = FakeSpeexPreprocessApi(probability = 90)
    private val resamplers = mutableListOf<Pair<Int, Int>>()

    private class RecordingSourceFactory(private val source: FakeCaptureSource) : PcmCaptureSourceFactory {
        var request: CaptureRequest? = null
        override fun open(request: CaptureRequest): PcmCaptureSource { this.request = request; return source }
    }

    private fun loud(value: Int = 8000) = ShortArray(480) { value.toShort() }

    private fun builder(source: FakeCaptureSource, inputMode: IInputMode = ContinuousInputMode()): Pair<AudioHandler.Builder, RecordingSourceFactory> {
        val factory = RecordingSourceFactory(source)
        val b = AudioHandler.Builder()
            .setContext(context).setLogger(logger).setAudioStream(AudioManager.STREAM_MUSIC)
            .setAudioSource(MediaRecorder.AudioSource.MIC).setInputSampleRate(48000)
            .setTargetBitrate(40000).setTargetFramesPerPacket(1).setAmplitudeBoost(1f)
            .setEncodeListener(encodeListener).setTalkingListener(outputListener).setInputMode(inputMode)
            .setCaptureSourceFactory(factory).setPlaybackSinkFactory(FakePlaybackSink.Factory(FakePlaybackSink()))
            .setPreprocessorFactory(CapturePreprocessorFactory({ speex }, { FakeRnnoiseApi() }, { FakeWebRtcApmApi() }) { warnings += it })
            .setResamplerFactory { inRate, outRate -> resamplers += inRate to outRate; FakeResampler(outRate / inRate) }
            .setEncoderFactory(encoders).setCoroutineScope(scope).setJoinTimeouts(300, 300)
        return b to factory
    }

    private fun await(timeoutMs: Long = 2000, condition: () -> Boolean) {
        val deadline = System.currentTimeMillis() + timeoutMs
        while (!condition() && System.currentTimeMillis() < deadline) Thread.sleep(5)
        assertThat(condition()).isTrue()
    }

    @Test
    fun `frames flow through the pipeline into encoded packets and the talking state`() {
        val source = FakeCaptureSource(frames = listOf(loud(), loud()))
        val (b, _) = builder(source)
        val handler = b.setNoiseSuppression(NoiseSuppressionMode.NONE).initialize(self, -1, HumlaUDPMessageType.UDPVoiceOpus, 0)
        await { packets.size >= 2 }
        handler.shutdown()
        assertThat(talkingStates.first()).isTrue()
        assertThat(encoders.created.single().first).isEqualTo(HumlaUDPMessageType.UDPVoiceOpus)
        assertThat(encoders.created.single().second.encodedFrames.first()[0]).isEqualTo(8000.toShort())
        assertThat(packets[0][0].toInt() and 0xFF).isEqualTo(HumlaUDPMessageType.UDPVoiceOpus.ordinal shl 5)
        assertThat(talkingStates.last()).isFalse()   // shutdown reports not talking
    }

    /** Handler-level regression: the denoiser output, not the microphone signal, drives voice activation. */
    @Test
    fun `voice activation uses the preprocessed frame`() {
        val gate = FakeSpeexPreprocessApi(probability = 0, onRun = { it.fill(0) })
        val source = FakeCaptureSource(frames = List(5) { loud() })
        val (b, _) = builder(source, ActivityInputMode(0.5f))
        b.setPreprocessorFactory(CapturePreprocessorFactory({ gate }, { FakeRnnoiseApi() }, { FakeWebRtcApmApi() }))
        val handler = b.setNoiseSuppression(NoiseSuppressionMode.SPEEX).initialize(self, -1, HumlaUDPMessageType.UDPVoiceOpus, 0)
        await { gate.getRequests.size >= 5 }
        handler.shutdown()
        assertThat(packets).isEmpty()
        assertThat(talkingStates).doesNotContain(true)
    }

    @Test
    fun `silenced capture is exposed and capture restarts after two seconds`() {
        val source = FakeCaptureSource()
        val (b, _) = builder(source)
        val handler = b.initialize(self, -1, HumlaUDPMessageType.UDPVoiceOpus, 0)
        await { "start" in source.events }
        source.silenceListener!!.invoke(true)
        assertThat(handler.captureState.value).isEqualTo(CaptureState.Silenced)
        scope.advanceTimeBy(1999); scope.runCurrent()
        assertThat(source.events.count { it == "start" }).isEqualTo(1)
        scope.advanceTimeBy(2); scope.runCurrent()
        await { source.events.count { it == "start" } == 2 }
        handler.shutdown()
    }

    @Test
    fun `shutdown returns within the configured join budget even when the source hangs`() {
        val source = FakeCaptureSource(ignoreStop = true)
        val (b, _) = builder(source)
        val handler = b.initialize(self, -1, HumlaUDPMessageType.UDPVoiceOpus, 0)
        await { "start" in source.events }
        val started = System.nanoTime()
        handler.shutdown()
        assertThat((System.nanoTime() - started) / 1_000_000).isLessThan(1500)
        assertThat(handler.isInitialized()).isFalse()
    }

    @Test
    fun `android effects switch to voice communication source and communication mode`() {
        val (b, factory) = builder(FakeCaptureSource())
        val handler = b.setAudioEffects(noiseSuppressor = true, automaticGainControl = false).initialize(self, -1, null, 0)
        assertThat(factory.request!!.audioSource).isEqualTo(MediaRecorder.AudioSource.VOICE_COMMUNICATION)
        assertThat(factory.request!!.effects.noiseSuppressor).isTrue()
        assertThat(audioManager.mode).isEqualTo(AudioManager.MODE_IN_COMMUNICATION)
        handler.shutdown()
        assertThat(audioManager.mode).isEqualTo(AudioManager.MODE_NORMAL)
    }

    @Test
    fun `plain capture keeps the requested source and normal mode`() {
        val (b, factory) = builder(FakeCaptureSource())
        val handler = b.initialize(self, -1, null, 0)
        assertThat(factory.request!!.audioSource).isEqualTo(MediaRecorder.AudioSource.MIC)
        assertThat(audioManager.mode).isEqualTo(AudioManager.MODE_NORMAL)
        handler.shutdown()
    }

    @Test
    fun `bluetooth capture runs at the sco rate and is resampled to 48 kHz`() {
        val (b, factory) = builder(FakeCaptureSource(sampleRate = 16000))
        val handler = b.setBluetoothEnabled(true).initialize(self, -1, null, 0)
        assertThat(factory.request!!.targetSampleRate).isEqualTo(16000)   // no SCO device in Robolectric → policy default
        assertThat(resamplers).containsExactly(16000 to 48000)
        handler.shutdown()
    }

    @Test
    fun `legacy builder setters map onto the new modes`() {
        val (b, factory) = builder(FakeCaptureSource())
        val handler = b.setPreprocessorEnabled(false).setEchoCancellationMethod("system").initialize(self, -1, null, 0)
        assertThat(factory.request!!.echo).isEqualTo(EchoCancellationMode.ANDROID)
        assertThat(speex.created).isEqualTo(0)
        handler.shutdown()
    }

    @Test
    fun `codec change from the server recreates the encoder`() {
        val (b, _) = builder(FakeCaptureSource())
        val handler = b.initialize(self, -1, HumlaUDPMessageType.UDPVoiceCELTAlpha, 0)
        handler.messageCodecVersion(se.lublin.humla.protobuf.Mumble.CodecVersion.newBuilder().setAlpha(0).setBeta(0).setPreferAlpha(true).setOpus(true).build())
        assertThat(encoders.created.map { it.first }).containsExactly(HumlaUDPMessageType.UDPVoiceCELTAlpha, HumlaUDPMessageType.UDPVoiceOpus).inOrder()
        assertThat(encoders.created[0].second.destroyed).isEqualTo(1)
        handler.shutdown()
    }
}
```

`libraries/humla/src/main/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/encoder/EncoderFactory.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.audio.encoder

import se.lublin.humla.exception.NativeAudioException
import se.lublin.humla.net.HumlaUDPMessageType
import se.lublin.humla.protocol.AudioHandler

/** Creates the outgoing codec encoder; null for codecs this client cannot encode. */
fun interface EncoderFactory {
    @Throws(NativeAudioException::class)
    fun create(codec: HumlaUDPMessageType, framesPerPacket: Int, bitrate: Int): IEncoder?

    companion object {
        @JvmField
        val DEFAULT: EncoderFactory = EncoderFactory { codec, framesPerPacket, bitrate ->
            when (codec) {
                HumlaUDPMessageType.UDPVoiceCELTAlpha ->
                    CELT7Encoder(AudioHandler.SAMPLE_RATE, AudioHandler.FRAME_SIZE, 1, framesPerPacket, bitrate, AudioHandler.MAX_BUFFER_SIZE)
                HumlaUDPMessageType.UDPVoiceCELTBeta -> CELT11Encoder(AudioHandler.SAMPLE_RATE, 1, framesPerPacket)
                HumlaUDPMessageType.UDPVoiceOpus ->
                    OpusEncoder(AudioHandler.SAMPLE_RATE, 1, AudioHandler.FRAME_SIZE, framesPerPacket, bitrate, AudioHandler.MAX_BUFFER_SIZE)
                else -> null
            }
        }
    }
}
```

```kotlin
package se.lublin.humla.protocol

import android.content.Context
import android.media.AudioManager
import android.util.Log
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.Job
import kotlinx.coroutines.SupervisorJob
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay

import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import se.lublin.humla.R
import se.lublin.humla.audio.AudioInput
import se.lublin.humla.audio.AudioOutput
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.AndroidAudioRecordSource
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.AndroidAudioTrackSink

import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.EchoCancellationMode
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.NoiseSuppressionMode
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.PcmCaptureSourceFactory
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.PcmPlaybackSinkFactory
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.Resampler
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.ScoSampleRatePolicy
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.SpeexResampler
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.VadConfig
import se.lublin.humla.audio.encoder.EncoderFactory

import se.lublin.humla.audio.inputmode.ActivityInputMode
import se.lublin.humla.audio.inputmode.IInputMode
import se.lublin.humla.exception.AudioException
import se.lublin.humla.exception.NativeAudioException
import se.lublin.humla.model.User
import se.lublin.humla.net.HumlaConnection
import se.lublin.humla.net.HumlaUDPMessageType
import se.lublin.humla.net.PacketBuffer
import se.lublin.humla.protobuf.Mumble
import se.lublin.humla.util.HumlaLogger
import se.lublin.humla.util.HumlaNetworkListener

/**
 * Bridges the protocol's audio messages to the capture pipeline and the playback thread.
 * Capture path (spec B1): AudioInput → CapturePipeline (resample → preprocess → VAD → boost) → encoder → listener.
 * [shutdown] is safe from any thread and bounded by the capture and playback join timeouts (2 s + 1 s by default).
 */
class AudioHandler private constructor(
    private val context: Context,
    private val logger: HumlaLogger,
    private val audioStream: Int,
    requestedAudioSource: Int,
    private val inputSampleRate: Int,
    private var bitrate: Int,
    private var framesPerPacket: Int,
    private val inputMode: IInputMode,
    private var targetId: Byte,
    private val amplitudeBoost: Float,
    private var bluetoothOn: Boolean,
    private val halfDuplex: Boolean,
    private val noiseSuppression: NoiseSuppressionMode,
    private val echoCancellation: EchoCancellationMode,
    private val audioEffects: AndroidAudioEffects,
    vadConfig: VadConfig?,
    private val encodeListener: AudioEncodeListener,
    outputListener: AudioOutput.AudioOutputListener,
    captureSourceFactory: PcmCaptureSourceFactory,
    playbackSinkFactory: PcmPlaybackSinkFactory,
    preprocessorFactory: CapturePreprocessorFactory,
    resamplerFactory: (inputRate: Int, outputRate: Int) -> Resampler,
    private val encoderFactory: EncoderFactory,
    private val scope: CoroutineScope,
    captureJoinTimeoutMs: Long,
    playbackJoinTimeoutMs: Long,
) : HumlaNetworkListener(), AudioInput.AudioInputListener {
    private val audioManager = context.getSystemService(Context.AUDIO_SERVICE) as AudioManager
    private val audioSource: Int = AudioSourcePolicy.resolve(requestedAudioSource, audioEffects, echoCancellation)
    private val communicationMode = AudioSourcePolicy.needsCommunicationMode(audioEffects, echoCancellation)
    private val input: AudioInput
    private val output: AudioOutput
    private val pipeline: CapturePipeline

    private val _captureState = MutableStateFlow<CaptureState>(CaptureState.Active)
    /** Spec §4: Active / Silenced / Error(msg); HumlaService forwards Silenced and Error to the chat log. */
    val captureState: StateFlow<CaptureState> = _captureState.asStateFlow()

    private var session = 0
    private var codec: HumlaUDPMessageType? = null
    private var encoder: IEncoder? = null
    private var frameCounter = 0
    @Volatile private var initialized = false
    @Volatile private var muted = false
    private var talking = false
    private val encoderLock = Any()
    private var restartJob: Job? = null

    init {
        if (communicationMode) audioManager.mode = AudioManager.MODE_IN_COMMUNICATION
        if (vadConfig != null && inputMode is ActivityInputMode) inputMode.setVadConfig(vadConfig)

        val scoDevice = if (bluetoothOn) ScoSampleRatePolicy.findScoInput(audioManager) else null
        val targetRate = if (bluetoothOn) ScoSampleRatePolicy.choose(scoDevice?.sampleRates ?: intArrayOf()) else inputSampleRate
        val source = captureSourceFactory.open(CaptureRequest(audioSource, targetRate, audioEffects, echoCancellation, scoDevice))
        val chain = preprocessorFactory.create(noiseSuppression, echoCancellation)
        val resampler = if (source.sampleRate != SAMPLE_RATE) resamplerFactory(source.sampleRate, SAMPLE_RATE) else null
        pipeline = CapturePipeline(resampler, chain.preprocessor, inputMode, amplitudeBoost)
        input = AudioInput(this, source, ::onCaptureStateChanged, captureJoinTimeoutMs)
        output = AudioOutput(outputListener, playbackSinkFactory, chain.farEndSink, playbackJoinTimeoutMs)
        Log.i(TAG, "capture ${source.sampleRate} Hz source=$audioSource ns=$noiseSuppression aec=$echoCancellation effects=$audioEffects bluetooth=$bluetoothOn")
    }

    @Synchronized
    @Throws(AudioException::class)
    fun initialize(self: User, maxBandwidth: Int, codec: HumlaUDPMessageType?) {
        if (initialized) return
        session = self.session
        setMaxBandwidth(maxBandwidth)
        setCodec(codec)
        muted = self.isMuted || self.isLocalMuted || self.isSuppressed
        if (input.isRecording()) throw AudioException("Attempted to start recording while recording!")
        input.startRecording()
        // SCO requires the VOICE_CALL stream.
        output.startPlaying(if (bluetoothOn) AudioManager.STREAM_VOICE_CALL else audioStream)
        initialized = true
    }

    fun isInitialized(): Boolean = initialized
    fun isPlaying(): Boolean = output.isPlaying()
    fun getCodec(): HumlaUDPMessageType? = codec

    @Throws(NativeAudioException::class)
    fun recreateEncoder() = setCodec(codec)

    @Throws(NativeAudioException::class)
    fun setCodec(codec: HumlaUDPMessageType?) {
        synchronized(encoderLock) {
            this.codec = codec
            encoder?.destroy()
            encoder = null
            if (codec == null) {
                Log.w(TAG, "setCodec(null) Input disabled.")
                return
            }
            encoder = encoderFactory.create(codec, framesPerPacket, bitrate)
            if (encoder == null) Log.w(TAG, "Unsupported codec $codec, input disabled.")
        }
    }

    fun getAudioStream(): Int = audioStream
    fun getAudioSource(): Int = audioSource
    fun getSampleRate(): Int = input.sampleRate
    fun getBitrate(): Int = bitrate
    fun getFramesPerPacket(): Int = framesPerPacket
    fun getAmplitudeBoost(): Float = amplitudeBoost
    fun isHalfDuplex(): Boolean = halfDuplex
    fun getCurrentBandwidth(): Int = HumlaConnection.calculateAudioBandwidth(bitrate, framesPerPacket)

    /** Desktop Mumble's AudioInput::adjustBandwidth. */
    private fun setMaxBandwidth(maxBandwidth: Int) {
        if (maxBandwidth == -1) return
        var newBitrate = bitrate
        var newFpp = framesPerPacket
        if (HumlaConnection.calculateAudioBandwidth(newBitrate, newFpp) > maxBandwidth) {
            if (newFpp <= 4 && maxBandwidth <= 32000) newFpp = 4
            else if (newFpp == 1 && maxBandwidth <= 64000) newFpp = 2
            else if (newFpp == 2 && maxBandwidth <= 48000) newFpp = 4
            while (HumlaConnection.calculateAudioBandwidth(newBitrate, newFpp) > maxBandwidth && newBitrate > 8000) newBitrate -= 1000
        }
        newBitrate = maxOf(8000, newBitrate)
        if (newBitrate != bitrate || newFpp != framesPerPacket) {
            bitrate = newBitrate
            framesPerPacket = newFpp
            logger.logInfo(context.getString(R.string.audio_max_bandwidth, maxBandwidth / 1000, maxBandwidth / 1000, newFpp * 10))
        }
    }

    /** Safe from any thread. Worst case: capture join + playback join timeouts. */
    @Synchronized
    fun shutdown() {
        restartJob?.cancel()
        restartJob = null
        input.shutdown()
        output.stopPlaying()
        synchronized(encoderLock) {
            encoder?.destroy()
            encoder = null
        }
        pipeline.release()
        if (communicationMode) audioManager.mode = AudioManager.MODE_NORMAL
        initialized = false
        bluetoothOn = false
        encodeListener.onTalkingStateChanged(false)
    }

    private fun onCaptureStateChanged(state: CaptureState) {
        _captureState.value = state
        if (state == CaptureState.Silenced) {
            restartJob?.cancel()
            restartJob = scope.launch {
                delay(SILENCE_RETRY_MS)
                restartCapture()
            }
        }
    }

    /** Spec B7: after the OS silenced us, re-open the capture stream (a fresh AudioRecord may be audible again). */
    @Synchronized
    private fun restartCapture() {
        if (!initialized) return
        Log.w(TAG, "capture was silenced; restarting AudioRecord")
        input.stopRecording()
        input.startRecording()
    }

    override fun messageCodecVersion(msg: Mumble.CodecVersion) {
        if (!initialized) return
        val newCodec = when {
            msg.hasOpus() && msg.opus -> HumlaUDPMessageType.UDPVoiceOpus
            msg.hasBeta() && !msg.preferAlpha -> HumlaUDPMessageType.UDPVoiceCELTBeta
            else -> HumlaUDPMessageType.UDPVoiceCELTAlpha
        }
        if (newCodec != codec) {
            try {
                setCodec(newCodec)
            } catch (e: NativeAudioException) {
                Log.e(TAG, "encoder creation failed", e)
                _captureState.value = CaptureState.Error("Encoder creation failed: ${e.message}")
            }
        }
    }

    override fun messageServerSync(msg: Mumble.ServerSync) {
        setMaxBandwidth(if (msg.hasMaxBandwidth()) msg.maxBandwidth else -1)
    }

    override fun messageUserState(msg: Mumble.UserState) {
        if (!initialized) return
        if (msg.hasSession() && msg.session == session && (msg.hasMute() || msg.hasSelfMute() || msg.hasSuppress())) {
            muted = msg.mute || msg.selfMute || msg.suppress
        }
    }

    override fun messageVoiceData(data: ByteArray, messageType: HumlaUDPMessageType) {
        output.queueVoiceData(data, messageType)
    }

    /** Capture thread. */
    override fun onAudioInputReceived(frame: ShortArray, frameSize: Int) {
        val processed = pipeline.process(frame, frameSize)
        val nowTalking = processed.transmit && !muted

        if (talking != nowTalking) {
            encodeListener.onTalkingStateChanged(nowTalking)
            if (halfDuplex) {
                audioManager.adjustStreamVolume(audioStream, if (nowTalking) AudioManager.ADJUST_MUTE else AudioManager.ADJUST_UNMUTE, 0)
            }
            if (!nowTalking) {
                synchronized(encoderLock) {
                    try { encoder?.terminate() } catch (e: NativeAudioException) { Log.e(TAG, "terminate failed", e) }
                }
            }
        }

        synchronized(encoderLock) {
            val enc = encoder
            if (enc != null) {
                if (nowTalking) {
                    try {
                        enc.encode(processed.samples, processed.length)
                        frameCounter++
                    } catch (e: NativeAudioException) {
                        Log.e(TAG, "encode failed", e)
                    }
                }
                if (enc.isReady) sendEncodedAudio(enc)
            }
        }

        talking = nowTalking
        if (!nowTalking) inputMode.waitForInput()
    }

    fun setVoiceTargetId(id: Byte) { targetId = id }
    fun clearVoiceTarget() { targetId = 0 }

    private fun sendEncodedAudio(enc: IEncoder) {
        val frames = enc.bufferedFrames
        var flags = (codec?.ordinal ?: 0) shl 5
        flags = flags or (targetId.toInt() and 0x1F)
        val packetBuffer = ByteArray(1024)
        packetBuffer[0] = (flags and 0xFF).toByte()
        val ds = PacketBuffer(packetBuffer, 1024)
        ds.skip(1)
        ds.writeLong((frameCounter - frames).toLong())
        enc.getEncodedData(ds)
        val length = ds.size()
        ds.rewind()
        encodeListener.onAudioEncoded(ds.dataBlock(length), length)
    }

    interface AudioEncodeListener {
        fun onAudioEncoded(data: ByteArray, length: Int)
        fun onTalkingStateChanged(talking: Boolean)
    }

    /** Configures and creates a handler. Stream A owns the only production caller (HumlaService). */
    class Builder {
        private var context: Context? = null
        private var logger: HumlaLogger? = null
        private var audioStream = 0
        private var audioSource = 0
        private var targetBitrate = 0
        private var targetFramesPerPacket = 0
        private var inputSampleRate = 0
        private var amplitudeBoost = 1f
        private var bluetoothEnabled = false
        private var halfDuplexEnabled = false
        private var noiseSuppression = NoiseSuppressionMode.SPEEX
        private var echoCancellation = EchoCancellationMode.NONE
        private var audioEffects = AndroidAudioEffects()
        private var vadConfig: VadConfig? = null
        private var inputMode: IInputMode? = null
        private var encodeListener: AudioEncodeListener? = null
        private var talkingListener: AudioOutput.AudioOutputListener? = null
        private var captureSourceFactory: PcmCaptureSourceFactory = AndroidAudioRecordSource.Factory()
        private var playbackSinkFactory: PcmPlaybackSinkFactory = AndroidAudioTrackSink.Factory()
        private var preprocessorFactory: CapturePreprocessorFactory? = null
        private var resamplerFactory: (Int, Int) -> Resampler = { inRate, outRate -> SpeexResampler(inRate, outRate) }
        private var encoderFactory: EncoderFactory = EncoderFactory.DEFAULT
        private var scope: CoroutineScope? = null
        private var captureJoinTimeoutMs = AudioInput.DEFAULT_JOIN_TIMEOUT_MS
        private var playbackJoinTimeoutMs = AudioOutput.DEFAULT_JOIN_TIMEOUT_MS

        fun setContext(context: Context) = apply { this.context = context }
        fun setLogger(logger: HumlaLogger) = apply { this.logger = logger }
        fun setAudioStream(audioStream: Int) = apply { this.audioStream = audioStream }
        fun setAudioSource(audioSource: Int) = apply { this.audioSource = audioSource }
        fun setTargetBitrate(targetBitrate: Int) = apply { this.targetBitrate = targetBitrate }
        fun setTargetFramesPerPacket(targetFramesPerPacket: Int) = apply { this.targetFramesPerPacket = targetFramesPerPacket }
        fun setInputSampleRate(inputSampleRate: Int) = apply { this.inputSampleRate = inputSampleRate }
        fun setAmplitudeBoost(amplitudeBoost: Float) = apply { this.amplitudeBoost = amplitudeBoost }
        fun setBluetoothEnabled(bluetoothEnabled: Boolean) = apply { this.bluetoothEnabled = bluetoothEnabled }
        fun setHalfDuplexEnabled(halfDuplexEnabled: Boolean) = apply { this.halfDuplexEnabled = halfDuplexEnabled }
        fun setEncodeListener(encodeListener: AudioEncodeListener) = apply { this.encodeListener = encodeListener }
        fun setTalkingListener(talkingListener: AudioOutput.AudioOutputListener) = apply { this.talkingListener = talkingListener }
        fun setInputMode(inputMode: IInputMode) = apply { this.inputMode = inputMode }

        // Spec §4 additions
        fun setNoiseSuppression(mode: NoiseSuppressionMode) = apply { noiseSuppression = mode }
        fun setEchoCancellation(mode: EchoCancellationMode) = apply { echoCancellation = mode }
        fun setVadConfig(config: VadConfig) = apply { vadConfig = config }
        fun setAudioEffects(noiseSuppressor: Boolean, automaticGainControl: Boolean) = apply { audioEffects = AndroidAudioEffects(noiseSuppressor, automaticGainControl) }

        /** Legacy: true → Speex, false → none. Prefer [setNoiseSuppression]. */
        fun setPreprocessorEnabled(preprocessorEnabled: Boolean) = apply {
            noiseSuppression = if (preprocessorEnabled) NoiseSuppressionMode.SPEEX else NoiseSuppressionMode.NONE
        }

        /** Legacy: "none" / "system" / "webrtc". Prefer [setEchoCancellation]. */
        fun setEchoCancellationMethod(echoCancellationMethod: String) = apply {
            echoCancellation = EchoCancellationMode.fromPreferenceValue(echoCancellationMethod)
        }

        // Test seams (same module only)
        internal fun setCaptureSourceFactory(factory: PcmCaptureSourceFactory) = apply { captureSourceFactory = factory }
        internal fun setPlaybackSinkFactory(factory: PcmPlaybackSinkFactory) = apply { playbackSinkFactory = factory }
        internal fun setPreprocessorFactory(factory: CapturePreprocessorFactory) = apply { preprocessorFactory = factory }
        internal fun setResamplerFactory(factory: (Int, Int) -> Resampler) = apply { resamplerFactory = factory }
        internal fun setEncoderFactory(factory: EncoderFactory) = apply { encoderFactory = factory }
        internal fun setCoroutineScope(scope: CoroutineScope) = apply { this.scope = scope }
        internal fun setJoinTimeouts(captureMs: Long, playbackMs: Long) = apply { captureJoinTimeoutMs = captureMs; playbackJoinTimeoutMs = playbackMs }

        @Throws(AudioException::class)
        fun initialize(self: User, maxBandwidth: Int, codec: HumlaUDPMessageType?, targetId: Byte): AudioHandler {
            val log = requireNotNull(logger) { "logger" }
            val handler = AudioHandler(
                requireNotNull(context) { "context" }, log, audioStream, audioSource, inputSampleRate, targetBitrate,
                targetFramesPerPacket, requireNotNull(inputMode) { "inputMode" }, targetId, amplitudeBoost, bluetoothEnabled,
                halfDuplexEnabled, noiseSuppression, echoCancellation, audioEffects, vadConfig,
                requireNotNull(encodeListener) { "encodeListener" }, requireNotNull(talkingListener) { "talkingListener" },
                captureSourceFactory, playbackSinkFactory,
                preprocessorFactory ?: CapturePreprocessorFactory(log = { log.logWarning(it) }),
                resamplerFactory, encoderFactory,
                scope ?: CoroutineScope(SupervisorJob() + Dispatchers.Default),
                captureJoinTimeoutMs, playbackJoinTimeoutMs,
            )
            handler.initialize(self, maxBandwidth, codec)
            return handler
        }
    }

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "AudioHandler"
        const val SAMPLE_RATE = 48000
        const val FRAME_SIZE = SAMPLE_RATE / 100
        const val MAX_BUFFER_SIZE = 960
        const val SILENCE_RETRY_MS = 2000L
    }
}
```

`app/src/test/java/se/lublin/mumla/SettingsAudioTest.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.mumla

import android.content.Context
import androidx.preference.PreferenceManager
import androidx.test.core.app.ApplicationProvider
import com.google.common.truth.Truth.assertThat
import org.junit.Test
import org.junit.runner.RunWith
import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.EchoCancellationMode
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.NoiseSuppressionMode
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.VadConfig
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.VadMode

@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
class SettingsAudioTest {
    private val context = ApplicationProvider.getApplicationContext<Context>()
    private val prefs = PreferenceManager.getDefaultSharedPreferences(context)
    private val settings get() = Settings.getInstance(context)

    @Test
    fun `noise suppression defaults to speex`() {
        assertThat(settings.getNoiseSuppressionMode()).isEqualTo(NoiseSuppressionMode.SPEEX)
    }

    @Test
    fun `legacy disabled preprocessor migrates to none until the new key is set`() {
        prefs.edit().putBoolean(Settings.PREF_PREPROCESSOR_ENABLED, false).commit()
        assertThat(settings.getNoiseSuppressionMode()).isEqualTo(NoiseSuppressionMode.NONE)
        prefs.edit().putString(Settings.PREF_NOISE_SUPPRESSION, "rnnoise").commit()
        assertThat(settings.getNoiseSuppressionMode()).isEqualTo(NoiseSuppressionMode.RNNOISE)
    }

    @Test
    fun `echo cancellation mode parses the stored method`() {
        prefs.edit().putString(Settings.PREF_ECHO_CANCELLATION_METHOD, "system").commit()
        assertThat(settings.getEchoCancellationMode()).isEqualTo(EchoCancellationMode.ANDROID)
        prefs.edit().putString(Settings.PREF_ECHO_CANCELLATION_METHOD, "webrtc").commit()
        assertThat(settings.getEchoCancellationMode()).isEqualTo(EchoCancellationMode.WEBRTC)
    }

    @Test
    fun `vad config defaults to probability 0_6 0_3 250`() {
        assertThat(settings.getVadMode()).isEqualTo(VadMode.PROBABILITY)
        assertThat(settings.getVadConfig()).isEqualTo(VadConfig.DEFAULT)
    }

    @Test
    fun `amplitude mode is driven by the legacy threshold slider`() {
        prefs.edit().putString(Settings.PREF_VAD_MODE, "amplitude").putInt(Settings.PREF_THRESHOLD, 70).putInt(Settings.PREF_VAD_HOLD_MS, 400).commit()
        assertThat(settings.getVadConfig()).isEqualTo(VadConfig.amplitude(0.7f, 400))
    }

    @Test
    fun `probability sliders are read in percent and stop never exceeds start`() {
        prefs.edit().putInt(Settings.PREF_VAD_START, 40).putInt(Settings.PREF_VAD_STOP, 55).putInt(Settings.PREF_VAD_HOLD_MS, 100).commit()
        assertThat(settings.getVadConfig()).isEqualTo(VadConfig(VadMode.PROBABILITY, 0.4f, 0.4f, 100))
    }

    @Test
    fun `android effects default off and read their toggles`() {
        assertThat(settings.isAndroidNoiseSuppressorEnabled()).isFalse()
        assertThat(settings.isAndroidAgcEnabled()).isFalse()
        prefs.edit().putBoolean(Settings.PREF_ANDROID_NOISE_SUPPRESSOR, true).putBoolean(Settings.PREF_ANDROID_AGC, true).commit()
        assertThat(settings.isAndroidNoiseSuppressorEnabled()).isTrue()
        assertThat(settings.isAndroidAgcEnabled()).isTrue()
    }
}
```

`app/src/test/java/se/lublin/mumla/preference/AudioSettingsPolicyTest.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.mumla.preference

import com.google.common.truth.Truth.assertThat
import org.junit.Test
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.VadMode

class AudioSettingsPolicyTest {
    @Test
    fun `amplitude mode enables the level slider and disables the probability sliders`() {
        assertThat(AudioSettingsPolicy.vadDependents(VadMode.AMPLITUDE)).isEqualTo(VadDependents(amplitudeControlsEnabled = true, probabilityControlsEnabled = false))
        assertThat(AudioSettingsPolicy.vadDependents(VadMode.PROBABILITY)).isEqualTo(VadDependents(amplitudeControlsEnabled = false, probabilityControlsEnabled = true))
    }

    @Test
    fun `system echo cancellation is offered only when the device has an AEC`() {
        assertThat(AudioSettingsPolicy.echoCancellationValues(androidAecAvailable = true)).containsExactly("none", "system", "webrtc").inOrder()
        assertThat(AudioSettingsPolicy.echoCancellationValues(androidAecAvailable = false)).containsExactly("none", "webrtc").inOrder()
    }

    @Test
    fun `a stored value that is no longer offered falls back to none`() {
        assertThat(AudioSettingsPolicy.fallbackEchoValue("system", listOf("none", "webrtc"))).isEqualTo("none")
        assertThat(AudioSettingsPolicy.fallbackEchoValue("webrtc", listOf("none", "webrtc"))).isEqualTo("webrtc")
        assertThat(AudioSettingsPolicy.fallbackEchoValue(null, listOf("none", "webrtc"))).isEqualTo("none")
    }
}
```

Inside the `companion object` of `app/src/main/java/se/lublin/mumla/Settings.kt` (next to `PREF_ECHO_CANCELLATION_METHOD`):
```kotlin
        // Stream B audio keys (spec B3–B6). Defaults are mirrored by settings_audio.xml.
        const val PREF_NOISE_SUPPRESSION = "noise_suppression"
        const val DEFAULT_NOISE_SUPPRESSION = "speex"
        const val ECHO_CANCELLATION_WEBRTC = "webrtc"
        const val PREF_VAD_MODE = "vad_mode"
        const val DEFAULT_VAD_MODE = "probability"
        const val PREF_VAD_START = "vad_start"
        const val DEFAULT_VAD_START = 60
        const val PREF_VAD_STOP = "vad_stop"
        const val DEFAULT_VAD_STOP = 30
        const val PREF_VAD_HOLD_MS = "vad_hold_ms"
        const val DEFAULT_VAD_HOLD_MS = 250
        const val PREF_ANDROID_NOISE_SUPPRESSOR = "android_noise_suppressor"
        const val DEFAULT_ANDROID_NOISE_SUPPRESSOR = false
        const val PREF_ANDROID_AGC = "android_agc"
        const val DEFAULT_ANDROID_AGC = false
```
In the class body (the `SharedPreferences` field is `preferences`, as in today's `Settings.java:168`; add the imports `se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.EchoCancellationMode`, `NoiseSuppressionMode`, `VadConfig`, `VadMode`):
```kotlin
    /** Migration: users who had unticked the old "Input pre-processing" box keep "none" until they pick a value. */
    fun getNoiseSuppressionMode(): NoiseSuppressionMode {
        val stored = preferences.getString(PREF_NOISE_SUPPRESSION, null)
        if (stored != null) return NoiseSuppressionMode.fromPreferenceValue(stored)
        val legacyEnabled = preferences.getBoolean(PREF_PREPROCESSOR_ENABLED, DEFAULT_PREPROCESSOR_ENABLED)
        return if (legacyEnabled) NoiseSuppressionMode.SPEEX else NoiseSuppressionMode.NONE
    }

    fun getEchoCancellationMode(): EchoCancellationMode = EchoCancellationMode.fromPreferenceValue(getEchoCancellationMethod())

    fun getVadMode(): VadMode = VadMode.fromPreferenceValue(preferences.getString(PREF_VAD_MODE, DEFAULT_VAD_MODE))

    fun getVadConfig(): VadConfig {
        val holdMs = preferences.getInt(PREF_VAD_HOLD_MS, DEFAULT_VAD_HOLD_MS).coerceAtLeast(0).toLong()
        return when (getVadMode()) {
            VadMode.AMPLITUDE -> VadConfig.amplitude(getDetectionThreshold(), holdMs)
            VadMode.PROBABILITY -> {
                val start = preferences.getInt(PREF_VAD_START, DEFAULT_VAD_START).coerceIn(0, 100) / 100f
                val stop = (preferences.getInt(PREF_VAD_STOP, DEFAULT_VAD_STOP).coerceIn(0, 100) / 100f).coerceAtMost(start)
                VadConfig.probability(start, stop, holdMs)
            }
        }
    }

    fun isAndroidNoiseSuppressorEnabled(): Boolean = preferences.getBoolean(PREF_ANDROID_NOISE_SUPPRESSOR, DEFAULT_ANDROID_NOISE_SUPPRESSOR)

    fun isAndroidAgcEnabled(): Boolean = preferences.getBoolean(PREF_ANDROID_AGC, DEFAULT_ANDROID_AGC)
```

`app/src/main/java/se/lublin/mumla/preference/AudioSettingsPolicy.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.mumla.preference

import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.VadMode

data class VadDependents(val amplitudeControlsEnabled: Boolean, val probabilityControlsEnabled: Boolean)

/** Pure decisions behind AudioSettingsFragment, so they are testable without a Fragment. */
object AudioSettingsPolicy {
    const val ECHO_NONE = "none"
    const val ECHO_ANDROID = "system"
    const val ECHO_WEBRTC = "webrtc"

    fun vadDependents(mode: VadMode): VadDependents = when (mode) {
        VadMode.AMPLITUDE -> VadDependents(amplitudeControlsEnabled = true, probabilityControlsEnabled = false)
        VadMode.PROBABILITY -> VadDependents(amplitudeControlsEnabled = false, probabilityControlsEnabled = true)
    }

    fun echoCancellationValues(androidAecAvailable: Boolean): List<String> =
        if (androidAecAvailable) listOf(ECHO_NONE, ECHO_ANDROID, ECHO_WEBRTC) else listOf(ECHO_NONE, ECHO_WEBRTC)

    fun fallbackEchoValue(current: String?, available: List<String>): String =
        if (current != null && current in available) current else ECHO_NONE
}
```

Delete the `.java`; create `app/src/main/java/se/lublin/mumla/preference/AudioSettingsFragment.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.mumla.preference

import android.media.AudioFormat
import android.media.AudioRecord
import android.media.audiofx.AcousticEchoCanceler
import android.os.Bundle
import androidx.preference.ListPreference
import androidx.preference.PreferenceCategory
import androidx.preference.PreferenceScreen
import se.lublin.mumla.R
import se.lublin.mumla.Settings

class AudioSettingsFragment : MumlaPreferenceFragment() {
    override fun onCreatePreferences(savedInstanceState: Bundle?, rootKey: String?) {
        setPreferencesFromResource(R.xml.settings_audio, rootKey)

        val inputPreference = requireNotNull(preferenceScreen.findPreference<ListPreference>(Settings.PREF_INPUT_METHOD))
        inputPreference.setOnPreferenceChangeListener { _, newValue ->
            updateAudioDependents(preferenceScreen, newValue as String)
            true
        }

        // Scan each sample rate and determine if the device supports it
        val inputQualityPreference = requireNotNull(preferenceScreen.findPreference<ListPreference>(Settings.PREF_INPUT_RATE))
        inputQualityPreference.entries = inputQualityPreference.entryValues.map { value ->
            val rate = value.toString().toInt()
            val supported = AudioRecord.getMinBufferSize(rate, AudioFormat.CHANNEL_IN_MONO, AudioFormat.ENCODING_PCM_16BIT) > 0
            "${rate}Hz" + if (supported) "" else " (unsupported)"
        }.toTypedArray()

        findPreference<ListPreference>(Settings.PREF_ECHO_CANCELLATION_METHOD)?.let { echoPref ->
            if (!AcousticEchoCanceler.isAvailable()) removeListEntry(echoPref, "system")
            val current = echoPref.value
            if (current == null || current !in echoPref.entryValues.map { it.toString() }) {
                echoPref.value = Settings.DEFAULT_ECHO_CANCELLATION_METHOD
            }
        }

        updateAudioDependents(preferenceScreen, inputPreference.value)
    }

    private fun removeListEntry(pref: ListPreference, valueToRemove: String) {
        val keep = pref.entryValues.indices.filter { pref.entryValues[it].toString() != valueToRemove }
        pref.entries = keep.map { pref.entries[it] }.toTypedArray()
        pref.entryValues = keep.map { pref.entryValues[it] }.toTypedArray()
    }

    private fun updateAudioDependents(screen: PreferenceScreen, inputMethod: String) {
        requireNotNull(screen.findPreference<PreferenceCategory>("ptt_settings")).isEnabled = Settings.ARRAY_INPUT_METHOD_PTT == inputMethod
        requireNotNull(screen.findPreference<PreferenceCategory>("vad_settings")).isEnabled = Settings.ARRAY_INPUT_METHOD_VOICE == inputMethod
    }
}
```

```kotlin
    private fun markAvailability(pref: CheckBoxPreference, available: Boolean) {
        if (!available) {
            pref.isEnabled = false
            pref.isChecked = false
            pref.summary = getString(R.string.audioEffectUnavailable)
        }
    }

    private fun applyVadDependents(mode: VadMode) {
        val d = AudioSettingsPolicy.vadDependents(mode)
        findPreference<Preference>(Settings.PREF_THRESHOLD)?.isEnabled = d.amplitudeControlsEnabled
        findPreference<Preference>(Settings.PREF_VAD_START)?.isEnabled = d.probabilityControlsEnabled
        findPreference<Preference>(Settings.PREF_VAD_STOP)?.isEnabled = d.probabilityControlsEnabled
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the app-side fakes and the failing session tests**

`app/src/test/java/se/lublin/mumla/audio/TestAudioFakes.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.mumla.audio

import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.CaptureRequest
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.PcmCaptureSource
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.PcmCaptureSourceFactory
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.PcmPlaybackSink
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.PcmPlaybackSinkFactory
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.SpeexPreprocessApi
import java.util.concurrent.CopyOnWriteArrayList
import java.util.concurrent.LinkedBlockingQueue
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit

class TestCaptureSource(frames: List<ShortArray>, override val sampleRate: Int = 48000) : PcmCaptureSource {
    override val audioSessionId = 1
    val events = CopyOnWriteArrayList<String>()
    private val queue = LinkedBlockingQueue(frames)
    @Volatile private var stopped = false
    override fun start() { events += "start" }
    override fun read(buffer: ShortArray, length: Int): Int {
        while (!stopped) {
            val f = queue.poll(10, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS) ?: continue
            System.arraycopy(f, 0, buffer, 0, minOf(f.size, length)); return minOf(f.size, length)
        }
        return 0
    }
    override fun stop() { events += "stop"; stopped = true }
    override fun release() { events += "release" }
    override fun setSilenceListener(listener: ((Boolean) -> Unit)?) = Unit
    class Factory(private val source: TestCaptureSource) : PcmCaptureSourceFactory {
        var request: CaptureRequest? = null
        override fun open(request: CaptureRequest): PcmCaptureSource { this.request = request; return source }
    }
}

class TestPlaybackSink : PcmPlaybackSink {
    override val minBufferSizeSamples = 960
    val events = CopyOnWriteArrayList<String>()
    val written = CopyOnWriteArrayList<ShortArray>()
    override fun play() { events += "play" }
    override fun write(buffer: ShortArray, length: Int): Int { written += buffer.copyOf(length); return length }
    override fun pause() { events += "pause" }
    override fun flush() { events += "flush" }
    override fun stop() { events += "stop" }
    override fun release() { events += "release" }
    class Factory(private val sink: TestPlaybackSink) : PcmPlaybackSinkFactory {
        var openedWith: Pair<Int, Int>? = null
        override fun open(audioStream: Int, sampleRate: Int): PcmPlaybackSink { openedWith = audioStream to sampleRate; return sink }
    }
}

/** Halves every sample and reports a fixed probability. */
class HalvingSpeexApi(private val probabilityPercent: Int) : SpeexPreprocessApi {
    override fun create(frameSize: Int, sampleRate: Int): Long = 1L
    override fun run(state: Long, frame: ShortArray): Boolean { for (i in frame.indices) frame[i] = (frame[i] / 2).toShort(); return probabilityPercent >= 50 }
    override fun setInt(state: Long, request: Int, value: Int) = Unit
    override fun getInt(state: Long, request: Int): Int = if (request == 45) probabilityPercent else 0
    override fun destroy(state: Long) = Unit
}
```

`app/src/test/java/se/lublin/mumla/audio/AudioTestSessionTest.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.mumla.audio

import com.google.common.truth.Truth.assertThat
import org.junit.Test
import org.junit.runner.RunWith
import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.AndroidAudioEffects
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.CapturePreprocessorFactory
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.EchoCancellationMode
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.NoiseSuppressionMode
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.VadConfig
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.WebRtcApmApi
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.RnnoiseApi
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.WebRtcApmConfig
import java.util.concurrent.CopyOnWriteArrayList

@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
class AudioTestSessionTest {
    private val readings = CopyOnWriteArrayList<MeterReading>()
    private val noRnnoise = object : RnnoiseApi { override fun create() = 0L; override fun processFrame(handle: Long, frame: ShortArray) = 0f; override fun destroy(handle: Long) = Unit }
    private val noApm = object : WebRtcApmApi {
        override fun create(sampleRate: Int, config: WebRtcApmConfig) = 0L
        override fun processCapture(handle: Long, frame: ShortArray) = -1
        override fun processRender(handle: Long, frame: ShortArray) = -1
        override fun lastCaptureLevelDbfs(handle: Long) = -100f
        override fun destroy(handle: Long) = Unit
    }
    private fun preprocessors(speexProbability: Int) = CapturePreprocessorFactory({ HalvingSpeexApi(speexProbability) }, { noRnnoise }, { noApm })
    private fun constant(v: Int) = ShortArray(480) { v.toShort() }

    private fun await(timeoutMs: Long = 2000, condition: () -> Boolean) {
        val deadline = System.currentTimeMillis() + timeoutMs
        while (!condition() && System.currentTimeMillis() < deadline) Thread.sleep(5)
        assertThat(condition()).isTrue()
    }

    private fun session(source: TestCaptureSource, sink: TestPlaybackSink, vad: VadConfig, noise: NoiseSuppressionMode, loopback: Boolean, speexProbability: Int = 90) =
        AudioTestSession(vad, noise, EchoCancellationMode.NONE, AndroidAudioEffects(), loopback, { readings += it }, readingIntervalFrames = 1,
            captureFactory = TestCaptureSource.Factory(source), sinkFactory = TestPlaybackSink.Factory(sink),
            preprocessorFactory = preprocessors(speexProbability), resamplerFactory = { _, _ -> error("no resampler expected at 48 kHz") })

    @Test
    fun `amplitude mode reports the level score and the vad decision`() {
        val source = TestCaptureSource(List(3) { constant(3277) })
        val s = session(source, TestPlaybackSink(), VadConfig.amplitude(0.7f), NoiseSuppressionMode.NONE, loopback = false)
        s.start()
        await { readings.size >= 3 }
        s.stop()
        assertThat(readings[0].score).isWithin(0.002f).of(0.792f)
        assertThat(readings[0].voice).isTrue()
    }

    @Test
    fun `probability mode reports the preprocessor probability`() {
        val source = TestCaptureSource(List(2) { constant(0) })
        val s = session(source, TestPlaybackSink(), VadConfig.probability(), NoiseSuppressionMode.SPEEX, loopback = false, speexProbability = 90)
        s.start()
        await { readings.size >= 2 }
        s.stop()
        assertThat(readings[0].score).isEqualTo(0.9f)
        assertThat(readings[0].voice).isTrue()
    }

    @Test
    fun `loopback plays the processed frame while voice is detected and silence otherwise`() {
        val sink = TestPlaybackSink()
        val source = TestCaptureSource(listOf(constant(20000), constant(0)))
        val s = session(source, sink, VadConfig.amplitude(0.5f, holdTimeMs = 0), NoiseSuppressionMode.SPEEX, loopback = true)
        s.start()
        await { sink.written.size >= 2 }
        s.stop()
        assertThat(sink.written[0][0]).isEqualTo(10000.toShort())   // halved by the "denoiser"
        assertThat(sink.written[1][0]).isEqualTo(0.toShort())
        assertThat(sink.events.first()).isEqualTo("play")
    }

    @Test
    fun `without loopback no playback sink is opened`() {
        val sink = TestPlaybackSink()
        val factory = TestPlaybackSink.Factory(sink)
        val s = AudioTestSession(VadConfig.DEFAULT, NoiseSuppressionMode.NONE, EchoCancellationMode.NONE, AndroidAudioEffects(), false, { readings += it }, 1,
            TestCaptureSource.Factory(TestCaptureSource(emptyList())), factory, preprocessors(0)) { _, _ -> error("unexpected") }
        s.start(); s.stop()
        assertThat(factory.openedWith).isNull()
    }

    @Test
    fun `stop releases the microphone and the sink`() {
        val source = TestCaptureSource(emptyList())
        val sink = TestPlaybackSink()
        val s = session(source, sink, VadConfig.DEFAULT, NoiseSuppressionMode.NONE, loopback = true)
        s.start()
        await { "start" in source.events }
        s.stop()
        assertThat(source.events.last()).isEqualTo("release")
        assertThat(sink.events.last()).isEqualTo("release")
    }
}
```

`app/src/main/java/se/lublin/mumla/audio/AudioTestSession.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.mumla.audio

import android.media.AudioManager
import android.media.MediaRecorder
import android.util.Log
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.AndroidAudioEffects
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.AndroidAudioRecordSource
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.AndroidAudioTrackSink
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.CapturePipeline
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.CapturePreprocessorFactory
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.CaptureRequest
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.EchoCancellationMode
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.NoiseSuppressionMode
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.PcmCaptureSource
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.PcmCaptureSourceFactory
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.PcmPlaybackSink
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.PcmPlaybackSinkFactory
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.Resampler
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.SpeexResampler
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.VadConfig
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.VadMode
import se.lublin.humla.audio.capture.VoiceActivityDetector
import se.lublin.humla.audio.inputmode.ActivityInputMode
import se.lublin.humla.exception.AudioInitializationException
import se.lublin.humla.protocol.AudioHandler

data class MeterReading(val score: Float, val voice: Boolean)

/**
 * Spec B10: a short-lived capture session for the settings screen. Runs the same pipeline the
 * service uses, reports the VAD score/decision per frame, and optionally plays the frames that
 * would be transmitted back to the user (loopback). One bare thread, released by [stop].
 */
class AudioTestSession(
    private val vadConfig: VadConfig,
    private val noiseSuppression: NoiseSuppressionMode,
    private val echoCancellation: EchoCancellationMode,
    private val effects: AndroidAudioEffects,
    private val loopback: Boolean,
    private val onReading: (MeterReading) -> Unit,
    private val readingIntervalFrames: Int = 5,
    private val captureFactory: PcmCaptureSourceFactory = AndroidAudioRecordSource.Factory(),
    private val sinkFactory: PcmPlaybackSinkFactory = AndroidAudioTrackSink.Factory(),
    private val preprocessorFactory: CapturePreprocessorFactory = CapturePreprocessorFactory(log = { Log.w(TAG, it) }),
    private val resamplerFactory: (Int, Int) -> Resampler = { inRate, outRate -> SpeexResampler(inRate, outRate) },
) {
    private var source: PcmCaptureSource? = null
    private var sink: PcmPlaybackSink? = null
    private var pipeline: CapturePipeline? = null
    private var thread: Thread? = null

    @Volatile
    private var running = false

    @Throws(AudioInitializationException::class)
    fun start() {
        check(thread == null) { "already started" }
        val src = captureFactory.open(CaptureRequest(MediaRecorder.AudioSource.MIC, AudioHandler.SAMPLE_RATE, effects, echoCancellation))
        val chain = preprocessorFactory.create(noiseSuppression, echoCancellation)
        val resampler = if (src.sampleRate != AudioHandler.SAMPLE_RATE) resamplerFactory(src.sampleRate, AudioHandler.SAMPLE_RATE) else null
        val pipe = CapturePipeline(resampler, chain.preprocessor, ActivityInputMode(VoiceActivityDetector(vadConfig)))
        val snk = if (loopback) sinkFactory.open(AudioManager.STREAM_MUSIC, AudioHandler.SAMPLE_RATE) else null
        source = src
        sink = snk
        pipeline = pipe
        running = true
        thread = Thread({ loop(src, snk, pipe) }, "mumla-audio-test").also { it.start() }
    }

    private fun loop(src: PcmCaptureSource, snk: PcmPlaybackSink?, pipe: CapturePipeline) {
        src.start()
        snk?.play()
        val frameSize = src.sampleRate / 100
        val buffer = ShortArray(frameSize)
        val silence = ShortArray(AudioHandler.FRAME_SIZE)
        var count = 0
        while (running) {
            val n = src.read(buffer, frameSize)
            if (n <= 0) {
                if (n < 0 && running) break
                continue
            }
            val frame = pipe.process(buffer, frameSize)
            val score = when (vadConfig.mode) {
                VadMode.AMPLITUDE -> VoiceActivityDetector.amplitudeScore(frame.samples, frame.length)
                VadMode.PROBABILITY -> frame.probability ?: VoiceActivityDetector.amplitudeScore(frame.samples, frame.length)
            }
            if (++count % readingIntervalFrames == 0) onReading(MeterReading(score, frame.transmit))
            snk?.write(if (frame.transmit) frame.samples else silence, frame.length)
        }
        src.stop()
    }

    fun stop() {
        running = false
        source?.stop()
        thread?.join(2000)
        thread = null
        sink?.let { it.flush(); it.stop(); it.release() }
        sink = null
        source?.release()
        source = null
        pipeline?.release()
        pipeline = null
    }

    private companion object {
        const val TAG = "AudioTestSession"
    }
}
```

`app/src/main/java/se/lublin/mumla/preference/LevelMeterView.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.mumla.preference

import android.content.Context
import android.graphics.Canvas
import android.graphics.Color
import android.graphics.Paint
import android.util.AttributeSet
import android.view.View

/** Horizontal level bar with start/stop threshold markers; fills green while voice is detected. */
class LevelMeterView @JvmOverloads constructor(context: Context, attrs: AttributeSet? = null) : View(context, attrs) {
    var level: Float = 0f
        set(value) { field = value.coerceIn(0f, 1f); invalidate() }
    var startThreshold: Float = 0.6f
        set(value) { field = value.coerceIn(0f, 1f); invalidate() }
    var stopThreshold: Float = 0.3f
        set(value) { field = value.coerceIn(0f, 1f); invalidate() }
    var voice: Boolean = false
        set(value) { field = value; invalidate() }

    private val trackPaint = Paint().apply { color = Color.argb(60, 128, 128, 128) }
    private val idlePaint = Paint().apply { color = Color.rgb(96, 125, 139) }
    private val voicePaint = Paint().apply { color = Color.rgb(76, 175, 80) }
    private val startPaint = Paint().apply { color = Color.rgb(76, 175, 80); strokeWidth = 4f }
    private val stopPaint = Paint().apply { color = Color.rgb(255, 152, 0); strokeWidth = 4f }

    override fun onDraw(canvas: Canvas) {
        super.onDraw(canvas)
        val w = width.toFloat()
        val h = height.toFloat()
        val radius = h / 2
        canvas.drawRoundRect(0f, 0f, w, h, radius, radius, trackPaint)
        canvas.drawRoundRect(0f, 0f, w * level, h, radius, radius, if (voice) voicePaint else idlePaint)
        canvas.drawLine(w * stopThreshold, 0f, w * stopThreshold, h, stopPaint)
        canvas.drawLine(w * startThreshold, 0f, w * startThreshold, h, startPaint)
    }
}
```

`app/src/main/java/se/lublin/mumla/preference/InputLevelMeterPreference.kt`:
```kotlin
package se.lublin.mumla.preference

import android.content.Context
import android.util.AttributeSet
import androidx.preference.Preference
import androidx.preference.PreferenceViewHolder
import se.lublin.mumla.R
import se.lublin.mumla.audio.MeterReading

/** Spec B10: shows the live input level and the start/stop thresholds while the screen is open. */
class InputLevelMeterPreference(context: Context, attrs: AttributeSet?) : Preference(context, attrs) {
    private var meter: LevelMeterView? = null
    private var reading = MeterReading(0f, false)
    private var start = 0.6f
    private var stop = 0.3f

    init {
        layoutResource = R.layout.preference_input_level_meter
        isSelectable = false
    }

    override fun onBindViewHolder(holder: PreferenceViewHolder) {
        super.onBindViewHolder(holder)
        meter = holder.findViewById(R.id.level_meter) as LevelMeterView
        apply()
    }

    fun setReading(reading: MeterReading) {
        this.reading = reading
        apply()
    }

    fun setThresholds(start: Float, stop: Float) {
        this.start = start
        this.stop = stop
        apply()
    }

    private fun apply() {
        meter?.let {
            it.level = reading.score
            it.voice = reading.voice
            it.startThreshold = start
            it.stopThreshold = stop
        }
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Wire the session into `AudioSettingsFragment`**

Add to `AudioSettingsFragment.kt`:
```kotlin
    private var session: AudioTestSession? = null
    private val mainHandler = Handler(Looper.getMainLooper())
    private val prefsListener = SharedPreferences.OnSharedPreferenceChangeListener { _, key ->
        if (key in RESTART_KEYS) restartSession()
    }

    override fun onResume() {
        super.onResume()
        preferenceManager.sharedPreferences?.registerOnSharedPreferenceChangeListener(prefsListener)
        findPreference<SwitchPreferenceCompat>(KEY_LOOPBACK)?.setOnPreferenceChangeListener { _, newValue ->
            restartSession(loopback = newValue as Boolean)
            true
        }
        restartSession()
    }

    override fun onPause() {
        preferenceManager.sharedPreferences?.unregisterOnSharedPreferenceChangeListener(prefsListener)
        stopSession()
        super.onPause()
    }

    private fun restartSession(loopback: Boolean = findPreference<SwitchPreferenceCompat>(KEY_LOOPBACK)?.isChecked ?: false) {
        stopSession()
        val meter = findPreference<InputLevelMeterPreference>(KEY_METER) ?: return
        val context = requireContext()
        if (ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(context, Manifest.permission.RECORD_AUDIO) != PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED) {
            meter.summary = getString(R.string.inputLevelMeterUnavailable)
            return
        }
        val settings = Settings.getInstance(context)
        val vad = settings.getVadConfig()
        meter.summary = getString(R.string.inputLevelMeterSum)
        meter.setThresholds(vad.startThreshold, vad.stopThreshold)
        val s = AudioTestSession(
            vad, settings.getNoiseSuppressionMode(), settings.getEchoCancellationMode(),
            AndroidAudioEffects(settings.isAndroidNoiseSuppressorEnabled(), settings.isAndroidAgcEnabled()),
            loopback, onReading = { reading -> mainHandler.post { meter.setReading(reading) } },
        )
        try {
            s.start()
            session = s
        } catch (e: AudioInitializationException) {
            meter.summary = getString(R.string.inputLevelMeterUnavailable)
        }
    }

    private fun stopSession() {
        session?.stop()
        session = null
        mainHandler.removeCallbacksAndMessages(null)
        findPreference<InputLevelMeterPreference>(KEY_METER)?.setReading(MeterReading(0f, false))
    }

    private companion object {
        const val KEY_METER = "input_level_meter"
        const val KEY_LOOPBACK = "audio_loopback_test"
        val RESTART_KEYS = setOf(
            Settings.PREF_VAD_MODE, Settings.PREF_THRESHOLD, Settings.PREF_VAD_START, Settings.PREF_VAD_STOP, Settings.PREF_VAD_HOLD_MS,
            Settings.PREF_NOISE_SUPPRESSION, Settings.PREF_ECHO_CANCELLATION_METHOD, Settings.PREF_ANDROID_NOISE_SUPPRESSOR, Settings.PREF_ANDROID_AGC,
        )
    }
```
