## 7. Cross-stream touches

Files this stream does not own but must touch; each hook is kept to the lines shown.

| File (owner) | Where | Lines | Why |
|---|---|---|---|
| `.gitmodules` (F) | append three `[submodule]` blocks | ~9 | Tasks 1–2 add `third_party/rnnoise`, `third_party/webrtc-audio-processing`, `third_party/abseil-cpp` as submodules of the main repo (spec §2 "third-party native sources … are git submodules of the main repo"). |
| `NOTICE.md` (F) | append | 3 | Spec §2 licensing: RNNoise (BSD-3), webrtc-audio-processing (BSD-3), abseil-cpp (Apache-2.0). |
| `libraries/humla/src/main/cpp/CMakeLists.txt` (F "initial", B afterwards) | append | 8 | Task 3: `add_subdirectory(rnnoise)`, `add_subdirectory(webrtc_apm)`, the two JNI shared libraries. |
| `libraries/humla/src/main/java/se/lublin/humla/audio/native/SpeexPreprocessNative.kt` + `libraries/humla/src/main/cpp/jni_speex.cpp` (created by F6, B-owned after) | add `ctlSetInt`/`ctlGetInt` only if F6 did not provide int-typed `speex_preprocess_ctl` entry points | ≤ 12 | Task 5 adapter. |
| `libraries/humla/src/main/java/se/lublin/humla/HumlaService.java` (A) | `configureExtras` | ~10 | Stream A maps `EXTRAS_NOISE_SUPPRESSION`, `EXTRAS_ECHO_CANCELLATION`, `EXTRAS_VAD_MODE`/`_START`/`_STOP`/`_HOLD_MS`, `EXTRAS_ANDROID_NS`, `EXTRAS_ANDROID_AGC` (keys defined by A, spec §4) onto `mAudioBuilder.setNoiseSuppression(NoiseSuppressionMode.fromPreferenceValue(s))`, `.setEchoCancellation(EchoCancellationMode.fromPreferenceValue(s))`, `.setVadConfig(new VadConfig(VadMode.fromPreferenceValue(mode), start, stop, holdMs))` (also `mActivityInputMode.setVadConfig(...)` for a live change), `.setAudioEffects(ns, agc)`, and collects `mAudioHandler.getCaptureState()` to forward `Silenced`/`Error` to `onLogWarning` (A8). Until A lands, the legacy `setPreprocessorEnabled`/`setEchoCancellationMethod` shims keep the existing extras working. |
| `app/src/main/java/se/lublin/mumla/service/MumlaService.java` (A) | `onSharedPreferenceChanged` | ~10 | Add `case Settings.PREF_NOISE_SUPPRESSION / PREF_VAD_MODE / PREF_VAD_START / PREF_VAD_STOP / PREF_VAD_HOLD_MS / PREF_ANDROID_NOISE_SUPPRESSOR / PREF_ANDROID_AGC` that put the corresponding `EXTRAS_*` values (`mSettings.getNoiseSuppressionMode().getPreferenceValue()`, `getEchoCancellationMode().getPreferenceValue()`, `getVadConfig()` fields, `isAndroidNoiseSuppressorEnabled()`, `isAndroidAgcEnabled()`) into `changedExtras`. |
| `app/src/main/java/se/lublin/mumla/app/ServerConnectTask.java` (unlisted in the ownership table; treated like A's service) | connect-intent extras (today lines 68, 85–86) | ~8 | Put the same `EXTRAS_*` values from `Settings` into the connect intent so a fresh connection starts with the user's noise/echo/VAD/effects choices. |

No other file outside the ownership table is modified. Translation files are never edited.

## 8. Self-review against the spec

- **B1 pipeline order** — Task 8 (`CapturePipeline`: resample → preprocess every frame → VAD → boost → encoder in Task 11). Test "preprocessing runs on every frame even while not transmitting".
- **B2 `CapturePreprocessor` + implementations + composition rule** — Tasks 4, 5, 6 (`CapturePreprocessorFactory`, test "webrtc echo runs before rnnoise and the probability comes from rnnoise").
- **B3 WebRTC APM vendored (v2.1, submodule path as specified), build decision documented (CMake port, §0.3), far-end from `AudioOutput` on every played frame, settings None/Android/WebRTC** — Tasks 2, 3, 6, 10, 12.
- **B4 RNNoise v0.2 submodule, reproducible pinned weights, settings None/Light/Strong** — Tasks 1, 3, 6, 12.
- **B5 VAD modes, hysteresis, hold, defaults 0.6/0.3/250, amplitude stop = start − 0.15** — Task 7; Settings mapping Task 12.
- **B6 NoiseSuppressor/AGC on the session, VOICE_COMMUNICATION + MODE_IN_COMMUNICATION whenever any effect or WebRTC AEC is active** — Task 9 (`AndroidAudioRecordSource.Factory`, `AudioSourcePolicy`), Task 11 (`AudioHandler` sets the mode; test "android effects switch to voice communication source and communication mode").
- **B7 `AudioRecordingCallback`, `isClientSilenced` → `CaptureState.Silenced`, retry after 2 s** — Tasks 9 and 11 (test "silenced capture is exposed and capture restarts after two seconds").
- **B8 try/finally around the packet lock, 100 ms predicate wait, volatile flags, `AudioRecord.stop()` before `join(2000)`, timeout logs and releases** — Tasks 9 and 10.
- **B9 no AGC, `SET_PROB_START`, `noiseSuppressDb` −15/−25/−35, summary text** — Task 5 (tests) and Task 12 (new summary strings, old checkbox removed).
- **B10 live meter with thresholds, loopback test, short-lived AudioRecord released on pause** — Task 13.
- **B11 SCO rate capture + resample; RNNoise/Speex after resampling** — Tasks 8, 9, 11 (test "bluetooth capture runs at the sco rate and is resampled to 48 kHz").
- **§4 Builder additions, `captureState: StateFlow<CaptureState>`, `shutdown()` any thread ≤ 3 s** — Task 11 (join budgets 2 s + 1 s; test "shutdown returns within the configured join budget").
- **§6 acceptance "VAD on preprocessed signal" regression test** — Task 8 `vad decides on the preprocessed frame, not the raw frame` (pure) and Task 11 `voice activation uses the preprocessed frame` (handler level).
- **§2 no javacpp/ndk-build left** — F6's responsibility; this plan deletes the two remaining javacpp-based encoder wrappers (Task 11).
- Type consistency checked: `CapturePreprocessor.process(ShortArray): Float?`, `IInputMode.shouldTransmit(ShortArray, Int, Float?)`, `VoiceActivityDetector.isVoice(ShortArray, Int, Float?)`, `CapturePipeline.process(ShortArray, Int): CaptureFrame`, `PcmCaptureSource.read(ShortArray, Int): Int`, `PcmPlaybackSink.write(ShortArray, Int): Int`, `FarEndSink.analyzeReverseStream(ShortArray)`, `EncoderFactory.create(HumlaUDPMessageType, Int, Int): IEncoder?` are used with these exact shapes in every task.
